# `export X='...'` the quotes are removed by the shell, and quotes left in a .env file are removed here too.
POSTGRES_DATABASE=orion_dev_metadata
POSTGRES_HOSTNAME=NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=change-me
POSTGRES_PORT=1524

#   production  -> /app/backend/cert/prod.pem

# POSTGRES_CERT_DIR=/some/dir          (directory holding dev.pem / uat.pem / prod.pem)
# POSTGRES_CA_FILE=/some/dir/ca.pem    (one specific CA file; wins over the above)
# POSTGRES_SSL=false                   (TLS is ON by default; only turn it off for a local test database)
# POSTGRES_ENABLED=false               (do not write metrics at all, e.g. when running without a database)

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 53088e95d2b7bcd48d4abbec8ec7f8a0377f5b7ee3b7f65ee77866e945f5585f README.md
# Workspace Mgr

Middleware between the **Orion** dashboard and **Red Hat OpenShift Dev Spaces**. On demand of a user it
creates, lists, starts, stops and deletes DevWorkspaces, creates the user's project (namespace) when it does not
exist, manages per-user Git credentials, and records every action to Postgres for the Dev Spaces dashboard.

Node 20+, TypeScript (strict, ESM), Express 4, `@kubernetes/client-node`, `pg`, `zod`.
Project rules: [docs/PROJECT_RULES.md](docs/PROJECT_RULES.md) · Checklist: [docs/CHECKLIST.md](docs/CHECKLIST.md)

## Quick start

```bash
npm install
cp .env.example .env        # edit; SECRET_KEY, ADMIN_*, DEVREG_URL are required
npm run dev                 # tsx watch (loads env from your shell; use `node --env-file=.env` if you prefer)
npm run build && npm start  # production
```

| Script                                                | What it does                               |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| `npm run typecheck` / `lint` / `format`               | tsc, eslint (0 warnings allowed), prettier |
| `npm test`                                            | unit + integration + e2e                   |
| `npm run test:unit` / `test:integration` / `test:e2e` | one layer                                  |
| `npm run test:coverage`                               | all layers with a coverage gate            |
| `npm run bundle` / `bundle:all` / `restore -- <file>` | see "Bundle and restore"                   |

## Postgres: the existing database

Metrics go into the **existing** Orion database. workspace-mgr creates nothing (no tables, schemas or migrations);
it only inserts rows into the table named by `POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME`.

Same variables as the original service:

```bash
export POSTGRES_DATABASE=orion_dev_metadata
export POSTGRES_HOSTNAME=NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net
export POSTGRES_PASSWORD=...
export POSTGRES_PORT='1524'
export POSTGRES_USER=app_orion_pgsql_dev
export POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME='metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"'
export NODE_ENV=development
```

- **TLS** is always on and the server certificate is verified, as before. `NODE_ENV` picks the CA file:
  `development` → `./certs/dev.pem` (hostname check relaxed), `uat` → `/app/backend/cert/uat.pem`,
  `production` → `/app/backend/cert/prod.pem`. Override with `POSTGRES_CERT_DIR` or `POSTGRES_CA_FILE`.
  The app refuses to start, naming the missing file, if the CA cannot be read.
- **Columns:** the service reads the table's column list once and writes only the columns that exist (it knows
  `timestamp, csiid, soeid, eventtype, resourcename, resourcetype, status, limitcpu, limitmemory, requestcpu,
requestmemory, duration, storage, useraction, errordetail, workspaceversion, editorname`). A table without
  `workspaceversion` / `editorname` still works; a warning names what is not stored.
- **Timestamps** are written as UTC wall-clock text, as the original did.
- A database problem never blocks a workspace action: it is logged and the action continues.

## API (version 1)

All routes live under **`/api/v1`**. Send `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>` except for `login`.
Errors are always `{ "error": string, "message": string, "code": string }`.

| Method | Path                                    | Purpose                                                        |
| ------ | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| POST   | `/api/v1/login`                         | `{username,password}` → `{token}`                              |
| GET    | `/api/v1/catalog`                       | Stacks from the devfile registry                               |
| GET    | `/api/v1/:soeid/projects`               | The user's projects (namespaces)                               |
| GET    | `/api/v1/:soeid/workspaces`             | List workspaces                                                |
| POST   | `/api/v1/:soeid/workspaces`             | Create a workspace (creates the project if needed)             |
| GET    | `/api/v1/:soeid/workspaces/:id`         | Workspace details                                              |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/:soeid/workspaces/:id`         | Delete                                                         |
| POST   | `/api/v1/:soeid/workspaces/:id/start`   | Start                                                          |
| POST   | `/api/v1/:soeid/workspaces/:id/stop`    | Stop                                                           |
| GET    | `/api/v1/:soeid/pats`                   | List GitHub PAT secrets                                        |
| GET    | `/api/v1/:soeid/pats/:tokenName?csiid=` | View (token is masked)                                         |
| POST   | `/api/v1/:soeid/pats/:tokenName`        | `{csiid,token}` create (409 if it exists)                      |
| PUT    | `/api/v1/:soeid/pats/:tokenName`        | `{csiid,token}` replace                                        |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/:soeid/pats/:tokenName?csiid=` | Delete (idempotent)                                            |
| GET    | `/api/v1/:soeid/gitconfig?csiid=`       | View `~/.gitconfig`                                            |
| PUT    | `/api/v1/:soeid/gitconfig`              | `{csiid,content}` create or replace                            |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/:soeid/gitconfig?csiid=`       | Delete (idempotent)                                            |
| GET    | `/api/v1/:soeid/deployment-projects`    | List build/deploy projects                                     |
| POST   | `/api/v1/:soeid/deployment-projects`    | `{csiid}` → 201 created, or 200 if it already exists           |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/:soeid/deployment-projects`    | Delete all of the user's deployment projects                   |
| GET    | `/healthz`, `/readyz`                   | Liveness (no dependencies) / readiness (Kubernetes + Postgres) |

Create-workspace body:

```json
{
  "devworkspaceValue": "java-maven",
  "csiid": "177398",
  "editor": "vscode",
  "stackVersion": "2.0.0",
  "repositoryUrl": "https://github.com/acme/app.git",
  "repositoryBranch": "main"
}
```

`editor` is `vscode` (default), `intellij` or `goland`; the last three fields are optional.

**Versioning.** `/api/v1` is the contract. A breaking change will ship as `/api/v2` next to it. The old unversioned
paths (`/login`, `/:soeid/...`) still work as **deprecated aliases** (`Deprecation: true`,
`Link: </api/v1/...>; rel="successor-version"`) so Orion keeps working while it migrates. Turn them off with
`LEGACY_UNVERSIONED_ROUTES=false`.

## Architecture

```
http (routes, auth, validation)  →  services (rules)  →  ports ← infra (Kubernetes, Postgres, registry, email)
                                         ↓
                                   factories (pure) , utils , constants
```

`src/container.ts` is the only place adapters are wired to services; tests wire in fakes
(`tests/helpers/InMemoryKube.ts` behaves like a cluster, `pg-mem` like Postgres).

## Tests

| Layer       | Where               | What is real                                                                              |
| ----------- | ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Unit        | `tests/unit`        | One module; everything else faked                                                         |
| Integration | `tests/integration` | Express routes + services + SQL (`pg-mem`, the shipped migration) + HTTP devfile registry |
| End to end  | `tests/e2e`         | A listening server driven only over HTTP through whole user journeys                      |

The cluster is always in memory: nothing here talks to a real OpenShift. Run a smoke test against your dev
cluster before first release (see "Not verified").

## One text file for the whole app (`pack.sh` / `unpack.sh`)

To move the app anywhere with just **two files** (`all-code-file.txt` and `unpack.sh`), no git or zip needed:

```bash
bash pack.sh                                   # everything needed to run, build and test → all-code-file.txt
bash unpack.sh all-code-file.txt -o /c/my/app  # rebuild the whole app there, files exactly as they are
cd /c/my/app && npm ci && npm test && npm run build && npm start
```

On Windows use Git Bash (paths like `/c/my/app`). Needs only bash, awk and coreutils.

- **Included:** `src`, `tests`, `scripts`, `docs`, `helm`, kustomize folders, `certs/` (if present), `package.json`,
  `package-lock.json`, all config files, `Dockerfile`, `.env.example`, `README.md`, `pack.sh`, `unpack.sh`.
- **Never included:** `node_modules`, `dist`, `coverage`, `.git`, `legacy`, `.env*` (secrets). `pack.sh` also refuses
  to pack any file that contains a private key.
- **Safe to run:** every file is verified against its SHA-256 before anything is written; existing files that differ
  are kept unless you pass `--force`; `--dry-run` previews; `--list` lists. Paths that could escape the target folder
  are rejected. If an editor or git converted the text file to CRLF line endings, `unpack.sh` converts it back, but only
  when every checksum then matches. `.gitattributes` keeps the `.sh` files LF so bash works on Windows checkouts.
- Verified by rebuilding the app from the text file in an empty folder and running `npm ci`, typecheck, lint, all
  tests and the build there.

## Bundle and restore (changed files only, Node)

Pack changed files into one text file, and rebuild them from it elsewhere:

```bash
node scripts/bundle.mjs                 # files added/modified since the last commit → workspace-mgr.bundle.txt
node scripts/bundle.mjs --all           # every project file
node scripts/restore.mjs workspace-mgr.bundle.txt --out ../copy            # recreate
node scripts/restore.mjs workspace-mgr.bundle.txt --out ../copy --dry-run  # preview
node scripts/restore.mjs workspace-mgr.bundle.txt --list                   # inspect
```

Every file is verified (size + SHA-256) before anything is written; existing files that differ are only
overwritten with `--force`; `node_modules`, `dist`, `.env*` (except `.env.example`) are never bundled;
a bundle whose line endings were converted to CRLF is repaired only when the checksums prove it exact.

## Assumptions (my defaults; please confirm)

You did not show the create-workspace payload, `index.ts`, `updateDevfile.ts` or the first three screenshots, so
these were decided without them:

- **Payload** is the shape above. `devworkspaceValue`+`csiid` come from the legacy code; the rest are additions.
- **Editor profiles:** JetBrains server ports `2222` (IntelliJ) and `3400` (GoLand), home volume `10Gi`. Read from
  a partly visible screenshot; verify in `src/factories/devfile-updater.ts`.
- **Workspaces are inlined** in the DevWorkspace (`spec.template` + `contributions`) rather than creating a separate
  DevWorkspaceTemplate as the oldest code did. Delete still cleans up legacy templates.
- **Limits:** 3 running / 5 total workspaces per user (`MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES`, `MAX_TOTAL_WORKSPACES`).
- **Missing config files** (`user-namespace.yaml`, `user-role-default.yaml`) are rebuilt in code
  (`src/constants/kubernetes-configs.ts`, `NamespaceService`). The user role there is my guess.
- **Auth:** only the admin login exists, as before; its token may act for any `:soeid` (what Orion needs today).
  Tokens with `role: user` are limited to their own `:soeid`.
- **Status codes** follow REST: login failure is `401` (was `400`), PAT create is `201`.
- **Git username** is `<soeid><GITHUB_USER_SUFFIX>` (legacy used `<soeid>_citi`).

## Intentionally not ported

`legacy/` keeps the original files. Not carried over because they were dead, broken or out of scope:
`Mix-Code.ts` (PVC wait experiment), `find-resources.ts` (index-based resource sum, replaced by
`summarizeComponents`), `remove-workspace-code.ts` (stand-alone script), `t.ts` (empty),
`devfileConverter.ts` (not used once workspaces are inlined). Out of scope for now: the 15-day idle cleanup cron,
OpenShift cluster metrics, the dashboard aggregation tables/queries (`prompt/*.md`).

## Not verified

- No run against a real OpenShift cluster, real Postgres, or the real devfile registry/mail/GraphQL services.
- `docker build` was not run. The Helm chart (`helm/`) has no Deployment template and its folder is named
  `template`, not `templates`; it was left unchanged.

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 368130c7b1df931def4df1ca3d4006fc6606afc9ba49d747a1aa607fe4495fc8 docs/PROJECT_RULES.md
# Workspace Mgr — Project Rules

Workspace Mgr is the backend/middleware between the Orion dashboard and OpenShift
Dev Spaces. It creates, lists, starts, stops and deletes DevWorkspaces for a user,
creates the user's project (namespace) when it does not exist, and records every
action to Postgres for the Dev Spaces dashboard.

These rules are binding for every change. If a rule must be broken, say why in the PR.

## 1. Architecture

1. **Layers, strictly top-down:** `http` → `services` → `infra`/`factories` → `domain`/`utils`.
   A lower layer never imports from a higher one.
2. **`http`** only parses/validates input, calls one service method, and maps the result or
   error to a response. No Kubernetes, SQL or business rules in routes.
3. **`services`** own business rules (limits, ordering of steps, naming, metrics events).
   They depend on **ports** (interfaces), never on concrete clients.
4. **`infra`** holds the concrete adapters (Kubernetes, Postgres, devfile registry, email).
   Every adapter implements a port declared in `src/infra/ports.ts`.
5. **`factories`** are pure functions (devfile → DevWorkspace, log records). No I/O.
6. **One composition root** (`src/container.ts`) wires adapters into services. Nothing else
   calls `new` on an adapter. Tests build the container with fakes.
7. No module-level side effects (no connecting to a DB, reading files, or reading `process.env`
   at import time). Config is read once in `src/config/env.ts` and passed in.

## 2. Language and style

1. TypeScript `strict: true`, ESM (`"module": "NodeNext"`). Local imports end in `.js`.
2. No `any` except at a third-party boundary, and then it is narrowed immediately.
   **No `// @ts-ignore`.** Use `// @ts-expect-error` with a reason if truly unavoidable.
3. Prefer `async/await`. No `.then().catch()` chains.
4. Functions are small and single-purpose. A service method that needs a comment block to
   explain its steps should be split.
5. Names: `camelCase` values, `PascalCase` types/classes, `kebab-case.ts` filenames for modules
   that export functions, `PascalCase.ts` for files whose main export is a class.
6. Comments explain _why_, not _what_. No commented-out code, no TODO without an issue id.
7. `eslint` and `prettier` must pass with zero warnings.

## 3. Errors

1. Throw typed errors from `src/utils/errors.ts` (`AppError` subclasses). Each carries an HTTP
   status and a stable `code`.
2. Never swallow an error silently. Either handle it, record it, or rethrow it.
   The only intentional swallow is the metrics recorder (rule 6.2), and it logs.
3. Kubernetes/axios errors are translated to `AppError` at the adapter boundary
   (`src/utils/k8s-errors.ts`). Callers never inspect `err.body.code`.
4. Error responses always have the shape `{ "error": string, "message": string, "code": string }`.
   Never return a stack trace or upstream response body to the client.

## 4. Security

1. `SECRET_KEY` is **required**; the process refuses to start without it. No fallback secret.
2. Tokens: `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>` only. Verify with the explicit algorithm `HS256`.
3. Admin credential comparison is constant-time (`crypto.timingSafeEqual`).
4. Every `:soeid` and `:id` path parameter is validated against an allow-list pattern before it
   is used in a namespace name, label selector or Kubernetes path.
5. A caller may only act on their own `:soeid` unless the token has the `admin` role.
6. Never log tokens, passwords, PAT values, or full request bodies. PAT values are masked
   (`ghp_ab…xyz`) everywhere except the Secret itself.
7. Secrets for Kubernetes and Postgres come from the environment / mounted files, never from
   source or from git.
8. All SQL is parameterised. No string-built queries.
9. Kubernetes access uses the pod's service account with the minimum RBAC in
   `kustomize.server/base/sa.yaml`; do not widen it without review.

## 5. Kubernetes behaviour

1. Operations are idempotent: "create or replace" for quotas, roles, bindings, ConfigMaps.
2. Workspace namespaces are found by **label + annotation**, not by listing the whole cluster.
   Deployment namespaces are created through OpenShift's `ProjectRequest`, which may not apply our labels, so
   they are found by their deterministic name (`<soeid>-devspaces-deploy-<csiid>`).
3. A workspace id is `metadata.name`; it is unique inside a user's namespaces.
4. Poll loops have a hard timeout and an interval, are injectable (clock/sleep) for tests, and
   fail with a typed error. They never silently "fix" state by restarting workspaces.
5. Deleting something that is already gone is a success.

## 6. Metrics (Postgres)

1. Every user-visible action (create/delete/start/stop of workspace; create/delete of project;
   PAT/gitconfig changes) records one row to `metrics."devspaces-metrics"`, success **or** failure.
2. Recording is best-effort: a database failure is logged and must never fail or delay the
   user's action beyond a short timeout.
3. The row shape is defined once (`MetricsRecord`). The metrics table **already exists** and is owned by the
   database team: the service never issues DDL (no `CREATE`, `ALTER`, `DROP`) and ships no migrations. It only
   `INSERT`s, plus read-only `SELECT`s of the table's column list so it can write into an older table that lacks
   some of our columns. A test asserts this. The table SQL under `tests/helpers/` is a test fixture only.
4. Postgres is configured only through the existing variables (`POSTGRES_DATABASE`, `_HOSTNAME`, `_PASSWORD`,
   `_PORT`, `_USER`, `_DATABASE_TABLENAME`) and `NODE_ENV`, which picks the CA file (dev/uat/prod) as before.
5. Timestamps are written as UTC wall-clock text (`2026-01-02 03:04:05.678`), as the original service did. The
   CST format is for logs and emails only.

## 7. Testing

1. Three layers, all required for a feature:
   - **Unit** (`tests/unit`): one module, all collaborators faked, no network, no timers
     (use fake clock). Fast (< 5 s total).
   - **Integration** (`tests/integration`): real HTTP routes + real services + real SQL
     (`pg-mem`) + in-memory Kubernetes + a local fake devfile registry. Verifies wiring.
   - **End to end** (`tests/e2e`): a real listening server, driven only over HTTP
     (`fetch`) through whole user journeys (login → create → start → stop → delete), then
     asserting the Kubernetes state and metrics rows.
2. A bug fix starts with a failing test.
3. Tests do not depend on order, wall-clock time, or real cluster/DB access.
4. Coverage gate: ≥ 85 % lines and ≥ 80 % branches on `src/` (excluding `src/index.ts`).
5. No `.only`, no `.skip` committed.

## 8. API contract

1. **URI versioning, industry standard:** every API route is served under `/api/v1`
   (`/api/v1/login`, `/api/v1/catalog`, `/api/v1/:soeid/workspaces[/:id[/start|/stop]]`, ...).
   Path structure after the prefix is identical to the pre-versioning layout.
   Operational probes `/healthz` and `/readyz` are unversioned.
2. Within a major version changes are additive only (new routes, new optional fields).
   A breaking change ships as `/api/v2` alongside `v1`; `v1` is then deprecated, never edited in place.
3. The pre-versioning paths (`/login`, `/:soeid/...`) exist only as deprecated aliases of `v1`
   (`Deprecation: true` and a `Link: rel="successor-version"` header) controlled by
   `LEGACY_UNVERSIONED_ROUTES`. Remove them once Orion has migrated.
4. Request bodies are validated with `zod`; unknown properties are rejected.
5. `409` for limit/conflict, `404` unknown resource, `400` invalid input, `401/403` auth,
   `502` upstream (cluster/registry) failure, `500` everything else.

## 9. Operations

1. `/healthz` (liveness) never touches dependencies. `/readyz` checks Kubernetes API and
   Postgres reachability.
2. Structured JSON logs, one line per event, with `requestId`, `soeid`, `event`.
3. Graceful shutdown on `SIGTERM`: stop accepting, drain, close the DB pool.
4. Config via environment variables only; every variable is documented in `.env.example`.
5. Container runs as non-root, read-only root filesystem compatible.

## 10. Git and review

1. Small, focused commits; imperative subject line.
2. CI order: `typecheck` → `lint` → `test:unit` → `test:integration` → `test:e2e` → `build`.
3. A PR is mergeable only when the [CHECKLIST](./CHECKLIST.md) items for its scope are ticked.

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 090c1f2f6269b5e30e9753cfbdbee6d9b8a5bd616200204047e891704c446415 pack.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Pack every file needed to run, build and test workspace-mgr into ONE text file.
# Rebuild the app from that file with:  bash unpack.sh all-code-file.txt
#
#   bash pack.sh                   -> all-code-file.txt
#   bash pack.sh -o other.txt      choose the output file
#   bash pack.sh --list            only list the files that would be packed
#
# Included : src, tests, scripts, docs, helm, kustomize.server, base, overlays, certs (if present),
#            README.md, package.json, package-lock.json, tsconfig*.json, vitest/eslint/prettier configs,
#            Dockerfile, .dockerignore, .gitignore, .env.example, pack.sh, unpack.sh
#            (no migrations: the Postgres table already exists and is never created by the app)
# Refused  : any file containing a private key (certs/ may hold public CA bundles only)
# Excluded : node_modules, dist, coverage, .git, legacy, prompt, .env (secrets), *.bundle.txt, the output file

set -euo pipefail
export LC_ALL=C

cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")"
OUT="all-code-file.txt"
LIST_ONLY=0

while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    -o) OUT="${2:?-o needs a file name}"; shift 2 ;;
    --list) LIST_ONLY=1; shift ;;
    -h|--help) sed -n '2,15p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; exit 0 ;;
    *) echo "pack: unknown argument: $1" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done

DIRS=(src tests scripts docs helm kustomize.server base overlays certs)
ROOT_FILES=(README.md package.json package-lock.json tsconfig.json tsconfig.build.json vitest.config.ts
  eslint.config.js .prettierrc.json .prettierignore .gitignore .gitattributes .dockerignore .env.example Dockerfile
  pack.sh unpack.sh)

collect() {
  for f in "${ROOT_FILES[@]}"; do [ -f "$f" ] && printf '%s\n' "$f"; done
  for d in "${DIRS[@]}"; do [ -d "$d" ] && find "$d" -type f; done
}

is_excluded() {
  case "/$1/" in
    */node_modules/*|*/dist/*|*/coverage/*|*/.git/*|*/legacy/*) return 0 ;;
  esac
  case "$1" in
    .env|.env.*|*/.env|*/.env.*) [ "$(basename "$1")" = ".env.example" ] || return 0 ;;
    *.bundle.txt|*.log|"$OUT") return 0 ;;
  esac
  return 1
}

FILES=()
while IFS= read -r f; do
  f="${f#./}"
  is_excluded "$f" && continue
  case "$f" in *[\'\"\\:]*|*$'\n'*) echo "pack: refusing unsafe file name: $f" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
  FILES+=("$f")
done < <(collect | sort -u)

if [ "$LIST_ONLY" = 1 ]; then
  printf '%s\n' "${FILES[@]}"
  echo "${#FILES[@]} file(s)" >&2
  exit 0
fi

for f in "${FILES[@]}"; do
  if grep -qE -e '-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----' "$f" 2>/dev/null; then
    echo "pack: refusing to pack $f: it contains a private key" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
done

hash_of() {
  if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then sha256sum "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1
  else shasum -a 256 "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1; fi
}

# Random marker token; if any file happened to contain it we would simply pick another.
TOKEN=""
while :; do
  TOKEN="$(head -c 12 /dev/urandom | od -An -tx1 | tr -d ' \n')"
  clash=0
  for f in "${FILES[@]}"; do
    if grep -qF "@@@FILE@$TOKEN" "$f" 2>/dev/null || grep -qF "@@@END@$TOKEN" "$f" 2>/dev/null; then clash=1; break; fi
  done
  [ "$clash" = 0 ] && break
done

TMP="$(mktemp)"
trap 'rm -f "$TMP"' EXIT

{
  echo "#### WORKSPACE-MGR ALL-CODE-FILE v1"
  echo "#### generated: $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
  echo "#### token: $TOKEN"
  echo "#### files: ${#FILES[@]}"
  echo "#### rebuild the app with: bash unpack.sh $(basename "$OUT") [-o DIR] [--force]"
  echo
  for f in "${FILES[@]}"; do
    sum="$(hash_of "$f")"
    if [ ! -s "$f" ]; then
      printf '@@@FILE@%s text empty %s %s\n' "$TOKEN" "$sum" "$f"
    elif [ "$(tr -d '\000' < "$f" | cmp -s - "$f" && echo text || echo binary)" = binary ]; then
      printf '@@@FILE@%s b64 nl %s %s\n' "$TOKEN" "$sum" "$f"
      base64 < "$f"
    else
      if [ -n "$(tail -c1 "$f")" ]; then nl=nonl; else nl=nl; fi
      printf '@@@FILE@%s text %s %s %s\n' "$TOKEN" "$nl" "$sum" "$f"
      cat "$f"
      [ "$nl" = nonl ] && printf '\n'
    fi
    printf '@@@END@%s\n' "$TOKEN"
  done
} > "$TMP"

mv "$TMP" "$OUT"
trap - EXIT
bytes="$(wc -c < "$OUT" | tr -d ' ')"
echo "Packed ${#FILES[@]} file(s), $((bytes / 1024)) KiB -> $OUT"
@@@END@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 3e8b33a1cbb8ca1170086af7125ae0cfa50da440469799c3c5a111ca8a5d4105 scripts/bundle.mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Collect the project's files into ONE text file that scripts/restore.mjs can turn back into the same files.
//
//   node scripts/bundle.mjs                   files added or modified since the last git commit (default)
//   node scripts/bundle.mjs --all             every project file (still skips node_modules, dist, .env, ...)
//   node scripts/bundle.mjs --out my.txt      choose the output file (default: workspace-mgr.bundle.txt)
//   node scripts/bundle.mjs --root <dir>      bundle another directory (default: the project root)
//   node scripts/bundle.mjs --note "text"     free-text note stored in the bundle header
//   node scripts/bundle.mjs --list            print what would be bundled and exit

import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import process from 'node:process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { createBundle, isExcluded } from './lib/bundle-format.mjs';

const MAX_FILE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const args = { all: false, list: false, root: undefined, out: undefined, note: undefined };
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const a = argv[i];
    if (a === '--all') args.all = true;
    else if (a === '--changed') args.all = false;
    else if (a === '--list') args.list = true;
    else if (a === '--root') args.root = argv[++i];
    else if (a === '--out') args.out = argv[++i];
    else if (a === '--note') args.note = argv[++i];
    else if (a === '-h' || a === '--help') args.help = true;
    else throw new Error(`Unknown argument: ${a}`);
  }
  return args;
}

const toPosix = (p) => p.split(path.sep).join('/');

function walk(root, dir = root, found = []) {
  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    const rel = toPosix(path.relative(root, full));
    if (isExcluded(rel)) continue;
    if (entry.isDirectory()) walk(root, full, found);
    else if (entry.isFile()) found.push(rel);
  }
  return found;
}

/** Files git sees as added, modified, renamed or untracked (not deleted, not ignored). */
function changedFiles(root) {
  // `--relative` / `ls-files` give paths relative to `root` even when the git repository starts above it.
  const git = (args) =>
    execFileSync('git', args, { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8', stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'ignore'] })
      .split('\0')
      .filter(Boolean);
  try {
    git(['rev-parse', '--is-inside-work-tree']);
  } catch {
    return undefined; // not a git repository (or git missing)
  }

  let tracked;
  try {
    // staged + unstaged changes against the last commit, deletions excluded
    tracked = git(['diff', '--name-only', '--relative', '--diff-filter=d', '-z', 'HEAD']);
  } catch {
    tracked = git(['ls-files', '--cached', '-z']); // repository without a first commit yet
  }
  const untracked = git(['ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard', '-z']);
  return [...tracked, ...untracked];
}

function main() {
  const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  if (args.help) {
    process.stdout.write(
      readFileSync(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url), 'utf8')
        .split('\n')
        .slice(1, 11)
        .map((l) => l.replace(/^\/\/ ?/, ''))
        .join('\n') + '\n',
    );
    return;
  }

  const root = path.resolve(args.root ?? path.join(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..'));
  const out = path.resolve(args.out ?? path.join(root, 'workspace-mgr.bundle.txt'));

  let mode = args.all ? 'all' : 'changed';
  let candidates;
  if (mode === 'changed') {
    candidates = changedFiles(root);
    if (candidates === undefined) {
      process.stderr.write('Not a git repository: falling back to --all\n');
      mode = 'all';
    }
  }
  if (mode === 'all') candidates = walk(root);

  const files = [];
  for (const rel of [...new Set(candidates)].sort()) {
    if (isExcluded(rel)) continue;
    const full = path.join(root, rel);
    if (!existsSync(full) || !statSync(full).isFile()) continue;
    if (statSync(full).size > MAX_FILE_BYTES) {
      process.stderr.write(`Skipping ${rel}: larger than ${MAX_FILE_BYTES / 1024 / 1024} MB\n`);
      continue;
    }
    // Never bundle the output file into itself
    if (path.resolve(full) === out) continue;
    files.push({ path: rel, content: readFileSync(full) });
  }

  if (args.list) {
    for (const f of files) process.stdout.write(`${f.path}\n`);
    process.stdout.write(`\n${files.length} file(s), mode: ${mode}\n`);
    return;
  }
  if (files.length === 0) {
    process.stderr.write('Nothing to bundle (no changed files). Use --all to bundle everything.\n');
    process.exitCode = 1;
    return;
  }

  const note = args.note ?? `mode=${mode}`;
  writeFileSync(out, createBundle(files, { note }), 'utf8');
  const bytes = files.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.content.length, 0);
  process.stdout.write(
    `Bundled ${files.length} file(s) (${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KiB, mode: ${mode})\n→ ${out}\n`,
  );
}

try {
  main();
} catch (error) {
  process.stderr.write(`bundle: ${error.message}\n`);
  process.exitCode = 1;
}

export const BUNDLE_MAGIC: string;
export function sha256(buffer: Buffer): string;
export function isTextBuffer(buffer: Buffer): boolean;
export function isSafeRelativePath(p: unknown): boolean;
export function isExcluded(relPath: string): boolean;
export function createBundle(
  files: { path: string; content: Buffer }[],
  options?: { generatedAt?: Date; note?: string },
): string;
export function parseBundle(text: string): {
  meta: { generated: string; files: number; note?: string; lineEndingsRepaired?: boolean };
  files: { path: string; enc: string; content: Buffer; sha256: string }[];
};

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 959329f0b11e8e6352bb66d361d07d5895517d02c1bf498c65515f1b57892a88 scripts/lib/bundle-format.mjs
// Shared by scripts/bundle.mjs and scripts/restore.mjs.
//
// A bundle is ONE text file holding many files, designed to survive copy/paste, email and chat tools:
//
//   WORKSPACE-MGR-BUNDLE v1
//   generated: 2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z
//   boundary: 3f9c...            <- random per bundle, so file contents can never collide with markers
//   files: 2
//   (blank line)
//   @@@ FILE <boundary> {"path":"src/a.ts","enc":"utf8","bytes":12,"sha256":"..."} @@@
//   ...file content exactly as it is...
//   @@@ END <boundary> @@@
//
// Text files are stored verbatim (CRLF, BOM and a missing final newline are all preserved).
// Binary files are stored as base64. Every file carries its byte length and SHA-256, verified on restore.

import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import path from 'node:path';

export const BUNDLE_MAGIC = 'WORKSPACE-MGR-BUNDLE v1';

const EXCLUDED_DIRS = new Set(['node_modules', 'dist', 'coverage', '.git', '.idea', '.vscode']);
const EXCLUDED_FILES = new Set(['.DS_Store', 'Thumbs.db']);

export const sha256 = (buffer) => createHash('sha256').update(buffer).digest('hex');

/** True when the bytes are valid UTF-8 without NUL bytes (so they can be stored verbatim as text). */
export function isTextBuffer(buffer) {
  if (buffer.includes(0)) return false;
  try {
    new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true, ignoreBOM: true }).decode(buffer);
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** Relative, forward-slash, no `..`, no drive letters: a path that cannot escape the target directory. */
export function isSafeRelativePath(p) {
  if (typeof p !== 'string' || p.length === 0 || p.includes('\0')) return false;
  if (p.startsWith('/') || p.startsWith('\\') || /^[A-Za-z]:/.test(p) || p.includes('\\')) return false;
  const normalized = path.posix.normalize(p);
  return normalized === p && !normalized.split('/').includes('..') && normalized !== '.';
}

/** Files that must never be bundled: dependencies, build output, VCS data, secrets, previous bundles. */
export function isExcluded(relPath) {
  const parts = relPath.split('/');
  if (parts.some((part) => EXCLUDED_DIRS.has(part))) return true;
  const name = parts[parts.length - 1] ?? '';
  if (EXCLUDED_FILES.has(name)) return true;
  if (name === '.env' || (name.startsWith('.env.') && name !== '.env.example')) return true; // secrets
  if (name.endsWith('.bundle.txt') || name.endsWith('.log')) return true;
  return false;
}

/**
 * @param {{ path: string, content: Buffer }[]} files
 * @param {{ generatedAt?: Date, note?: string }} [options]
 */
export function createBundle(files, options = {}) {
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const file of files) {
    if (!isSafeRelativePath(file.path)) throw new Error(`Refusing to bundle unsafe path: ${file.path}`);
    if (seen.has(file.path)) throw new Error(`Duplicate path in bundle: ${file.path}`);
    seen.add(file.path);
  }

  const encoded = files.map((file) => {
    const text = isTextBuffer(file.content);
    return {
      ...file,
      enc: text ? 'utf8' : 'base64',
      body: text ? file.content.toString('utf8') : wrap64(file.content),
    };
  });

  let boundary;
  do {
    boundary = randomBytes(12).toString('hex');
  } while (encoded.some((f) => f.body.includes(boundary)));

  const lines = [
    BUNDLE_MAGIC,
    `generated: ${(options.generatedAt ?? new Date()).toISOString()}`,
    `boundary: ${boundary}`,
    `files: ${files.length}`,
  ];
  if (options.note) lines.push(`note: ${options.note.replace(/[\r\n]+/g, ' ')}`);
  let out = `${lines.join('\n')}\n\n`;

  for (const f of encoded) {
    const meta = JSON.stringify({
      path: f.path,
      enc: f.enc,
      bytes: f.content.length,
      sha256: sha256(f.content),
    });
    out += `@@@ FILE ${boundary} ${meta} @@@\n${f.body}\n@@@ END ${boundary} @@@\n`;
  }
  return out;
}

const wrap64 = (buffer) => buffer.toString('base64').replace(/(.{76})/g, '$1\n');

/**
 * @param {string} text
 * @returns {{ meta: { generated: string, files: number, note?: string, lineEndingsRepaired?: boolean }, files: { path: string, enc: string, content: Buffer, sha256: string }[] }}
 */
export function parseBundle(text) {
  try {
    return parseStrict(text);
  } catch (error) {
    // An editor, e-mail client or `git autocrlf` may have turned every LF into CRLF. Undo that, but only
    // accept the result if every file still matches its recorded size and SHA-256; otherwise report the
    // original problem, never a silently altered file.
    if (text.includes('\r\n')) {
      try {
        const repaired = parseStrict(text.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n'));
        repaired.meta.lineEndingsRepaired = true;
        return repaired;
      } catch (repairError) {
        throw new Error(
          `${error.message}. The line endings look converted to CRLF, and converting them back did not ` +
            `restore every file exactly (${repairError.message}). Re-copy the bundle without line-ending conversion.`,
          { cause: repairError },
        );
      }
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

function parseStrict(text) {
  if (!text.startsWith(`${BUNDLE_MAGIC}\n`) && !text.startsWith(`${BUNDLE_MAGIC}\r\n`)) {
    throw new Error(`Not a workspace-mgr bundle (first line must be "${BUNDLE_MAGIC}")`);
  }
  const header = text.slice(0, text.search(/\r?\n\r?\n/) + 1 || undefined);
  const field = (name) => new RegExp(`^${name}: (.*?)\\r?$`, 'm').exec(header)?.[1];
  const boundary = field('boundary');
  const declared = Number(field('files'));
  if (!boundary || !/^[0-9a-f]{16,}$/.test(boundary)) throw new Error('Bundle header has no valid boundary');
  if (!Number.isInteger(declared) || declared < 0) throw new Error('Bundle header has no valid file count');

  const startMarker = `@@@ FILE ${boundary} `;
  const endMarker = `\n@@@ END ${boundary} @@@`;
  const files = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  let cursor = header.length;

  for (;;) {
    const start = text.indexOf(startMarker, cursor);
    if (start === -1) break;
    const headerEnd = text.indexOf(' @@@\n', start);
    if (headerEnd === -1) throw new Error(`Malformed file header at offset ${start}`);

    let meta;
    try {
      meta = JSON.parse(text.slice(start + startMarker.length, headerEnd));
    } catch {
      throw new Error(`Unreadable file header at offset ${start}`);
    }
    const bodyStart = headerEnd + ' @@@\n'.length;
    const bodyEnd = text.indexOf(endMarker, bodyStart - 1);
    if (bodyEnd === -1 || bodyEnd < bodyStart - 1) throw new Error(`Missing end marker for ${meta.path}`);

    if (!isSafeRelativePath(meta.path))
      throw new Error(`Unsafe path in bundle: ${JSON.stringify(meta.path)}`);
    if (seen.has(meta.path)) throw new Error(`Duplicate path in bundle: ${meta.path}`);
    seen.add(meta.path);

    const body = text.slice(bodyStart, Math.max(bodyStart, bodyEnd));
    let content;
    if (meta.enc === 'utf8') content = Buffer.from(body, 'utf8');
    else if (meta.enc === 'base64') content = Buffer.from(body.replace(/\s+/g, ''), 'base64');
    else throw new Error(`Unknown encoding "${meta.enc}" for ${meta.path}`);

    if (content.length !== meta.bytes) {
      throw new Error(
        `Size mismatch for ${meta.path}: expected ${meta.bytes} bytes, found ${content.length}`,
      );
    }
    if (sha256(content) !== meta.sha256) throw new Error(`Checksum mismatch for ${meta.path}`);

    files.push({ path: meta.path, enc: meta.enc, content, sha256: meta.sha256 });
    cursor = bodyEnd + endMarker.length;
  }

  if (files.length !== declared) {
    throw new Error(`Bundle declares ${declared} files but contains ${files.length} (truncated or edited?)`);
  }
  return { meta: { generated: field('generated') ?? '', files: declared, note: field('note') }, files };
}
@@@END@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e
@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 33572740d68ea3489966b5d63a7d2f2d3ea478da956bfe3cba0ec77eeec55743 scripts/restore.mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Recreate the files stored in a bundle produced by scripts/bundle.mjs.
//
//   node scripts/restore.mjs workspace-mgr.bundle.txt             restore into the current directory
//   node scripts/restore.mjs bundle.txt --out C:\some\folder      restore into another directory
//   node scripts/restore.mjs bundle.txt --dry-run                 show what would happen, write nothing
//   node scripts/restore.mjs bundle.txt --force                   overwrite existing files that differ
//   node scripts/restore.mjs bundle.txt --list                    list the files in the bundle
//
// Safety: every file is checked (size + SHA-256) before anything is written; paths that could escape the
// target directory are rejected; existing files that differ are NOT overwritten unless you pass --force.

import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import process from 'node:process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { parseBundle } from './lib/bundle-format.mjs';

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const args = { input: undefined, out: process.cwd(), force: false, dryRun: false, list: false };
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const a = argv[i];
    if (a === '--out') args.out = path.resolve(argv[++i] ?? '');
    else if (a === '--in') args.input = argv[++i];
    else if (a === '--force') args.force = true;
    else if (a === '--dry-run') args.dryRun = true;
    else if (a === '--list') args.list = true;
    else if (a === '-h' || a === '--help') args.help = true;
    else if (a.startsWith('--')) throw new Error(`Unknown argument: ${a}`);
    else if (!args.input) args.input = a;
    else throw new Error(`Unexpected argument: ${a}`);
  }
  return args;
}

function main() {
  const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  if (args.help || !args.input) {
    process.stdout.write(
      readFileSync(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url), 'utf8')
        .split('\n')
        .slice(1, 14)
        .map((l) => l.replace(/^\/\/ ?/, ''))
        .join('\n') + '\n',
    );
    process.exitCode = args.help ? 0 : 1;
    return;
  }

  const { meta, files } = parseBundle(readFileSync(path.resolve(args.input), 'utf8'));

  if (meta.lineEndingsRepaired) {
    process.stderr.write(
      'Note: the bundle had its line endings converted to CRLF (editor/git autocrlf?). ' +
        'They were converted back, and every file still matches its checksum.\n',
    );
  }

  if (args.list) {
    for (const f of files) process.stdout.write(`${f.path}  (${f.content.length} bytes, ${f.enc})\n`);
    process.stdout.write(
      `\n${files.length} file(s), generated ${meta.generated}${meta.note ? `, ${meta.note}` : ''}\n`,
    );
    return;
  }

  const root = path.resolve(args.out);
  const counts = { created: 0, overwritten: 0, unchanged: 0, skipped: 0 };

  for (const file of files) {
    const target = path.resolve(root, ...file.path.split('/'));
    // Defence in depth on top of the path check in parseBundle
    if (target !== root && !target.startsWith(root + path.sep))
      throw new Error(`Path escapes target: ${file.path}`);

    let action;
    if (!existsSync(target)) action = 'created';
    else if (!statSync(target).isFile()) action = 'skipped';
    else if (readFileSync(target).equals(file.content)) action = 'unchanged';
    else action = args.force ? 'overwritten' : 'skipped';

    if (action === 'created' || action === 'overwritten') {
      if (!args.dryRun) {
        mkdirSync(path.dirname(target), { recursive: true });
        writeFileSync(target, file.content);
      }
    }
    counts[action]++;
    const note = action === 'skipped' ? '  (exists and differs; use --force to overwrite)' : '';
    process.stdout.write(`${(args.dryRun ? 'would be ' : '') + action}`.padEnd(20) + `${file.path}${note}\n`);
  }

  process.stdout.write(
    `\n${args.dryRun ? 'Dry run: ' : ''}${counts.created} created, ${counts.overwritten} overwritten, ` +
      `${counts.unchanged} unchanged, ${counts.skipped} skipped → ${root}\n`,
  );
  if (counts.skipped > 0) process.exitCode = 2;
}

try {
  main();
} catch (error) {
  process.stderr.write(`restore: ${error.message}\n`);
  process.exitCode = 1;
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl a9ee0738b50d7c0620dc568b71624ed5fbb44ff5146b1418653e2e5127611027 src/config/env.ts
import { z } from 'zod';

const flag = (def: 'true' | 'false' = 'false') =>
  z
    .enum(['true', 'false'])
    .default(def)
    .transform((v) => v === 'true');

const intFrom = (def: number) => z.coerce.number().int().positive().default(def);

const schema = z.object({
  PORT: intFrom(3050),
  NODE_ENV: z.string().default('development'),
  LOG_LEVEL: z.enum(['debug', 'info', 'warn', 'error', 'silent']).default('info'),

  SECRET_KEY: z.string().min(16, 'SECRET_KEY must be at least 16 characters'),
  ADMIN_USERNAME: z.string().min(1, 'ADMIN_USERNAME is required'),
  ADMIN_PASSWORD: z.string().min(1, 'ADMIN_PASSWORD is required'),
  JWT_EXPIRES_IN: z.string().default('60d'),
  LEGACY_UNVERSIONED_ROUTES: flag('true'),

  CLUSTER_API_URL: z.string().default(''),
  KUBECONFIG_PATH: z.string().optional(),
  DEPLOY_PROJECT_REQUESTER: z.string().default('devspaces-support-team'),
  DEV_SPACES_SERVICE_FQDN: z.string().default(''),

  DEVREG_URL: z.string().min(1, 'DEVREG_URL is required'),
  EDITOR_VSCODE: z.string().default('che-code'),
  EDITOR_INTELLIJ: z.string().default('che-idea'),
  EDITOR_GOLAND: z.string().default('che-goland'),
  WORKSPACE_PROXY_URL: z.string().optional(),

  MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES: intFrom(3),
  MAX_TOTAL_WORKSPACES: intFrom(5),

  POSTGRES_ENABLED: flag('true'),
  POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: z.string().default('localhost'),
  POSTGRES_PORT: intFrom(5432),
  POSTGRES_DATABASE: z.string().default(''),
  POSTGRES_USER: z.string().default(''),
  POSTGRES_PASSWORD: z.string().default(''),
  // The existing database requires TLS with a CA file chosen by NODE_ENV (see infra/db/pg-ssl.ts).
  POSTGRES_SSL: flag('true'),
  POSTGRES_CA_FILE: z.string().optional(),
  POSTGRES_CERT_DIR: z.string().optional(),
  POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME: z.string().default('metrics."devspaces-metrics"'),

  NOTIFICATION_MAIL_URL: z.string().optional(),
  NOTIFICATION_EMAIL_USERNAME: z.string().default(''),
  NOTIFICATION_EMAIL_PASSWORD: z.string().default(''),
  NOTIFICATION_SENDER: z.string().default('noreply@example.com'),
  NOTIFICATION_CC: z.string().optional(),
  EMAIL_DOMAIN: z.string().default('example.com'),
  USER_DIRECTORY_GRAPHQL_URL: z.string().optional(),

  GITHUB_URL: z.string().default('https://github.com'),
  GITHUB_USER_SUFFIX: z.string().default(''),
});

/** `'1524'` → `1524`, `"a b"` → `a b`; anything not wrapped in one matching pair of quotes is untouched. */
export function unquote(value: string): string {
  const match = /^(['"])(.*)\1$/s.exec(value);
  return match?.[2] ?? value;
}

export interface AppConfig {
  port: number;
  nodeEnv: string;
  logLevel: 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error' | 'silent';
  /** Serve the pre-versioning paths (/login, /:soeid/...) as deprecated aliases of /api/v1. */
  legacyRoutes: boolean;
  auth: { secretKey: string; adminUsername: string; adminPassword: string; expiresIn: string };
  cluster: {
    apiUrl: string;
    kubeconfigPath?: string;
    deployProjectRequester: string;
    devSpacesServiceFqdn: string;
  };
  devreg: {
    url: string;
    editors: { vscode: string; intellij: string; goland: string };
    workspaceProxyUrl?: string;
  };
  limits: { maxRunning: number; maxTotal: number };
  postgres: {
    enabled: boolean;
    host: string;
    port: number;
    database: string;
    user: string;
    password: string;
    ssl: boolean;
    /** Explicit CA bundle; when unset the file is chosen from NODE_ENV (dev.pem / uat.pem / prod.pem). */
    caFile?: string;
    certDir?: string;
    /** NODE_ENV, which selects the CA file and whether the hostname check is relaxed (development only). */
    env: string;
    /** Existing metrics table, e.g. metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev". The service only ever inserts into it. */
    table: string;
  };
  notification: {
    url?: string;
    username: string;
    password: string;
    sender: string;
    cc?: string;
    emailDomain: string;
    userDirectoryUrl?: string;
  };
  github: { url: string; userSuffix: string };
}

/** Parse and validate the environment once, at startup. Throws a readable error listing every problem. */
export function loadConfig(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): AppConfig {
  // An exported-but-empty variable (`FOO=`) is treated as unset. Values that still carry their shell quotes
  // (`'1524'`, `'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"'` from a .env file or a pasted export line) are unwrapped.
  const cleaned = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(env)
      .filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined && v !== '')
      .map(([k, v]) => [k, unquote(v as string)]),
  );
  const parsed = schema.safeParse(cleaned);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    const problems = parsed.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join('.') || '(root)'}: ${i.message}`);
    throw new Error(`Invalid configuration:\n  - ${problems.join('\n  - ')}`);
  }
  const e = parsed.data;
  if (e.POSTGRES_ENABLED && (!e.POSTGRES_DATABASE || !e.POSTGRES_USER)) {
    throw new Error(
      'Invalid configuration:\n  - POSTGRES_DATABASE and POSTGRES_USER are required when POSTGRES_ENABLED=true',
    );
  }
  return {
    port: e.PORT,
    nodeEnv: e.NODE_ENV,
    logLevel: e.LOG_LEVEL,
    legacyRoutes: e.LEGACY_UNVERSIONED_ROUTES,
    auth: {
      secretKey: e.SECRET_KEY,
      adminUsername: e.ADMIN_USERNAME,
      adminPassword: e.ADMIN_PASSWORD,
      expiresIn: e.JWT_EXPIRES_IN,
    },
    cluster: {
      apiUrl: e.CLUSTER_API_URL,
      kubeconfigPath: e.KUBECONFIG_PATH,
      deployProjectRequester: e.DEPLOY_PROJECT_REQUESTER,
      devSpacesServiceFqdn: e.DEV_SPACES_SERVICE_FQDN,
    },
    devreg: {
      url: e.DEVREG_URL,
      editors: { vscode: e.EDITOR_VSCODE, intellij: e.EDITOR_INTELLIJ, goland: e.EDITOR_GOLAND },
      workspaceProxyUrl: e.WORKSPACE_PROXY_URL,
    },
    limits: { maxRunning: e.MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES, maxTotal: e.MAX_TOTAL_WORKSPACES },
    postgres: {
      enabled: e.POSTGRES_ENABLED,
      host: e.POSTGRES_HOSTNAME,
      port: e.POSTGRES_PORT,
      database: e.POSTGRES_DATABASE,
      user: e.POSTGRES_USER,
      password: e.POSTGRES_PASSWORD,
      ssl: e.POSTGRES_SSL,
      caFile: e.POSTGRES_CA_FILE,
      certDir: e.POSTGRES_CERT_DIR,
      env: e.NODE_ENV,
      table: e.POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME,
    },
    notification: {
      url: e.NOTIFICATION_MAIL_URL,
      username: e.NOTIFICATION_EMAIL_USERNAME,
      password: e.NOTIFICATION_EMAIL_PASSWORD,
      sender: e.NOTIFICATION_SENDER,
      cc: e.NOTIFICATION_CC,
      emailDomain: e.EMAIL_DOMAIN,
      userDirectoryUrl: e.USER_DIRECTORY_GRAPHQL_URL,
    },
    github: { url: e.GITHUB_URL.replace(/\/$/, ''), userSuffix: e.GITHUB_USER_SUFFIX },
  };
}

import type { AppConfig } from './config/env.js';
import { MetricsRepository, NoopMetricsStore, createPgPool } from './infra/db/MetricsRepository.js';
import { DevfileRegistryClient, createRegistryHttp } from './infra/devreg/DevfileRegistryClient.js';
import { EmailNotifier, NoopNotifier } from './infra/email/EmailNotifier.js';
import { GraphqlUserDirectory, NullUserDirectory } from './infra/email/GraphqlUserDirectory.js';
import { KubeClient, loadKubeConfig } from './infra/kube/KubeClient.js';
import {
  systemClock,
  type Clock,
  type DevfileRegistry,
  type KubeGateway,
  type MetricsStore,
  type Notifier,
} from './infra/ports.js';
import { DeploymentNamespaceService } from './services/DeploymentNamespaceService.js';
import { GitConfigService } from './services/GitConfigService.js';
import { MetricsRecorder } from './services/MetricsRecorder.js';
import { NamespaceService } from './services/NamespaceService.js';
import { PatService } from './services/PatService.js';
import { WorkspaceService } from './services/WorkspaceService.js';
import type { Logger } from './utils/logger.js';

export interface Dependencies {
  kube: KubeGateway;
  metrics: MetricsStore;
  registry: DevfileRegistry;
  notifier: Notifier;
  clock?: Clock;
}

export interface Container {
  config: AppConfig;
  logger: Logger;
  kube: KubeGateway;
  metrics: MetricsStore;
  registry: DevfileRegistry;
  recorder: MetricsRecorder;
  namespaces: NamespaceService;
  workspaces: WorkspaceService;
  pats: PatService;
  gitConfig: GitConfigService;
  deployments: DeploymentNamespaceService;
}

/** The single place where adapters are wired into services. Tests call this with fakes. */
export function buildContainer(config: AppConfig, logger: Logger, deps: Dependencies): Container {
  const clock = deps.clock ?? systemClock;
  const recorder = new MetricsRecorder(deps.metrics, clock, logger);
  const namespaces = new NamespaceService(
    deps.kube,
    recorder,
    { workspaceProxyUrl: config.devreg.workspaceProxyUrl, emailDomain: config.notification.emailDomain },
    logger,
  );
  return {
    config,
    logger,
    kube: deps.kube,
    metrics: deps.metrics,
    registry: deps.registry,
    recorder,
    namespaces,
    workspaces: new WorkspaceService(
      deps.kube,
      namespaces,
      deps.registry,
      recorder,
      {
        limits: config.limits,
        editors: config.devreg.editors,
        devSpacesServiceFqdn: config.cluster.devSpacesServiceFqdn,
      },
      clock,
      logger,
    ),
    pats: new PatService(deps.kube, namespaces, recorder, config.github),
    gitConfig: new GitConfigService(deps.kube, namespaces, recorder),
    deployments: new DeploymentNamespaceService(
      deps.kube,
      recorder,
      deps.notifier,
      config.cluster.deployProjectRequester,
      logger,
    ),
  };
}

/** Real adapters for a running pod. */
export function createProductionDependencies(config: AppConfig, logger: Logger): Dependencies {
  const directory = config.notification.userDirectoryUrl
    ? new GraphqlUserDirectory(config.notification.userDirectoryUrl, logger)
    : new NullUserDirectory();
  return {
    kube: new KubeClient(loadKubeConfig(config.cluster.kubeconfigPath)),
    metrics: config.postgres.enabled
      ? new MetricsRepository(createPgPool(config.postgres, logger), config.postgres.table, logger)
      : new NoopMetricsStore(),
    registry: new DevfileRegistryClient(createRegistryHttp(config.devreg.url)),
    notifier: config.notification.url
      ? new EmailNotifier(config.notification, config.cluster.apiUrl, directory, logger)
      : new NoopNotifier(),
  };
}

export interface CatalogEntry {

  tags?: string[];
  versions?: CatalogVersion[];
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 4223cc814ef9cda93ace9dbd33d088c2a1aa89085c0be108d040a79901d89eea src/factories/devfile-updater.ts
import { dump, load } from 'js-yaml';
import type { Devfile, EditorKey } from '../domain/types.js';
import { BadRequestError } from '../utils/errors.js';

interface EditorProfile {
  serverComponentName: string;
  serverPath: string;
  partOf: string;
  targetPort: number;
}

/** JetBrains editors need their server wired into the stack's `tools` container; VS Code needs nothing. */
const PROFILES: Record<EditorKey, EditorProfile | null> = {
  vscode: null,
  intellij: {
    serverComponentName: 'che-idea-server',
    serverPath: '/che-idea-server',
    partOf: 'che-idea-server.eclipse.org',
    targetPort: 2222,
  },
  goland: {
    serverComponentName: 'che-goland-server',
    serverPath: '/che-goland-server',
    partOf: 'che-goland-server.eclipse.org',
    targetPort: 3400,
  },
};

export const DEFAULT_HOME_VOLUME_SIZE = '10Gi';

export function parseDevfile(yamlText: string): Devfile {
  let parsed: unknown;
  try {
    parsed = load(yamlText);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Devfile is not valid YAML: ${(error as Error).message}`, 'INVALID_DEVFILE');
  }
  if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Devfile must be a YAML mapping', 'INVALID_DEVFILE');
  }
  const devfile = parsed as Devfile;
  if (!Array.isArray(devfile.components)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Devfile has no components', 'INVALID_DEVFILE');
  }
  return devfile;
}

/** Returns a modified deep copy; the input is never mutated. */
export function applyEditorProfile(
  devfile: Devfile,
  editor: EditorKey,
  homeVolumeSize = DEFAULT_HOME_VOLUME_SIZE,
): Devfile {
  const updated = structuredClone(devfile);
  const profile = PROFILES[editor];
  if (!profile) return updated;

  delete updated.commands;
  delete updated.events;

  const components = updated.components ?? [];
  const tools = components.find((c) => c.name === 'tools');
  if (tools?.container) {
    if (tools.container.volumeMounts) {
      tools.container.volumeMounts = tools.container.volumeMounts.filter((vm) => vm.name !== 'venv');
      const editorMount = tools.container.volumeMounts.find((vm) => vm.name === 'checode');
      if (editorMount) {
        editorMount.name = profile.serverComponentName;
        editorMount.path = profile.serverPath;
      }
    }
    delete tools.container.env;
    tools.container.endpoints = [
      {
        attributes: {
          cookiesAuthEnabled: true,
          discoverable: false,
          type: 'main',
          urlRewriteSupported: true,
        },
        exposure: 'public',
        name: profile.serverComponentName,
        protocol: 'https',
        secure: true,
        targetPort: profile.targetPort,
      },
    ];
    tools.attributes = { ...tools.attributes, 'app.kubernetes.io/part-of': profile.partOf };
  }

  updated.components = components.filter((c) => !(c.name === 'venv' && c.volume !== undefined));

  const editorVolume = updated.components.find((c) => c.name === 'checode' && c.volume !== undefined);
  if (editorVolume) editorVolume.name = profile.serverComponentName;

  const home = updated.components.find((c) => c.name === 'home' && c.volume !== undefined);
  if (home?.volume) home.volume.size = homeVolumeSize;

  return updated;
}

export function updateDevfileYaml(yamlText: string, editor: EditorKey): string {
  return dump(applyEditorProfile(parseDevfile(yamlText), editor), { indent: 2 });
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 68e5bbc464e816685e318623188faefa7bd97cf0577a5bdbdaf061c70945af24 src/factories/workspace-factory.ts
import type { Devfile, DevWorkspaceResource, EditorKey } from '../domain/types.js';

export const WORKSPACE_LABELS = {
  csiid: 'devspaces.citi.com/csiid',
  stack: 'devspaces.citi.com/stack',
  managedBy: 'app.kubernetes.io/managed-by',
} as const;

export interface BuildWorkspaceArgs {
  name: string;
  namespace: string;
  csiid: string;
  stack: string;
  stackVersion: string;
  devfile: Devfile;
  editor: EditorKey;
  /** Editor id as the Dev Spaces dashboard knows it, e.g. `che-code`. */
  editorId: string;
  devSpacesServiceFqdn: string;
  repositoryUrl?: string;
  repositoryBranch?: string;
}

const EDITOR_DISPLAY: Record<EditorKey, string> = {
  vscode: 'Visual Studio Code',
  intellij: 'IntelliJ IDEA',
  goland: 'GoLand',
};

function repoName(url: string): string {
  const last = url
    .replace(/\.git$/, '')
    .split('/')
    .filter(Boolean)
    .pop();
  return (last ?? 'project').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, '-');
}

export function buildDevWorkspaceObject(a: BuildWorkspaceArgs): DevWorkspaceResource {
  const projects = [...((a.devfile.projects as unknown[] | undefined) ?? [])];
  if (a.repositoryUrl) {
    projects.push({
      name: repoName(a.repositoryUrl),
      git: {
        remotes: { origin: a.repositoryUrl },
        ...(a.repositoryBranch ? { checkoutFrom: { revision: a.repositoryBranch } } : {}),
      },
    });
  }

  const template: NonNullable<NonNullable<DevWorkspaceResource['spec']>['template']> = {
    attributes: {
      'controller.devfile.io/devworkspace-config': {
        name: 'devworkspace-config',
        namespace: 'openshift-devspaces',
      },
      'controller.devfile.io/scc': 'container-build',
      'controller.devfile.io/storage-type': 'per-workspace',
    },
    components: a.devfile.components ?? [],
  };
  if (projects.length > 0) template.projects = projects;
  if (Array.isArray(a.devfile.commands)) template.commands = a.devfile.commands;
  if (a.devfile.events) template.events = a.devfile.events;

  return {
    apiVersion: 'workspace.devfile.io/v1alpha2',
    kind: 'DevWorkspace',
    metadata: {
      name: a.name,
      namespace: a.namespace,
      annotations: {
        dw: a.name,
        workspaceVersion: a.stackVersion,
        workspaceDisplayName: a.devfile.metadata?.displayName ?? a.devfile.metadata?.name ?? a.stack,
        workspaceDescription: a.devfile.metadata?.description ?? '',
        editorDisplayName: EDITOR_DISPLAY[a.editor],
      },
      labels: {
        [WORKSPACE_LABELS.csiid]: a.csiid,
        [WORKSPACE_LABELS.stack]: a.stack,
        [WORKSPACE_LABELS.managedBy]: 'workspace-mgr',
      },
    },
    spec: {
      started: true,
      routingClass: 'che',
      template,
      contributions: [
        {
          name: 'ide',
          uri: `${a.devSpacesServiceFqdn}/dashboard/api/editors/devfile?che-editor=che-incubator/${a.editorId}/latest`,
        },
      ],
    },
  };
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 45b9c5fffe345d409bc3da27ac99c2b5d0bc2d771aced37388f922cd953b3218 src/http/app.ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import express, { Router, type Express, type Request, type RequestHandler, type Response } from 'express';
import type { Container } from '../container.js';
import { parseCsiid, parseTokenName } from '../utils/names.js';
import { UnauthorizedError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { authenticate, authorizeSoeid, checkAdminCredentials, getSoeid, signToken } from './auth.js';
import { asyncHandler, createErrorHandler, errorBody, notFoundHandler } from './errors.js';
import {
  createWorkspaceSchema,
  csiidBodySchema,
  csiidQuerySchema,
  gitConfigBodySchema,
  loginSchema,
  parse,
  patBodySchema,
} from './schemas.js';

export const API_PREFIX = '/api/v1';

const param = (req: Request, name: string): string => req.params[name] ?? '';

/** RFC 8594-style signalling that an unversioned path is going away. */
const deprecatedAlias: RequestHandler = (req, res, next) => {
  res.setHeader('Deprecation', 'true');
  res.setHeader('Link', `<${API_PREFIX}${req.path}>; rel="successor-version"`);
  next();
};

export function createApp(c: Container): Express {
  const app = express();
  app.disable('x-powered-by');
  app.use(express.json({ limit: '256kb' }));

  app.use((req, res, next) => {
    const incoming = req.header('x-request-id');
    const requestId = incoming && /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{8,64}$/.test(incoming) ? incoming : randomUUID();
    res.locals.requestId = requestId;
    res.setHeader('x-request-id', requestId);
    const started = Date.now();
    res.on('finish', () =>
      c.logger.info('request', {
        requestId,
        method: req.method,
        path: req.path,
        status: res.statusCode,
        ms: Date.now() - started,
        soeid: res.locals.soeid as string | undefined,
      }),
    );
    next();
  });

  // ---- operations ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  app.get('/healthz', (_req, res) => {
    res.send('OK');
  });

  app.get(
    '/readyz',
    asyncHandler(async (_req, res) => {
      const checks: Record<string, string> = {};
      await Promise.all([
        c.kube.ping().then(
          () => (checks.kubernetes = 'ok'),
          () => (checks.kubernetes = 'unavailable'),
        ),
        c.metrics.ping().then(
          () => (checks.postgres = 'ok'),
          () => (checks.postgres = 'unavailable'),
        ),
      ]);
      const ready = Object.values(checks).every((v) => v === 'ok');
      res.status(ready ? 200 : 503).json({ status: ready ? 'ready' : 'not-ready', checks });
    }),
  );

  // ---- versioned API (mounted at /api/v1, and optionally at / as a deprecated alias) --------
  const api = Router();

  api.post('/login', (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const { username, password } = parse(loginSchema, req.body);
      if (!checkAdminCredentials(c.config.auth, username, password)) {
        throw new UnauthorizedError('Username or password is incorrect');
      }
      res.json({ token: signToken(c.config.auth, { soeid: username, role: 'admin' }) });
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  });

  const auth = authenticate(c.config.auth);

  api.get(
    '/catalog',
    auth,
    asyncHandler(async (_req, res) => {
      res.json(await c.registry.getCatalog());
    }),
  );

  // ---- per-user routes ----------------------------------------------------------------------
  const user = Router({ mergeParams: true });

  user.get(
    '/projects',
    asyncHandler(async (_req, res) => {
      res.json(await c.namespaces.listProjects(getSoeid(res)));
    }),
  );

  user.get(
    '/workspaces',
    asyncHandler(async (_req, res) => {
      res.json(await c.workspaces.list(getSoeid(res)));
    }),
  );

  user.get(
    '/workspaces/:id',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      res.json(await c.workspaces.get(getSoeid(res), param(req, 'id')));
    }),
  );

  user.post(
    '/workspaces',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const body = parse(createWorkspaceSchema, req.body);
      const workspace = await c.workspaces.create({
        soeid: getSoeid(res),
        csiid: parseCsiid(body.csiid),
        devworkspaceValue: body.devworkspaceValue,
        editor: body.editor,
        stackVersion: body.stackVersion,
        repositoryUrl: body.repositoryUrl,
        repositoryBranch: body.repositoryBranch,
      });
      res.status(200).json(workspace);
    }),
  );

  user.delete(
    '/workspaces/:id',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const id = param(req, 'id');
      await c.workspaces.delete(getSoeid(res), id);
      res.json({ message: `Workspace with id ${id} is terminating.` });
    }),
  );

  user.post(
    '/workspaces/:id/start',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const id = param(req, 'id');
      await c.workspaces.start(getSoeid(res), id);
      res.json({ message: `Workspace with id ${id} is starting.` });
    }),
  );

  user.post(
    '/workspaces/:id/stop',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const id = param(req, 'id');
      await c.workspaces.stop(getSoeid(res), id);
      res.json({ message: `Workspace with id ${id} is stopping.` });
    }),
  );

  // Personal access tokens
  user.get(
    '/pats',
    asyncHandler(async (_req, res) => {
      res.json(await c.pats.list(getSoeid(res)));
    }),
  );
  user.get(
    '/pats/:tokenName',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const { csiid } = parse(csiidQuerySchema, req.query);
      res.json(await c.pats.view(getSoeid(res), parseCsiid(csiid), parseTokenName(param(req, 'tokenName'))));
    }),
  );
  user.post(
    '/pats/:tokenName',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const body = parse(patBodySchema, req.body);
      const created = await c.pats.create(
        getSoeid(res),
        parseCsiid(body.csiid),
        parseTokenName(param(req, 'tokenName')),
        body.token,
      );
      res.status(201).json(created);
    }),
  );
  user.put(
    '/pats/:tokenName',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const body = parse(patBodySchema, req.body);
      res.json(
        await c.pats.update(
          getSoeid(res),
          parseCsiid(body.csiid),
          parseTokenName(param(req, 'tokenName')),
          body.token,
        ),
      );
    }),
  );
  user.delete(
    '/pats/:tokenName',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const { csiid } = parse(csiidQuerySchema, req.query);
      const tokenName = parseTokenName(param(req, 'tokenName'));
      const removed = await c.pats.delete(getSoeid(res), parseCsiid(csiid), tokenName);
      res.json({
        message: removed ? `PAT '${tokenName}' deleted.` : `PAT '${tokenName}' not found; nothing to delete.`,
      });
    }),
  );

  // Git config
  user.get(
    '/gitconfig',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const { csiid } = parse(csiidQuerySchema, req.query);
      res.json(await c.gitConfig.view(getSoeid(res), parseCsiid(csiid)));
    }),
  );
  user.put(
    '/gitconfig',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const body = parse(gitConfigBodySchema, req.body);
      res.json(await c.gitConfig.upsert(getSoeid(res), parseCsiid(body.csiid), body.content));
    }),
  );
  user.delete(
    '/gitconfig',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const { csiid } = parse(csiidQuerySchema, req.query);
      const removed = await c.gitConfig.delete(getSoeid(res), parseCsiid(csiid));
      res.json({ message: removed ? 'Git config deleted.' : 'Git config not found; nothing to delete.' });
    }),
  );

  // Deployment (build/deploy) projects
  user.get(
    '/deployment-projects',
    asyncHandler(async (_req, res) => {
      res.json(await c.deployments.list(getSoeid(res)));
    }),
  );
  user.post(
    '/deployment-projects',
    asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
      const { csiid } = parse(csiidBodySchema, req.body);
      const result = await c.deployments.ensure(getSoeid(res), parseCsiid(csiid));
      res.status(result.exists ? 200 : 201).json(result);
    }),
  );
  user.delete(
    '/deployment-projects',
    asyncHandler(async (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
      res.json(await c.deployments.delete(getSoeid(res)));
    }),
  );

  api.use('/:soeid', auth, authorizeSoeid(), user);

  app.use(API_PREFIX, api);
  if (c.config.legacyRoutes) {
    // Pre-versioning paths kept so existing Orion builds keep working while they migrate to /api/v1.
    const legacy = Router();
    // Unknown /api/... paths (e.g. a future /api/v2) must 404, not be read as `/:soeid` = "api".
    legacy.use((req, _res, next) =>
      req.path === '/api' || req.path.startsWith('/api/') ? next('router') : next(),
    );
    legacy.use(deprecatedAlias, api);
    app.use('/', legacy);
  }

  app.use(notFoundHandler);
  app.use(createErrorHandler(c.logger));
  return app;
}

export { errorBody };

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl a72df90092a3b730385a4efb584ba916fb2bf23497b97eb4a9f6b15276b07bbe src/http/auth.ts
import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import type { NextFunction, Request, RequestHandler, Response } from 'express';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import type { AppConfig } from '../config/env.js';
import type { Principal } from '../domain/types.js';
import { ForbiddenError, UnauthorizedError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { parseSoeid } from '../utils/names.js';

const sha256 = (value: string): Buffer => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest();

/** Constant-time string comparison (hashing equalises lengths). */
export function safeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  return timingSafeEqual(sha256(a), sha256(b));
}

export function checkAdminCredentials(auth: AppConfig['auth'], username: string, password: string): boolean {
  // Evaluate both so timing does not reveal which one was wrong.
  const userOk = safeEqual(username, auth.adminUsername);
  const passOk = safeEqual(password, auth.adminPassword);
  return userOk && passOk;
}

export function signToken(auth: AppConfig['auth'], principal: Principal): string {
  return jwt.sign({ role: principal.role }, auth.secretKey, {
    algorithm: 'HS256',
    subject: principal.soeid,
    expiresIn: auth.expiresIn as jwt.SignOptions['expiresIn'],
  });
}

export function getPrincipal(res: Response): Principal {
  const principal = res.locals.principal as Principal | undefined;
  if (!principal) throw new UnauthorizedError();
  return principal;
}

/** `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>` → `res.locals.principal`. */
export function authenticate(auth: AppConfig['auth']): RequestHandler {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const match = /^Bearer (\S+)$/.exec(req.headers.authorization ?? '');
    if (!match?.[1]) return next(new UnauthorizedError('A bearer token is required for authentication'));
    try {
      const decoded = jwt.verify(match[1], auth.secretKey, { algorithms: ['HS256'] });
      if (typeof decoded === 'string' || typeof decoded.sub !== 'string') throw new Error('malformed claims');
      const role = decoded.role === 'admin' ? 'admin' : 'user';
      res.locals.principal = { soeid: decoded.sub, role } satisfies Principal;
      return next();
    } catch {
      return next(new UnauthorizedError('Invalid token'));
    }
  };
}

/** Validates `:soeid` and makes sure the caller may act for that user (admins may act for anyone). */
export function authorizeSoeid(): RequestHandler {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    try {
      const soeid = parseSoeid(req.params.soeid ?? '');
      const principal = getPrincipal(res);
      if (principal.role !== 'admin' && principal.soeid.toLowerCase() !== soeid) {
        throw new ForbiddenError('You may only access your own resources');
      }
      res.locals.soeid = soeid;
      next();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  };
}

export const getSoeid = (res: Response): string => res.locals.soeid as string;
@@@END@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e
@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 81175f0d8599a983a6fd1048a9c1df7da44eaf9dd410a26fe788543bf8969ed3 src/http/errors.ts
import type { ErrorRequestHandler, NextFunction, Request, RequestHandler, Response } from 'express';
import { AppError, errorMessage } from '../utils/errors.js';
import type { Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';

export interface ErrorBody {
  error: string;
  message: string;
  code: string;
}

export const errorBody = (message: string, code: string): ErrorBody => ({ error: message, message, code });

/** Express 4 does not catch rejected promises; this forwards them to the error handler. */
export const asyncHandler =
  (fn: (req: Request, res: Response) => Promise<void>): RequestHandler =>
  (req, res, next: NextFunction) => {
    fn(req, res).catch(next);
  };

export const notFoundHandler: RequestHandler = (req, res) => {
  res.status(404).json(errorBody(`Route not found: ${req.method} ${req.path}`, 'ROUTE_NOT_FOUND'));
};

export function createErrorHandler(logger: Logger): ErrorRequestHandler {
  return (err: unknown, req, res, next) => {
    if (res.headersSent) return next(err);
    const requestId = res.locals.requestId as string | undefined;

    if (err instanceof AppError) {
      if (err.status >= 500)
        logger.error('request.failed', { requestId, code: err.code, error: err.message });
      res.status(err.status).json(errorBody(err.message, err.code));
      return;
    }
    // body-parser errors carry a status and a type
    const parserError = err as { status?: number; type?: string };
    if (parserError.type === 'entity.parse.failed') {
      res.status(400).json(errorBody('Request body is not valid JSON', 'INVALID_JSON'));
      return;
    }
    if (parserError.type === 'entity.too.large') {
      res.status(413).json(errorBody('Request body is too large', 'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE'));
      return;
    }
    // Never leak internals for unexpected errors.
    logger.error('request.crashed', { requestId, path: req.path, error: errorMessage(err) });
    res.status(500).json(errorBody('Internal server error', 'INTERNAL_ERROR'));
  };
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 095483dd33b3e5bfeb087b80beca0cbfc0de083fb68ac73e0bca482c3e390f86 src/http/schemas.ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { BadRequestError } from '../utils/errors.js';

const identifier = /^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$/;

const csiid = z
  .union([z.string(), z.number()])
  .transform((v) => String(v))
  .pipe(z.string().regex(/^\d{1,10}$/, 'csiid must be numeric'));

export const loginSchema = z.strictObject({ username: z.string().min(1), password: z.string().min(1) });

export const createWorkspaceSchema = z.strictObject({
  devworkspaceValue: z.string().min(1).max(100).regex(identifier, 'invalid stack name'),
  csiid,
  editor: z.enum(['vscode', 'intellij', 'goland']).default('vscode'),
  stackVersion: z.string().max(50).regex(identifier, 'invalid stack version').optional(),
  repositoryUrl: z
    .url({ protocol: /^https$/ })
    .max(500)
    .optional(),
  repositoryBranch: z
    .string()
    .max(200)
    .regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9._/-]+$/, 'invalid branch name')
    .optional(),
});

export const csiidQuerySchema = z.object({ csiid });
export const csiidBodySchema = z.strictObject({ csiid });
export const patBodySchema = z.strictObject({ csiid, token: z.string().min(1).max(200) });
export const gitConfigBodySchema = z.strictObject({ csiid, content: z.string().min(1) });

export function parse<T extends z.ZodType>(schema: T, data: unknown): z.output<T> {
  const result = schema.safeParse(data ?? {});
  if (!result.success) {
    const message = result.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join('.') || 'body'}: ${i.message}`).join('; ');
    throw new BadRequestError(message, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');
  }
  return result.data;
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 28ddf1c1bfbebc746c58bfeb5f727524a9de7cc596007460d01ef607c433b816 src/index.ts
import type { Server } from 'node:http';
import { loadConfig } from './config/env.js';
import { buildContainer, createProductionDependencies } from './container.js';
import { createApp } from './http/app.js';
import { createLogger } from './utils/logger.js';
import { errorMessage } from './utils/errors.js';

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  const config = loadConfig();
  const logger = createLogger(config.logLevel);
  const container = buildContainer(config, logger, createProductionDependencies(config, logger));
  const server: Server = createApp(container).listen(config.port, () =>
    logger.info('server.listening', { port: config.port, cluster: config.cluster.apiUrl }),
  );

  let shuttingDown = false;
  const shutdown = (signal: string): void => {
    if (shuttingDown) return;
    shuttingDown = true;
    logger.info('server.shutdown', { signal });
    server.close(() => {
      container.metrics.close().finally(() => process.exit(0));
    });
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(1), 10_000).unref();
  };
  process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));
  process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'));
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  process.stderr.write(`Fatal: ${errorMessage(error)}\n`);
  process.exit(1);
});
