@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 5e7d858e09542cd3b1764ea68c52574667ef7879e4732ae2078b4bb6a79bee56 src/infra/db/MetricsRepository.ts
import pg from 'pg';
import type { AppConfig } from '../../config/env.js';
import type { MetricsRecord } from '../../domain/types.js';
import { errorMessage } from '../../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../../utils/logger.js';
import type { MetricsStore } from '../ports.js';
import { buildSslOptions } from './pg-ssl.js';

/** The subset of `pg.Pool` the repository needs (also satisfied by pg-mem's pool in tests). */
export interface Queryable {
  query(text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<unknown>;
  end(): Promise<void>;
}

const TABLE_RE = /^("[\w-]+"|[A-Za-z_]\w*)(\.("[\w-]+"|[A-Za-z_]\w*))?$/;

export function assertSafeTableName(table: string): string {
  if (!TABLE_RE.test(table)) throw new Error(`Unsafe metrics table name: ${table}`);
  return table;
}

/** `metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"` → `{ schema: 'metrics', name: 'devspaces-metrics-dev' }`. */
export function parseTableName(table: string): { schema?: string; name: string } {
  const unq = (s: string): string => s.replace(/^"|"$/g, '');
  const parts = assertSafeTableName(table).match(/"[\w-]+"|[A-Za-z_]\w*/g) ?? [];
  const name = unq(parts[parts.length - 1] ?? '');
  return parts.length > 1 ? { schema: unq(parts[0] ?? ''), name } : { name };
}

export function createPgPool(config: AppConfig['postgres'], logger: Logger = silentLogger): pg.Pool {
  const pool = new pg.Pool({
    host: config.host,
    port: config.port,
    database: config.database,
    user: config.user,
    password: config.password,
    ssl: buildSslOptions(config),
    max: 5,
    connectionTimeoutMillis: 3000,
    idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
    statement_timeout: 5000,
  });
  // An idle client dropped by the server emits 'error'; unhandled, it would crash the process.
  pool.on('error', (error) => logger.error('postgres.pool_error', { error: errorMessage(error) }));
  return pool;
}

/** UTC wall-clock text (`2026-01-02 03:04:05.678`), the format the original service wrote. */
export const formatUtc = (date: Date): string => date.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').replace('Z', '');

/** Every value the service can store, keyed by column name. */
export function candidateColumns(r: MetricsRecord): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    timestamp: formatUtc(r.timestamp),
    csiid: r.csiid,
    soeid: r.soeid,
    eventtype: r.eventtype,
    resourcename: r.resourcename,
    resourcetype: r.resourcetype,
    status: JSON.stringify(r.status),
    limitcpu: r.limitcpu,
    limitmemory: r.limitmemory,
    requestcpu: r.requestcpu,
    requestmemory: r.requestmemory,
    duration: r.duration,
    storage: r.storage,
    useraction: r.useraction,
    workspaceversion: r.workspaceversion,
    editorname: r.editorname,
    errordetail: r.status.failed ? r.status.error : null,
  };
}

/**
 * Writes metrics rows into an EXISTING table. It never issues DDL (no CREATE/ALTER/DROP): it reads the table's
 * column list once and inserts only the columns that exist, so a table that predates some of our fields still works.
 */
export class MetricsRepository implements MetricsStore {
  private readonly table: string;
  private columns?: Promise<Set<string> | undefined>;
  private warnedMissing = false;

  constructor(
    private readonly db: Queryable,
    table = 'metrics."devspaces-metrics"',
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
  ) {
    this.table = assertSafeTableName(table);
  }

  private knownColumns(): Promise<Set<string> | undefined> {
    this.columns ??= this.discoverColumns();
    return this.columns;
  }

  /**
   * Read-only column discovery, two independent ways: the catalog (works with INSERT-only privileges),
   * then an empty SELECT whose result metadata lists the columns (works when the catalog is hidden).
   */
  private async discoverColumns(): Promise<Set<string> | undefined> {
    const errors: string[] = [];

    try {
      const { schema, name } = parseTableName(this.table);
      const result = (await this.db.query(
        schema
          ? 'SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_name = $2'
          : 'SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = current_schema() AND table_name = $1',
        schema ? [schema, name] : [name],
      )) as { rows?: { column_name: string }[] };
      const names = (result.rows ?? []).map((row) => row.column_name.toLowerCase());
      if (names.length > 0) return new Set(names);
    } catch (error) {
      errors.push(`information_schema: ${errorMessage(error)}`);
    }

    try {
      const result = (await this.db.query(`SELECT * FROM ${this.table} LIMIT 0`)) as {
        fields?: { name: string }[];
      };
      const names = (result.fields ?? []).map((field) => field.name.toLowerCase());
      if (names.length > 0) return new Set(names);
    } catch (error) {
      errors.push(`select: ${errorMessage(error)}`);
      // Do not cache a failure: try again on the next insert.
      this.columns = undefined;
    }

    this.logger.warn('metrics.columns_unknown', {
      table: this.table,
      reason: 'could not read the table columns; inserting all fields',
      errors: errors.join(' | '),
    });
    return undefined;
  }

  async insert(record: MetricsRecord): Promise<void> {
    const all = candidateColumns(record);
    const known = await this.knownColumns();
    const names = known ? Object.keys(all).filter((column) => known.has(column)) : Object.keys(all);

    if (known && !this.warnedMissing) {
      const missing = Object.keys(all).filter((column) => !known.has(column));
      if (missing.length > 0) {
        this.warnedMissing = true;
        this.logger.warn('metrics.columns_missing', { table: this.table, missing: missing.join(',') });
      }
    }
    if (names.length === 0) throw new Error(`Table ${this.table} has none of the expected metrics columns`);

    const columns = names.map((column) => `"${column}"`).join(', ');
    const placeholders = names.map((_, i) => `$${i + 1}`).join(', ');
    await this.db.query(
      `INSERT INTO ${this.table} (${columns}) VALUES (${placeholders})`,
      names.map((column) => all[column]),
    );
  }

  async ping(): Promise<void> {
    await this.db.query('SELECT 1');
  }

  close(): Promise<void> {
    return this.db.end();
  }
}

/** Used when POSTGRES_ENABLED=false (local runs); drops records. */
export class NoopMetricsStore implements MetricsStore {
  async insert(): Promise<void> {}
  async ping(): Promise<void> {}
  async close(): Promise<void> {}
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 1578559fcf5b368425e1057050783dcf66672c9df5b1f3241945ffa3bc495b96 src/infra/db/pg-ssl.ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import type { ConnectionOptions } from 'node:tls';
import type { AppConfig } from '../../config/env.js';

type PgConfig = AppConfig['postgres'];

/** Same mapping the original service used: one CA bundle per environment. */
const CERT_FILES: Record<string, string> = {
  development: 'dev.pem',
  uat: 'uat.pem',
  production: 'prod.pem',
};

/** Directory used by the original service when running in a container. */
const CONTAINER_CERT_DIR = '/app/backend/cert';

/**
 * Which CA bundle to trust: an explicit POSTGRES_CA_FILE, else `<certDir>/<dev|uat|prod>.pem` chosen by NODE_ENV
 * (unknown environments fall back to dev.pem). `certDir` defaults to `./certs` in development and
 * `/app/backend/cert` otherwise, as in the original code.
 */
export function resolveCaPath(
  config: Pick<PgConfig, 'env' | 'caFile' | 'certDir'>,
  cwd = process.cwd(),
): string {
  if (config.caFile) return config.caFile;
  const file = CERT_FILES[config.env] ?? 'dev.pem';
  const dir =
    config.certDir ?? (config.env === 'development' ? path.resolve(cwd, 'certs') : CONTAINER_CERT_DIR);
  return path.join(dir, file);
}

/**
 * TLS options for the existing Postgres server. The server certificate is always verified against the CA;
 * only in `development` is the hostname check skipped (the dev instance is reached through a different name).
 */
export function buildSslOptions(
  config: Pick<PgConfig, 'ssl' | 'env' | 'caFile' | 'certDir'>,
  readFile: (file: string) => string = (file) => readFileSync(file, 'utf8'),
): false | ConnectionOptions {
  if (!config.ssl) return false;
  const caPath = resolveCaPath(config);
  let ca: string;
  try {
    ca = readFile(caPath);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(
      `Cannot read the Postgres CA certificate at ${caPath} (NODE_ENV=${config.env}): ${(error as Error).message}. ` +
        'Put the CA there, or set POSTGRES_CA_FILE / POSTGRES_CERT_DIR.',
      { cause: error },
    );
  }
  return config.env === 'development'
    ? { rejectUnauthorized: true, checkServerIdentity: () => undefined, ca }
    : { rejectUnauthorized: true, ca };
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 1bf9d87822afedca45dd73f4f522977690b61f453d20140ce38e09f4beab8115 src/infra/devreg/DevfileRegistryClient.ts
import axios, { type AxiosInstance } from 'axios';
import type { CatalogEntry } from '../../domain/types.js';
import { UpstreamError, WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError } from '../../utils/errors.js';
import { describeError } from '../../utils/k8s-errors.js';
import type { DevfileRegistry } from '../ports.js';

export function createRegistryHttp(baseURL: string): AxiosInstance {
  return axios.create({ baseURL, timeout: 10_000, headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });
}

export class DevfileRegistryClient implements DevfileRegistry {
  constructor(private readonly http: AxiosInstance) {}

  async getCatalog(): Promise<CatalogEntry[]> {
    try {
      const res = await this.http.get<unknown>('v2index/all');
      if (!Array.isArray(res.data)) throw new Error('registry index is not a list');
      return res.data as CatalogEntry[];
    } catch (error) {
      throw new UpstreamError(describeError('Devfile registry catalog', error), { cause: error });
    }
  }

  async getDevfile(stack: string, version?: string): Promise<{ yaml: string; version: string }> {
    const catalog = await this.getCatalog();
    const entry = catalog.find((e) => e.name === stack);
    if (!entry) throw new WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError(`No such workspace config: ${stack}`);

    const versions = entry.versions ?? [];
    const chosen = version
      ? versions.find((v) => v.version === version)
      : (versions.find((v) => v.default) ?? versions[0]);
    if (!chosen) {
      throw new WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError(
        version ? `No version '${version}' for workspace config: ${stack}` : `No versions for ${stack}`,
      );
    }

    try {
      const res = await this.http.get<string>(
        `devfiles/${encodeURIComponent(stack)}/${encodeURIComponent(chosen.version)}`,
        { responseType: 'text', transformResponse: (d: unknown) => d, headers: { Accept: 'text/plain' } },
      );
      return { yaml: String(res.data), version: chosen.version };
    } catch (error) {
      throw new UpstreamError(describeError(`Devfile '${stack}'`, error), { cause: error });
    }
  }
}

import type { AppConfig } from '../../config/env.js';
import { errorMessage } from '../../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../../utils/logger.js';
import type {
  NamespaceCreatedEmail,
  NamespaceExistsEmail,
  NamespacesDeletedEmail,
  Notifier,
  UserDirectory,
} from '../ports.js';
import {
  clusterLinks,
  deletedNamespacesEmailBody,
  existingNamespaceEmailBody,
  newNamespaceEmailBody,
} from './email-templates.js';

export type FetchFn = (url: string, init: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;

export class EmailNotifier implements Notifier {
  constructor(
    private readonly config: AppConfig['notification'],
    private readonly clusterApiUrl: string,
    private readonly directory: UserDirectory,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
    private readonly fetchFn: FetchFn = (url, init) => fetch(url, init),
  ) {}

  async namespaceCreated(e: NamespaceCreatedEmail): Promise<void> {
    await this.send(e.soeid, 'Success! Your DevSpaces Deployment Project is Ready!', (firstName, recipient) =>
      newNamespaceEmailBody({
        firstName,
        soeid: e.soeid,
        namespace: e.namespace,
        cluster: clusterLinks(this.clusterApiUrl),
        recipient,
      }),
    );
  }

  async namespaceAlreadyExists(e: NamespaceExistsEmail): Promise<void> {
    await this.send(
      e.soeid,
      'Important: Your DevSpaces Deployment Project Already Exists',
      (firstName, recipient) =>
        existingNamespaceEmailBody({
          firstName,
          soeid: e.soeid,
          newNamespace: e.newNamespace,
          existingNamespace: e.existingNamespace,
          cluster: clusterLinks(this.clusterApiUrl),
          recipient,
        }),
    );
  }

  async namespacesDeleted(e: NamespacesDeletedEmail): Promise<void> {
    if (e.namespaces.length === 0) return;
    await this.send(
      e.soeid,
      'Confirmation: DevSpaces Deployment Project(s) Deleted',
      (firstName, recipient) =>
        deletedNamespacesEmailBody({ firstName, soeid: e.soeid, namespaces: e.namespaces, recipient }),
    );
  }

  private async send(
    soeid: string,
    subject: string,
    body: (firstName: string | undefined, recipient: string) => string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { url, username, password, sender, cc, emailDomain } = this.config;
    if (!url) {
      this.logger.debug('email.skipped', { reason: 'NOTIFICATION_MAIL_URL not set', subject });
      return;
    }
    const recipient = `${soeid}@${emailDomain}`;
    try {
      const firstName = await this.directory.firstName(soeid);
      const response = await this.fetchFn(`${url.replace(/\/$/, '')}/send-mail/v2`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Basic ${Buffer.from(`${username}:${password}`).toString('base64')}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          sender,
          receiver: recipient,
          receiverName: soeid.toUpperCase(),
          cc: cc ?? '',
          bcc: '',
          subject,
          emailMessage: body(firstName, recipient),
          isHtml: true,
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`mail service answered ${response.status}`);
      this.logger.info('email.sent', { soeid, subject });
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.error('email.failed', { soeid, subject, error: errorMessage(error) });
    }
  }
}

export class NoopNotifier implements Notifier {
  async namespaceCreated(): Promise<void> {}
  async namespaceAlreadyExists(): Promise<void> {}
  async namespacesDeleted(): Promise<void> {}
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl d5ffe80f059a28eb9af910ed64c749016ea125a352c889f6f4a1fe55da7190da src/infra/email/GraphqlUserDirectory.ts
import { errorMessage } from '../../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../../utils/logger.js';
import type { UserDirectory } from '../ports.js';
import type { FetchFn } from './EmailNotifier.js';

const QUERY = 'query($id: String!) { users(filter: { soeId: { equals: $id } }) { firstName } }';

export class GraphqlUserDirectory implements UserDirectory {
  constructor(
    private readonly url: string,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
    private readonly fetchFn: FetchFn = (url, init) => fetch(url, init),
  ) {}

  async firstName(soeid: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
    try {
      const response = await this.fetchFn(this.url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ query: QUERY, variables: { id: soeid.toUpperCase() } }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5_000),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`user directory answered ${response.status}`);
      const json = (await response.json()) as { data?: { users?: { firstName?: string }[] } };
      return json.data?.users?.[0]?.firstName || undefined;
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.warn('userdirectory.failed', { soeid, error: errorMessage(error) });
      return undefined;
    }
  }
}

export class NullUserDirectory implements UserDirectory {
  async firstName(): Promise<string | undefined> {
    return undefined;
  }
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 1044ec59c17b9cdb7b503e157a127a47a86ceb9ff9ebedb7329da5d9c1661f9c src/infra/email/email-templates.ts
export const escapeHtml = (value: string): string =>
  value
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');

export interface ClusterLinks {
  apiUrl: string;
  consoleUrl: string;
  tokenUrl: string;
}

/** `https://api.<domain>:6443` → console and OAuth token URLs on `apps.<domain>`. */
export function clusterLinks(apiUrl: string): ClusterLinks {
  try {
    const url = new URL(apiUrl);
    const apps = url.hostname.replace(/^api\./, 'apps.');
    return {
      apiUrl,
      consoleUrl: `https://console-openshift-console.${apps}/`,
      tokenUrl: `https://oauth-openshift.${apps}/oauth/token/request`,
    };
  } catch {
    return { apiUrl, consoleUrl: '', tokenUrl: '' };
  }
}

const STYLE = `body{margin:0;padding:0;background:#f4f6f9;font-family:'Segoe UI',Arial,sans-serif;color:#323130}
.wrapper{max-width:700px;margin:20px auto;background:#fff;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden}
.header{background:#0f4c81;color:#fff;padding:24px;text-align:center}.header h1{margin:0;font-size:26px}
.content{padding:28px}code,pre{font-family:Consolas,'Courier New',monospace;background:#1e1e1e;color:#fff;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:4px}
.footer{background:#0f4c81;color:#fff;padding:16px;text-align:center;font-size:.9em}a{color:#0078d4}`;

function layout(title: string, heading: string, body: string, recipient: string): string {
  return `<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0"><title>${escapeHtml(title)}</title><style>${STYLE}</style></head><body><div class="wrapper"><div class="header"><h1>${escapeHtml(heading)}</h1></div><div class="content">${body}</div><div class="footer"><p>&copy; ${new Date().getFullYear()} DevSpaces Team.</p><p>This email was sent to ${escapeHtml(recipient)}.</p><p>Confidentiality Notice: this email and any attachments are confidential and intended solely for the addressee.</p></div></div></body></html>`;
}

const greeting = (firstName: string | undefined, soeid: string): string =>
  escapeHtml(firstName ?? `User ${soeid.toUpperCase()}`);

const links = (c: ClusterLinks): string =>
  `<ul><li>Cluster API endpoint: <a href="${escapeHtml(c.apiUrl)}">${escapeHtml(c.apiUrl)}</a></li>` +
  (c.consoleUrl
    ? `<li>OpenShift console: <a href="${escapeHtml(c.consoleUrl)}">${escapeHtml(c.consoleUrl)}</a></li>`
    : '') +
  (c.tokenUrl
    ? `<li>Generate a login token: <a href="${escapeHtml(c.tokenUrl)}">${escapeHtml(c.tokenUrl)}</a></li>`
    : '') +
  `</ul>`;

export function newNamespaceEmailBody(args: {
  firstName?: string;
  soeid: string;
  namespace: string;
  cluster: ClusterLinks;
  recipient: string;
}): string {
  return layout(
    'DevSpaces Deployment Project Ready',
    'DevSpaces Deployment Project Ready',
    `<p>Dear ${greeting(args.firstName, args.soeid)},</p>
<p>Your OpenShift deployment project <code>${escapeHtml(args.namespace)}</code> has been created and is ready for use.</p>
<h2>Quick start</h2>
<ol><li>Generate a login token and run the <code>oc login</code> command it gives you.</li>
<li>Select your project: <pre>oc project ${escapeHtml(args.namespace)}</pre></li>
<li>Build and deploy your application inside this project.</li></ol>
${links(args.cluster)}
<p>Newly provisioned resources may take several minutes to become fully available.</p>`,
    args.recipient,
  );
}

export function existingNamespaceEmailBody(args: {
  firstName?: string;
  soeid: string;
  newNamespace: string;
  existingNamespace: string;
  cluster: ClusterLinks;
  recipient: string;
}): string {
  return layout(
    'Important: Your DevSpaces Deployment Project Already Exists',
    'DevSpaces Notification',
    `<p>Dear ${greeting(args.firstName, args.soeid)},</p>
<p>You recently asked for a new deployment project (<code>${escapeHtml(args.newNamespace)}</code>), but you already have one,
so no new project was created.</p>
<h2>Connect to your existing deployment project</h2>
<pre>oc project ${escapeHtml(args.existingNamespace)}</pre>
${links(args.cluster)}`,
    args.recipient,
  );
}

export function deletedNamespacesEmailBody(args: {
  firstName?: string;
  soeid: string;
  namespaces: string[];
  recipient: string;
}): string {
  const items = args.namespaces.map((ns) => `<li><code>${escapeHtml(ns)}</code></li>`).join('');
  return layout(
    'Confirmation: DevSpaces Deployment Project(s) Deleted',
    'DevSpaces Notification',
    `<p>Dear ${greeting(args.firstName, args.soeid)},</p>
<p>The following DevSpaces deployment project(s) associated with your user id have been deleted:</p>
<ul>${items}</ul>
<p>Thank you for managing your resources.</p>`,
    args.recipient,
  );
}

import {
  CoreV1Api,

  type KubernetesObject,
} from '@kubernetes/client-node';
import type { DevWorkspaceResource, KubeObject } from '../../domain/types.js';
import { isK8sStatus, toAppError } from '../../utils/k8s-errors.js';
import type { KubeGateway, NamespacedKind } from '../ports.js';

const API_VERSION: Record<NamespacedKind, string> = {
  Secret: 'v1',
  ConfigMap: 'v1',
  ResourceQuota: 'v1',
  LimitRange: 'v1',
  Role: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
  RoleBinding: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
  NetworkPolicy: 'networking.k8s.io/v1',
  DevWorkspace: 'workspace.devfile.io/v1alpha2',
  DevWorkspaceTemplate: 'workspace.devfile.io/v1alpha2',
};

const asK8s = (o: unknown): KubernetesObject => o as KubernetesObject;
const asKube = <T>(o: unknown): T => o as T;

export function loadKubeConfig(kubeconfigPath?: string): KubeConfig {
  const kc = new KubeConfig();
  if (kubeconfigPath) kc.loadFromFile(kubeconfigPath);
  else kc.loadFromDefault();
  return kc;
}

/** Kubernetes/OpenShift access through the pod's service account (or a kubeconfig for local runs). */
export class KubeClient implements KubeGateway {
  private readonly core: CoreV1Api;
  private readonly objects: KubernetesObjectApi;

  constructor(kubeConfig: KubeConfig) {
    this.core = kubeConfig.makeApiClient(CoreV1Api);
    this.objects = KubernetesObjectApi.makeApiClient(kubeConfig);
  }

  private async call<T>(context: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      throw toAppError(context, error);
    }
  }

  private async orNull<T>(context: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | null> {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      if (isK8sStatus(error, 404)) return null;
      throw toAppError(context, error);
    }
  }

  private header(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string) {
    return { apiVersion: API_VERSION[kind], kind, metadata: { name, namespace } };
  }

  private body<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): KubernetesObject {
    return asK8s({
      ...body,
      apiVersion: API_VERSION[kind],
      kind,
      metadata: { ...body.metadata, namespace },
    });
  }

  async listNamespaces(labelSelector?: string): Promise<KubeObject[]> {
    const res = await this.call('List namespaces', () => this.core.listNamespace({ labelSelector }));
    return asKube<KubeObject[]>(res.items);
  }

  getNamespace(name: string): Promise<KubeObject | null> {
    return this.orNull(`Read namespace '${name}'`, async () =>
      asKube<KubeObject>(await this.core.readNamespace({ name })),
    );
  }

  async createNamespace(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject> {
    const created = await this.call(`Create namespace '${body.metadata?.name}'`, () =>
      this.core.createNamespace({ body: asKube(body) }),
    );
    return asKube<KubeObject>(created);
  }

  async deleteNamespace(name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = await this.orNull(`Delete namespace '${name}'`, () => this.core.deleteNamespace({ name }));
    return result !== null;
  }

  async createProject(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject> {
    const name = body.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const request = asK8s({ ...body, apiVersion: 'project.openshift.io/v1', kind: 'ProjectRequest' });
    const created = await this.call(`Create project '${name}'`, () => this.objects.create(request));
    const result = asKube<KubeObject>(created);
    return result.metadata?.name ? result : { ...result, metadata: { ...result.metadata, name } };
  }

  get<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<T | null> {
    return this.orNull(`Read ${kind} '${name}'`, async () =>
      asKube<T>(await this.objects.read(this.header(kind, namespace, name))),
    );
  }

  async list<T extends KubeObject>(
    kind: NamespacedKind,
    namespace: string,
    labelSelector?: string,
  ): Promise<T[]> {
    const res = await this.call(`List ${kind} in '${namespace}'`, () =>
      this.objects.list(
        API_VERSION[kind],
        kind,
        namespace,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        labelSelector,
      ),
    );
    return asKube<T[]>(res.items);
  }

  async create<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T> {
    const created = await this.call(`Create ${kind} '${body.metadata?.name}'`, () =>
      this.objects.create(this.body(kind, namespace, body)),
    );
    return asKube<T>(created);
  }

  async apply<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T> {
    const name = body.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const context = `Apply ${kind} '${name}'`;
    const replaceExisting = async (): Promise<T | null> => {
      const existing = await this.get<T>(kind, namespace, name);
      if (!existing) return null;
      const spec = this.body(kind, namespace, {
        ...body,
        metadata: { ...body.metadata, resourceVersion: existing.metadata?.resourceVersion },
      });
      return asKube<T>(await this.call(context, () => this.objects.replace(spec)));
    };

    const replaced = await replaceExisting();
    if (replaced) return replaced;
    try {
      return asKube<T>(await this.objects.create(this.body(kind, namespace, body)));
    } catch (error) {
      if (isK8sStatus(error, 409)) {
        // Lost a creation race with another request; replace what the winner created.
        const retried = await replaceExisting();
        if (retried) return retried;
      }
      throw toAppError(context, error);
    }
  }

  async delete(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = await this.orNull(`Delete ${kind} '${name}'`, () =>
      this.objects.delete(asK8s(this.header(kind, namespace, name))),
    );
    return result !== null;
  }

  async setWorkspaceStarted(
    namespace: string,
    name: string,
    started: boolean,
  ): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource> {
    const patch = asK8s({ ...this.header('DevWorkspace', namespace, name), spec: { started } });
    const patched = await this.call(`${started ? 'Start' : 'Stop'} DevWorkspace '${name}'`, () =>
      this.objects.patch(patch, undefined, undefined, undefined, undefined, PatchStrategy.MergePatch),
    );
    return asKube<DevWorkspaceResource>(patched);
  }

  async ping(): Promise<void> {
    await this.call('Kubernetes API ping', () => this.core.getAPIResources());
  }
}

import type { CatalogEntry, DevWorkspaceResource, KubeObject, MetricsRecord } from '../domain/types.js';

export type NamespacedKind =
  | 'Secret'
  | 'ConfigMap'
  | 'ResourceQuota'
  | 'LimitRange'
  | 'Role'
  | 'RoleBinding'
  | 'NetworkPolicy'
  | 'DevWorkspace'
  | 'DevWorkspaceTemplate';

/** Everything the services need from Kubernetes/OpenShift. Implemented by KubeClient and by the in-memory fake. */
export interface KubeGateway {
  listNamespaces(labelSelector?: string): Promise<KubeObject[]>;
  getNamespace(name: string): Promise<KubeObject | null>;
  createNamespace(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject>;
  deleteNamespace(name: string): Promise<boolean>;
  /** OpenShift ProjectRequest; the resulting namespace is returned. */
  createProject(body: KubeObject): Promise<KubeObject>;

  get<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<T | null>;
  list<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, labelSelector?: string): Promise<T[]>;
  create<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T>;
  /** Create the object, or replace it when it already exists. */
  apply<T extends KubeObject>(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, body: T): Promise<T>;
  /** Returns false when the object was already gone. */
  delete(kind: NamespacedKind, namespace: string, name: string): Promise<boolean>;

  setWorkspaceStarted(namespace: string, name: string, started: boolean): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource>;
  ping(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface MetricsStore {
  insert(record: MetricsRecord): Promise<void>;
  ping(): Promise<void>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface DevfileRegistry {
  getCatalog(): Promise<CatalogEntry[]>;
  /** Raw devfile YAML for a stack. Throws WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError for unknown stack/version. */
  getDevfile(stack: string, version?: string): Promise<{ yaml: string; version: string }>;
}

export interface NamespaceCreatedEmail {
  soeid: string;
  namespace: string;
}
export interface NamespaceExistsEmail {
  soeid: string;
  newNamespace: string;
  existingNamespace: string;
}
export interface NamespacesDeletedEmail {
  soeid: string;
  namespaces: string[];
}

/** Best-effort user notifications; implementations must never throw. */
export interface Notifier {
  namespaceCreated(event: NamespaceCreatedEmail): Promise<void>;
  namespaceAlreadyExists(event: NamespaceExistsEmail): Promise<void>;
  namespacesDeleted(event: NamespacesDeletedEmail): Promise<void>;
}

/** Looks up a person's first name for greetings; resolves undefined when unknown or unavailable. */
export interface UserDirectory {
  firstName(soeid: string): Promise<string | undefined>;
}

export interface Clock {
  now(): Date;
  sleep(ms: number): Promise<void>;
}

export const systemClock: Clock = {
  now: () => new Date(),
  sleep: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
};

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 3ddf77f2558b484ec3f18c145c1a318a08fb8b773273b5ac9df92e0e7ee0c549 src/services/DeploymentNamespaceService.ts
import {
  ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME,
  BUILDER_ROLE_CONFIG,
  DEPLOYER_ROLE_CONFIG,
  DEPLOYMENT_LIMIT_RANGE_DEFAULT_CONFIG,
  DEPLOYMENT_RESOURCE_QUOTA_COMPUTE_CONFIG,
  DEPLOYMENT_RESOURCE_QUOTA_STORAGE_CONFIG,
  LABEL_COMPONENT,
  LABEL_CSIID,
  LABEL_DEPLOYMENT_NAMESPACE,
} from '../constants/kubernetes-configs.js';
import type { KubeObject } from '../domain/types.js';
import type { KubeGateway, Notifier } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { errorMessage } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';
import { csiidFromDeploymentNamespace, deploymentNamespace } from '../utils/names.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';

export interface DeploymentEnsureResult {
  exists: boolean;
  name: string;
}

export interface DeploymentDeleteResult {
  exists: boolean;
  deleted: string[];
  failed: string[];
  message: string;
}

export class DeploymentNamespaceService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
    private readonly notifier: Notifier,
    private readonly requester: string,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
  ) {}

  async list(soeid: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const pattern = new RegExp(`^${soeid}-devspaces-deploy-\\d+$`);
    const all = await this.kube.listNamespaces();
    return all.map((ns) => ns.metadata?.name ?? '').filter((name) => pattern.test(name));
  }

  /** Returns the existing deployment project for this CSI id, or creates and provisions a new one. */
  async ensure(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<DeploymentEnsureResult> {
    const desired = deploymentNamespace(soeid, csiid);
    if (await this.kube.getNamespace(desired)) {
      await this.notifier.namespaceAlreadyExists({
        soeid,
        newNamespace: desired,
        existingNamespace: desired,
      });
      return { exists: true, name: desired };
    }

    const actual = await this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'deployment_namespace', resourcename: desired },
      () => this.createAndProvision(soeid, csiid, desired),
    );
    await this.notifier.namespaceCreated({ soeid, namespace: actual });
    return { exists: false, name: actual };
  }

  private async createAndProvision(soeid: string, csiid: string, desired: string): Promise<string> {
    const project = await this.kube.createProject({
      metadata: {
        name: desired,
        labels: {
          [LABEL_COMPONENT]: LABEL_DEPLOYMENT_NAMESPACE,
          'app.kubernetes.io/managed-by': 'devspaces-support-team',
          'devspaces.citi.com/purpose': 'deployment-resource-management',
          [LABEL_CSIID]: csiid,
        },
        annotations: {
          [ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME]: soeid,
          'devspaces.citi.com/creator': 'workspace-manager-automation',
          'openshift.io/requester': this.requester,
          'openshift.io/display-name': `DevSpaces Deployment Project for User ${soeid} [CSIID: ${csiid}]`,
          'openshift.io/description': `Temporary deployment testing project for user ${soeid} [CSIID: ${csiid}], removable by DevSpaces support`,
        },
      },
    });
    const name = project.metadata?.name ?? desired;

    await this.kube.apply('ResourceQuota', name, DEPLOYMENT_RESOURCE_QUOTA_COMPUTE_CONFIG);
    await this.kube.apply('LimitRange', name, DEPLOYMENT_LIMIT_RANGE_DEFAULT_CONFIG);
    await this.kube.apply('ResourceQuota', name, DEPLOYMENT_RESOURCE_QUOTA_STORAGE_CONFIG);
    await this.grantAccess(soeid, name);
    this.logger.info('deployment_namespace.created', { soeid, namespace: name });
    return name;
  }

  private async grantAccess(soeid: string, namespace: string): Promise<void> {
    for (const role of [BUILDER_ROLE_CONFIG, DEPLOYER_ROLE_CONFIG]) {
      const roleName = role.metadata?.name ?? '';
      await this.kube.apply('Role', namespace, role);
      await this.kube.apply('RoleBinding', namespace, this.binding(soeid, namespace, roleName));
    }
  }

  private binding(soeid: string, namespace: string, roleName: string): KubeObject {
    return {
      apiVersion: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
      kind: 'RoleBinding',
      metadata: { name: `${roleName}-${soeid}-binding` },
      subjects: [
        { kind: 'User', name: soeid, apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io' },
        { kind: 'ServiceAccount', name: 'default', namespace },
      ],
      roleRef: { apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io', kind: 'Role', name: roleName },
    };
  }

  async delete(soeid: string): Promise<DeploymentDeleteResult> {
    const names = await this.list(soeid);
    if (names.length === 0) {
      return { exists: false, deleted: [], failed: [], message: 'No deployment namespaces found for user.' };
    }

    const deleted: string[] = [];
    const failed: string[] = [];
    for (const name of names) {
      try {
        await this.recorder.track(
          {
            soeid,
            csiid: csiidFromDeploymentNamespace(name) ?? '',
            eventtype: 'delete',
            resourcetype: 'deployment_namespace',
            resourcename: name,
          },
          () => this.kube.deleteNamespace(name),
        );
        deleted.push(name);
      } catch (error) {
        this.logger.error('deployment_namespace.delete_failed', {
          soeid,
          namespace: name,
          error: errorMessage(error),
        });
        failed.push(name);
      }
    }

    if (deleted.length > 0) await this.notifier.namespacesDeleted({ soeid, namespaces: deleted });

    const parts: string[] = [];
    if (deleted.length > 0)
      parts.push(`Deleted ${deleted.length} deployment namespace(s): ${deleted.join(', ')}`);
    if (failed.length > 0) parts.push(`Failed to delete ${failed.length}: ${failed.join(', ')}`);
    return { exists: deleted.length > 0, deleted, failed, message: parts.join('. ') };
  }
}

import type { KubeObject } from '../domain/types.js';
import type { KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { BadRequestError, NotFoundError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';
import type { NamespaceService } from './NamespaceService.js';

export const GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP = 'workspace-userdata-gitconfig-configmap';
const MAX_CONTENT_BYTES = 64 * 1024;

export interface GitConfigFile {
  name: string;
  namespace: string;
  content: string;
}

export class GitConfigService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly namespaces: NamespaceService,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
  ) {}

  async view(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<GitConfigFile> {
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const configMap = await this.kube.get<KubeObject>('ConfigMap', namespace, GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP);
    if (!configMap) throw new NotFoundError('Git config not found', 'GITCONFIG_NOT_FOUND');
    return {
      name: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP,
      namespace,
      content: (configMap.data as Record<string, string> | undefined)?.['.gitconfig'] ?? '',
    };
  }

  /** Create or replace the user's `~/.gitconfig`. */
  async upsert(soeid: string, csiid: string, content: string): Promise<GitConfigFile> {
    if (content.trim().length === 0) throw new BadRequestError('Git config content must not be empty');
    if (Buffer.byteLength(content) > MAX_CONTENT_BYTES) {
      throw new BadRequestError('Git config content is too large (max 64 KiB)');
    }
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const existed = (await this.kube.get('ConfigMap', namespace, GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP)) !== null;

    await this.recorder.track(
      {
        soeid,
        csiid,
        eventtype: existed ? 'update' : 'create',
        resourcetype: 'gitconfig',
        resourcename: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP,
      },
      () =>
        this.kube.apply('ConfigMap', namespace, {
          apiVersion: 'v1',
          kind: 'ConfigMap',
          metadata: {
            name: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP,
            labels: {
              'controller.devfile.io/mount-to-devworkspace': 'true',
              'controller.devfile.io/watch-configmap': 'true',
            },
            annotations: {
              'controller.devfile.io/mount-as': 'subpath',
              'controller.devfile.io/mount-path': '/home/user',
            },
          },
          data: { '.gitconfig': content },
        }),
    );
    return { name: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP, namespace, content };
  }

  /** Returns false when there was nothing to delete. */
  async delete(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    return this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'delete', resourcetype: 'gitconfig', resourcename: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP },
      () => this.kube.delete('ConfigMap', namespace, GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP),
    );
  }
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl abbd2201b0851ea4db5801c6909b953da56d15dfd2d716c4a0c52ed010225b10 src/services/MetricsRecorder.ts
import { buildLogRecord, type MetricsInput } from '../factories/log-factory.js';
import type { MetricsRecord } from '../domain/types.js';
import { systemClock, type Clock, type MetricsStore } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { errorMessage } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';

export type MetricBase = MetricsInput;

/**
 * Writes one metrics row per user action. Recording is best-effort: a slow or broken database is logged
 * and never fails the user's action.
 */
export class MetricsRecorder {
  constructor(
    private readonly store: MetricsStore,
    private readonly clock: Clock = systemClock,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
    private readonly timeoutMs = 2000,
  ) {}

  async record(input: MetricsInput): Promise<void> {
    const record = buildLogRecord(input, this.clock.now());
    let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
    try {
      await Promise.race([
        this.store.insert(record),
        new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
          timer = setTimeout(
            () => reject(new Error(`metrics insert timed out after ${this.timeoutMs}ms`)),
            this.timeoutMs,
          );
        }),
      ]);
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.error('metrics.insert_failed', {
        soeid: record.soeid,
        eventtype: record.eventtype,
        resourcetype: record.resourcetype,
        error: errorMessage(error),
      });
    } finally {
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }

  /** Run `fn`, record success (with optional extra fields from the result) or failure, and pass the outcome through. */
  async track<T>(
    base: MetricBase,
    fn: () => Promise<T>,
    onSuccess?: (result: T) => Partial<MetricsRecord>,
  ): Promise<T> {
    let result: T;
    try {
      result = await fn();
    } catch (error) {
      await this.record({ ...base, status: { failed: true, error: errorMessage(error), message: '' } });
      throw error;
    }
    await this.record({ ...base, ...(onSuccess?.(result) ?? {}) });
    return result;
  }
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 7695b3705491084f684544c57e7627752ccdeae7dfae47e2e70fd1445ef5be63 src/services/NamespaceService.ts
import {
  ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME,
  LABEL_COMPONENT,
  LABEL_CSIID,
  LABEL_WORKSPACES_NAMESPACE,
  NETWORK_POLICIES_DEFAULT_CONFIG,
  ORION_LIMIT_RANGE_CONFIG,
  ORION_RESOURCE_QUOTA_CONFIG,
  USER_ROLE_CONFIG,
  WORKSPACES_NAMESPACE_SELECTOR,
} from '../constants/kubernetes-configs.js';
import type { KubeObject, Project } from '../domain/types.js';
import type { KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';
import { csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace, workspaceNamespace } from '../utils/names.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';

export interface NamespaceServiceOptions {
  workspaceProxyUrl?: string;
  emailDomain: string;
}

export interface EnsuredNamespace {
  name: string;
  created: boolean;
}

export class NamespaceService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
    private readonly options: NamespaceServiceOptions,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
  ) {}

  /** All workspace namespaces owned by the user (by label and Che username annotation). */
  async listForUser(soeid: string): Promise<KubeObject[]> {
    const all = await this.kube.listNamespaces(WORKSPACES_NAMESPACE_SELECTOR);
    return all.filter((ns) => ns.metadata?.annotations?.[ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME] === soeid);
  }

  async listProjects(soeid: string): Promise<Project[]> {
    const namespaces = await this.listForUser(soeid);
    return namespaces.map((ns) => {
      const name = ns.metadata?.name ?? '';
      return {
        name,
        soeid,
        csiid: ns.metadata?.labels?.[LABEL_CSIID] ?? csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace(name) ?? '',
        createdAt: ns.metadata?.creationTimestamp
          ? new Date(ns.metadata.creationTimestamp).toISOString()
          : '',
      };
    });
  }

  /** The namespace must already exist (PATs, git config); never creates one. */
  async require(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<string> {
    const name = workspaceNamespace(soeid, csiid);
    const existing = await this.kube.getNamespace(name);
    if (!existing) throw new NotFoundError(`Project '${name}' not found`, 'PROJECT_NOT_FOUND');
    this.assertOwner(existing, soeid);
    return name;
  }

  /** Find the user's namespace for this CSI id, creating and provisioning it when it does not exist. */
  async ensure(soeid: string, csiid: string): Promise<EnsuredNamespace> {
    const name = workspaceNamespace(soeid, csiid);
    const existing = await this.kube.getNamespace(name);
    if (existing) {
      this.assertOwner(existing, soeid);
      return { name, created: false };
    }

    this.logger.info('namespace.creating', { soeid, namespace: name });
    const created = await this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'project', resourcename: name },
      () => this.createAndProvision(soeid, csiid, name),
    );
    return { name, created };
  }

  private async createAndProvision(soeid: string, csiid: string, name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await this.kube.createNamespace({
        apiVersion: 'v1',
        kind: 'Namespace',
        metadata: {
          name,
          labels: {
            [LABEL_COMPONENT]: LABEL_WORKSPACES_NAMESPACE,
            'app.kubernetes.io/part-of': 'che.eclipse.org',
            'app.kubernetes.io/managed-by': 'workspace-mgr',
            [LABEL_CSIID]: csiid,
          },
          annotations: { [ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME]: soeid },
        },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof ConflictError) {
        // Another request created it between our read and write; use theirs.
        const raced = await this.kube.getNamespace(name);
        if (raced) {
          this.assertOwner(raced, soeid);
          return false;
        }
      }
      throw error;
    }

    try {
      await this.provision(soeid, name);
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.error('namespace.provision_failed', { soeid, namespace: name });
      await this.kube.deleteNamespace(name).catch(() => false);
      throw error;
    }
    return true;
  }

  private async provision(soeid: string, namespace: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.kube.apply('Role', namespace, USER_ROLE_CONFIG);
    await this.kube.apply('RoleBinding', namespace, {
      apiVersion: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1',
      kind: 'RoleBinding',
      metadata: {
        name: `devspaces-project-access-${soeid}`,
        labels: { [LABEL_COMPONENT]: 'devspaces-workspace-management' },
      },
      subjects: [{ kind: 'User', name: soeid, apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io' }],
      roleRef: {
        kind: 'Role',
        name: USER_ROLE_CONFIG.metadata?.name,
        apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io',
      },
    });
    await this.kube.apply('ResourceQuota', namespace, ORION_RESOURCE_QUOTA_CONFIG);
    await this.kube.apply('LimitRange', namespace, ORION_LIMIT_RANGE_CONFIG);
    for (const policy of NETWORK_POLICIES_DEFAULT_CONFIG) {
      await this.kube.apply('NetworkPolicy', namespace, policy);
    }
    await this.applyProxyConfig(soeid, namespace);
  }

  private async applyProxyConfig(soeid: string, namespace: string): Promise<void> {
    const proxy = this.options.workspaceProxyUrl;
    if (!proxy) return;
    const noProxy = 'localhost,127.0.0.1,*.svc,*.svc.cluster.local';

    await this.kube.apply('ConfigMap', namespace, {
      apiVersion: 'v1',
      kind: 'ConfigMap',
      metadata: {
        name: 'proxy-env-vars-ws-mgr',
        labels: {
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-to-devworkspace': 'true',
          'controller.devfile.io/watch-configmap': 'true',
        },
        annotations: { 'controller.devfile.io/mount-as': 'env' },
      },
      data: {
        HTTP_PROXY: proxy,
        HTTPS_PROXY: proxy,
        http_proxy: proxy,
        https_proxy: proxy,
        NO_PROXY: noProxy,
        no_proxy: noProxy,
      },
    });

    await this.kube.apply('ConfigMap', namespace, {
      apiVersion: 'v1',
      kind: 'ConfigMap',
      metadata: {
        name: 'workspace-userdata-gitconfig-configmap-ws-mgr',
        labels: {
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-to-devworkspace': 'true',
          'controller.devfile.io/watch-configmap': 'true',
        },
        annotations: {
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-as': 'subpath',
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-path': '/etc/',
        },
      },
      data: {
        gitconfig: `[user]\n  name = ${soeid}\n  email = ${soeid}@${this.options.emailDomain}\n[http]\n  sslVerify = false\n[proxy]\n  http = ${proxy}\n  https = ${proxy}\n`,
      },
    });
  }

  private assertOwner(namespace: KubeObject, soeid: string): void {
    const owner = namespace.metadata?.annotations?.[ANNOTATION_CHE_USERNAME];
    if (owner !== soeid) {
      throw new ConflictError(
        `Namespace '${namespace.metadata?.name}' exists but belongs to another user`,
        'NAMESPACE_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH',
      );
    }
  }
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl d31e2ae9fc952894f65e4cbb996044bc6e0d67d1cdd561df2383d0d8b8c08e37 src/services/PatService.ts
import type { AppConfig } from '../config/env.js';
import type { KubeObject } from '../domain/types.js';
import type { KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import { BadRequestError, ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '../utils/errors.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';
import type { NamespaceService } from './NamespaceService.js';

export const PAT_SECRET_PREFIX = 'git-credentials-secret-';
const CREDENTIALS_KEY = 'credentials';
const GIT_CREDENTIAL_SELECTOR = 'controller.devfile.io/git-credential=true';
const GITHUB_PAT_RE = /^ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}$/;

export interface PatSummary {
  namespace: string;
  name: string;
  createdOn: string;
  lastTransitionTime: string;
}

export interface PatDetails extends PatSummary {
  token: string;
  scmUrl: string;
}

/** `ghp_ab…xyz`; the full value is never returned or logged. */
export function maskToken(token: string): string {
  return token.length > 5 ? `${token.slice(0, 2)}…${token.slice(-3)}` : '***';
}

export function assertValidGithubToken(token: string): void {
  if (!GITHUB_PAT_RE.test(token)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(
      "Invalid GitHub PAT format: token must start with 'ghp_' and be 40 characters long",
      'INVALID_PAT',
    );
  }
}

const iso = (value: Date | string | undefined): string => (value ? new Date(value).toISOString() : '');

export class PatService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly namespaces: NamespaceService,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
    private readonly github: AppConfig['github'],
  ) {}

  async list(soeid: string): Promise<PatSummary[]> {
    const namespaces = await this.namespaces.listForUser(soeid);
    const result: PatSummary[] = [];
    for (const ns of namespaces) {
      const namespace = ns.metadata?.name ?? '';
      const secrets = await this.kube.list<KubeObject>('Secret', namespace, GIT_CREDENTIAL_SELECTOR);
      for (const secret of secrets) {
        const name = secret.metadata?.name ?? '';
        if (name.startsWith(PAT_SECRET_PREFIX)) result.push(this.summary(namespace, secret));
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  async view(soeid: string, csiid: string, tokenName: string): Promise<PatDetails> {
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const secret = await this.kube.get<KubeObject>('Secret', namespace, this.secretName(tokenName));
    if (!secret) throw new NotFoundError(`PAT '${tokenName}' not found`, 'PAT_NOT_FOUND');
    const encoded = (secret.data as Record<string, string> | undefined)?.[CREDENTIALS_KEY] ?? '';
    const decoded = Buffer.from(encoded, 'base64').toString('utf8');
    const token = /:([^:@]+)@[^@]+$/.exec(decoded)?.[1] ?? '';
    return {
      ...this.summary(namespace, secret),
      token: token ? maskToken(token) : '[unrecognized format]',
      scmUrl: secret.metadata?.annotations?.['che.eclipse.org/scm-url'] ?? this.github.url,
    };
  }

  async create(soeid: string, csiid: string, tokenName: string, token: string): Promise<PatSummary> {
    assertValidGithubToken(token);
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const name = this.secretName(tokenName);
    if (await this.kube.get('Secret', namespace, name)) {
      throw new ConflictError(`PAT '${tokenName}' already exists`, 'PAT_EXISTS');
    }
    const created = await this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'pat', resourcename: name },
      () => this.kube.create<KubeObject>('Secret', namespace, this.secretBody(soeid, namespace, name, token)),
    );
    return this.summary(namespace, created);
  }

  async update(soeid: string, csiid: string, tokenName: string, token: string): Promise<PatSummary> {
    assertValidGithubToken(token);
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const name = this.secretName(tokenName);
    if (!(await this.kube.get('Secret', namespace, name))) {
      throw new NotFoundError(`PAT '${tokenName}' not found`, 'PAT_NOT_FOUND');
    }
    const updated = await this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'update', resourcetype: 'pat', resourcename: name },
      () => this.kube.apply<KubeObject>('Secret', namespace, this.secretBody(soeid, namespace, name, token)),
    );
    return this.summary(namespace, updated);
  }

  /** Deleting a token that is already gone is a success (returns false). */
  async delete(soeid: string, csiid: string, tokenName: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const namespace = await this.namespaces.require(soeid, csiid);
    const name = this.secretName(tokenName);
    return this.recorder.track(
      { soeid, csiid, eventtype: 'delete', resourcetype: 'pat', resourcename: name },
      () => this.kube.delete('Secret', namespace, name),
    );
  }

  private secretName(tokenName: string): string {
    return `${PAT_SECRET_PREFIX}${tokenName}`;
  }

  private summary(namespace: string, secret: KubeObject): PatSummary {
    const name = secret.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const createdOn = iso(secret.metadata?.creationTimestamp);
    return {
      namespace,
      name: name.startsWith(PAT_SECRET_PREFIX) ? name.slice(PAT_SECRET_PREFIX.length) : name,
      createdOn,
      lastTransitionTime: secret.metadata?.annotations?.lastTransitionTime ?? createdOn,
    };
  }

  private secretBody(soeid: string, namespace: string, name: string, token: string): KubeObject {
    const scm = new URL(this.github.url);
    const user = encodeURIComponent(`${soeid}${this.github.userSuffix}`);
    const credentials = `${scm.protocol}//${user}:${token}@${scm.host}`;
    return {
      apiVersion: 'v1',
      kind: 'Secret',
      metadata: {
        name,
        namespace,
        labels: {
          'app.kubernetes.io/component': 'workspace-secret',
          'app.kubernetes.io/part-of': 'che.eclipse.org',
          'controller.devfile.io/git-credential': 'true',
          'controller.devfile.io/watch-secret': 'true',
        },
        annotations: {
          'che.eclipse.org/automount-workspace-secret': 'true',
          'che.eclipse.org/git-credential': 'true',
          'che.eclipse.org/mount-as': 'file',
          'che.eclipse.org/mount-path': '/.git-credentials',
          'che.eclipse.org/scm-url': this.github.url,
          'controller.devfile.io/mount-path': '/.git-credentials',
          lastTransitionTime: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
      },
      type: 'Opaque',
      data: { [CREDENTIALS_KEY]: Buffer.from(credentials).toString('base64') },
    };
  }
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl dd60974795fd2544666e4679b2e4199a324791f4871cdf0e06db2bff30d5c008 src/services/WorkspaceService.ts
import type { AppConfig } from '../config/env.js';
import type { CreateWorkspaceInput, DevWorkspaceResource, EditorKey, Workspace } from '../domain/types.js';
import { applyEditorProfile, parseDevfile } from '../factories/devfile-updater.js';
import { buildDevWorkspaceObject, WORKSPACE_LABELS } from '../factories/workspace-factory.js';
import { systemClock, type Clock, type DevfileRegistry, type KubeGateway } from '../infra/ports.js';
import {
  NotFoundError,
  WorkspaceCountExceededError,
  WorkspaceRunCountExceededError,
} from '../utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger, type Logger } from '../utils/logger.js';
import { cleanWorkspaceName, csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace } from '../utils/names.js';
import { summarizeComponents } from '../utils/resources.js';
import { durationSince } from '../utils/timestamp.js';
import type { MetricsRecorder } from './MetricsRecorder.js';
import type { NamespaceService } from './NamespaceService.js';

export interface WorkspaceServiceOptions {
  limits: AppConfig['limits'];
  editors: AppConfig['devreg']['editors'];
  devSpacesServiceFqdn: string;
}

const ACTIVE_PHASES = new Set(['Running', 'Starting', 'Stopping']);

/** Running, starting, stopping — or not yet reporting a phase — all still hold cluster resources. */
export const isActive = (dw: DevWorkspaceResource): boolean =>
  !dw.status?.phase || ACTIVE_PHASES.has(dw.status.phase);

export function toWorkspace(dw: DevWorkspaceResource): Workspace {
  const meta = dw.metadata ?? {};
  const annotations = meta.annotations ?? {};
  const namespace = meta.namespace ?? '';
  const resources = summarizeComponents(dw.spec?.template?.components);
  return {
    id: meta.name ?? '',
    name: meta.name ?? '',
    displayName: annotations.workspaceDisplayName ?? meta.name ?? '',
    description: annotations.workspaceDescription ?? '',
    namespace,
    csiid: meta.labels?.[WORKSPACE_LABELS.csiid] ?? csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace(namespace) ?? '',
    phase: dw.status?.phase ?? 'Starting',
    mainUrl: dw.status?.mainUrl ?? '',
    editor: annotations.editorDisplayName ?? '',
    version: annotations.workspaceVersion ?? '',
    stack: meta.labels?.[WORKSPACE_LABELS.stack] ?? '',
    created: meta.creationTimestamp ? new Date(meta.creationTimestamp).toISOString() : '',
    limitcpu: resources.limitcpu,
    limitmemory: resources.limitmemory,
    storage: resources.storage,
  };
}

/** When the workspace last became ready; falls back to creation time. */
function runningSince(dw: DevWorkspaceResource): Date | string | undefined {
  const ready = dw.status?.conditions?.find(
    (c) => (c.type === 'Started' || c.type === 'Ready') && c.status === 'True',
  );
  return ready?.lastTransitionTime ?? dw.metadata?.creationTimestamp;
}

export class WorkspaceService {
  constructor(
    private readonly kube: KubeGateway,
    private readonly namespaces: NamespaceService,
    private readonly registry: DevfileRegistry,
    private readonly recorder: MetricsRecorder,
    private readonly options: WorkspaceServiceOptions,
    private readonly clock: Clock = systemClock,
    private readonly logger: Logger = silentLogger,
  ) {}

  private async listResources(soeid: string): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource[]> {
    const namespaces = await this.namespaces.listForUser(soeid);
    const lists = await Promise.all(
      namespaces.map((ns) => this.kube.list<DevWorkspaceResource>('DevWorkspace', ns.metadata?.name ?? '')),
    );
    return lists.flat();
  }

  async list(soeid: string): Promise<Workspace[]> {
    return (await this.listResources(soeid)).map(toWorkspace);
  }

  private async find(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<DevWorkspaceResource> {
    const found = (await this.listResources(soeid)).find((dw) => dw.metadata?.name === id);
    if (!found) throw new NotFoundError(`Workspace '${id}' not found`, 'WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND');
    return found;
  }

  async get(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<Workspace> {
    return toWorkspace(await this.find(soeid, id));
  }

  private assertCanRunAnother(all: DevWorkspaceResource[]): void {
    const active = all.filter(isActive).length;
    if (active >= this.options.limits.maxRunning) {
      throw new WorkspaceRunCountExceededError(
        `You already have ${active} running workspace(s); the limit is ${this.options.limits.maxRunning}. Stop one first.`,
      );
    }
  }

  private assertCanCreateAnother(all: DevWorkspaceResource[]): void {
    if (all.length >= this.options.limits.maxTotal) {
      throw new WorkspaceCountExceededError(
        `You already have ${all.length} workspace(s); the limit is ${this.options.limits.maxTotal}. Delete one first.`,
      );
    }
    this.assertCanRunAnother(all);
  }

  async create(input: CreateWorkspaceInput): Promise<Workspace> {
    const { soeid, csiid } = input;
    this.assertCanCreateAnother(await this.listResources(soeid));

    const { name: namespace } = await this.namespaces.ensure(soeid, csiid);
    const { yaml, version } = await this.registry.getDevfile(input.devworkspaceValue, input.stackVersion);
    const devfile = applyEditorProfile(parseDevfile(yaml), input.editor);
    const body = buildDevWorkspaceObject({
      name: cleanWorkspaceName(input.devworkspaceValue),
      namespace,
      csiid,
      stack: input.devworkspaceValue,
      stackVersion: version,
      devfile,
      editor: input.editor,
      editorId: this.editorId(input.editor),
      devSpacesServiceFqdn: this.options.devSpacesServiceFqdn,
      repositoryUrl: input.repositoryUrl,
      repositoryBranch: input.repositoryBranch,
    });

    const created = await this.recorder.track(
      {
        soeid,
        csiid,
        eventtype: 'create',
        resourcetype: 'workspace',
        resourcename: body.metadata?.name ?? '',
        workspaceversion: version,
        editorname: input.editor,
        ...summarizeComponents(devfile.components),
      },
      () => this.kube.create<DevWorkspaceResource>('DevWorkspace', namespace, body),
    );
    this.logger.info('workspace.created', { soeid, namespace, workspace: created.metadata?.name });
    return toWorkspace(created);
  }

  async start(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<Workspace> {
    const dw = await this.find(soeid, id);
    if (dw.status?.phase === 'Running' || dw.status?.phase === 'Starting') return toWorkspace(dw);
    this.assertCanRunAnother(await this.listResources(soeid));
    return this.setStarted(soeid, dw, true);
  }

  async stop(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<Workspace> {
    const dw = await this.find(soeid, id);
    return this.setStarted(soeid, dw, false);
  }

  private async setStarted(soeid: string, dw: DevWorkspaceResource, started: boolean): Promise<Workspace> {
    const namespace = dw.metadata?.namespace ?? '';
    const name = dw.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const resources = summarizeComponents(dw.spec?.template?.components);
    const patched = await this.recorder.track(
      {
        soeid,
        csiid: toWorkspace(dw).csiid,
        eventtype: started ? 'start' : 'stop',
        resourcetype: 'workspace',
        resourcename: name,
        workspaceversion: dw.metadata?.annotations?.workspaceVersion ?? '',
        editorname: dw.metadata?.annotations?.editorDisplayName ?? '',
        duration: started ? '0 seconds' : durationSince(runningSince(dw), this.clock.now()),
        ...resources,
      },
      () => this.kube.setWorkspaceStarted(namespace, name, started),
    );
    return toWorkspace({ ...dw, ...patched, metadata: { ...dw.metadata, ...patched.metadata } });
  }

  async delete(soeid: string, id: string): Promise<void> {
    const dw = await this.find(soeid, id);
    const namespace = dw.metadata?.namespace ?? '';
    const name = dw.metadata?.name ?? '';
    const resources = summarizeComponents(dw.spec?.template?.components);

    await this.recorder.track(
      {
        soeid,
        csiid: toWorkspace(dw).csiid,
        eventtype: 'delete',
        resourcetype: 'workspace',
        resourcename: name,
        workspaceversion: dw.metadata?.annotations?.workspaceVersion ?? '',
        editorname: dw.metadata?.annotations?.editorDisplayName ?? '',
        duration: durationSince(dw.metadata?.creationTimestamp, this.clock.now()),
        ...resources,
      },
      async () => {
        await this.kube.delete('DevWorkspace', namespace, name);
        // Workspaces created by the legacy flow reference a separate DevWorkspaceTemplate.
        for (const contribution of dw.spec?.contributions ?? []) {
          if (contribution.kubernetes?.name) {
            await this.kube.delete('DevWorkspaceTemplate', namespace, contribution.kubernetes.name);
          }
        }
      },
    );
    this.logger.info('workspace.deleted', { soeid, namespace, workspace: name });
  }

  private editorId(editor: EditorKey): string {
    return this.options.editors[editor];
  }
}

export class AppError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public readonly status: number,
    public readonly code: string,
    options?: { cause?: unknown },
  ) {
    super(message, options);
    this.name = new.target.name;
  }
}

export class BadRequestError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, code = 'BAD_REQUEST') {
    super(message, 400, code);
  }
}

export class UnauthorizedError extends AppError {
  constructor(message = 'Authentication required') {
    super(message, 401, 'UNAUTHORIZED');
  }
}

export class ForbiddenError extends AppError {
  constructor(message = 'Forbidden', options?: { cause?: unknown }) {
    super(message, 403, 'FORBIDDEN', options);
  }
}

export class NotFoundError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, code = 'NOT_FOUND', options?: { cause?: unknown }) {
    super(message, 404, code, options);
  }
}

export class ConflictError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, code = 'CONFLICT', options?: { cause?: unknown }) {
    super(message, 409, code, options);
  }
}

export class UpstreamError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, options?: { cause?: unknown }) {
    super(message, 502, 'UPSTREAM_ERROR', options);
  }
}

export class TimeoutError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message, 504, 'TIMEOUT');
  }
}

export class WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError extends NotFoundError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message, 'WORKSPACE_CONFIG_NOT_FOUND');
  }
}

export class WorkspaceCountExceededError extends ConflictError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message, 'WORKSPACE_COUNT_EXCEEDED');
  }
}

export class WorkspaceRunCountExceededError extends ConflictError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message, 'WORKSPACE_RUN_COUNT_EXCEEDED');
  }
}

export class WorkspaceStartTimeoutError extends TimeoutError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export class WorkspaceStopTimeoutError extends TimeoutError {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export function errorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 34ecf9a94cb4e28680deacec7ce37e4a91c17d9b398740fc4da976d98af15b73 src/utils/k8s-errors.ts
import axios from 'axios';
import { AppError, ConflictError, ForbiddenError, NotFoundError, UpstreamError } from './errors.js';

interface KubeErrorLike {
  code?: unknown;
  statusCode?: unknown;
  body?: unknown;
  response?: { status?: unknown };
  message?: unknown;
}

function asNumber(value: unknown): number | undefined {
  return typeof value === 'number' ? value : undefined;
}

function parseBody(body: unknown): { code?: number; message?: string } {
  if (typeof body === 'string') {
    try {
      return parseBody(JSON.parse(body));
    } catch {
      return { message: body };
    }
  }
  if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
    const b = body as { code?: unknown; message?: unknown };
    return { code: asNumber(b.code), message: typeof b.message === 'string' ? b.message : undefined };
  }
  return {};
}

/** HTTP status carried by a Kubernetes client / axios error, if any. */
export function k8sStatus(error: unknown): number | undefined {
  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') return undefined;
  const e = error as KubeErrorLike;
  return asNumber(e.code) ?? asNumber(e.statusCode) ?? asNumber(e.response?.status) ?? parseBody(e.body).code;
}

export const isK8sStatus = (error: unknown, status: number): boolean => k8sStatus(error) === status;

/** Human readable message for an unknown failure. Never includes upstream response bodies verbatim for 5xx. */
export function describeError(context: string, error: unknown): string {
  const status = k8sStatus(error);
  const kubeMessage = parseBody((error as KubeErrorLike | undefined)?.body).message;

  if (status === 404) return `${context}: Resource not found (404)`;
  if (status !== undefined && !axios.isAxiosError(error)) {
    return `${context}: Kubernetes API error (${status}) ${kubeMessage ?? (error as Error)?.message ?? ''}`.trim();
  }
  if (axios.isAxiosError(error)) {
    if (error.response) return `${context}: HTTP ${error.response.status} ${error.message}`;
    if (error.request) return `${context}: No response received from server`;
    return `${context}: Axios error ${error.message}`;
  }
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    if (error.message.includes('certificate') || error.message.includes('SSL')) {
      return `${context}: TLS/SSL certificate error`;
    }
    return `${context}: ${error.message}`;
  }
  return `${context}: ${String(error)}`;
}

/** Translate whatever a Kubernetes call threw into an AppError. */
export function toAppError(context: string, error: unknown): AppError {
  if (error instanceof AppError) return error;
  const status = k8sStatus(error);
  const message = describeError(context, error);
  if (status === 404) return new NotFoundError(message, 'NOT_FOUND', { cause: error });
  if (status === 403) {
    return new ForbiddenError(`${context}: Permission denied, insufficient privileges`, { cause: error });
  }
  if (status === 409) return new ConflictError(message, 'CONFLICT', { cause: error });
  return new UpstreamError(message, { cause: error });
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 22b0cdd84fc7895580be2925fd37b215c58840158dee3f1b630b354c94a9b08d src/utils/logger.ts
export type LogLevel = 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error' | 'silent';

const ORDER: Record<LogLevel, number> = { debug: 10, info: 20, warn: 30, error: 40, silent: 100 };
const SENSITIVE = /token|password|authorization|secret|pat$|credential|cookie/i;

export interface Logger {
  debug(event: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
  info(event: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
  warn(event: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
  error(event: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
  child(bindings: Record<string, unknown>): Logger;
}

export type LogSink = (line: string) => void;

export function redact(value: unknown, depth = 0): unknown {
  if (depth > 5 || value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;
  if (value instanceof Error) return { name: value.name, message: value.message };
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((v) => redact(v, depth + 1));
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>).map(([k, v]) => [
      k,
      SENSITIVE.test(k) ? '[redacted]' : redact(v, depth + 1),
    ]),
  );
}

export function createLogger(
  level: LogLevel = 'info',
  sink: LogSink = (line) => process.stdout.write(`${line}\n`),
  bindings: Record<string, unknown> = {},
): Logger {
  const write = (lvl: Exclude<LogLevel, 'silent'>, event: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>): void => {
    if (ORDER[lvl] < ORDER[level]) return;
    sink(
      JSON.stringify({
        time: new Date().toISOString(),
        level: lvl,
        event,
        ...(redact(bindings) as object),
        ...(redact(fields ?? {}) as object),
      }),
    );
  };
  return {
    debug: (e, f) => write('debug', e, f),
    info: (e, f) => write('info', e, f),
    warn: (e, f) => write('warn', e, f),
    error: (e, f) => write('error', e, f),
    child: (b) => createLogger(level, sink, { ...bindings, ...b }),
  };
}

export const silentLogger: Logger = createLogger('silent');

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 2bbec4e3dc0d2adb41de8a5030dcce0008ab44cf4ef127dc3e474602bcd88f66 src/utils/names.ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { BadRequestError } from './errors.js';

const SOEID_RE = /^[a-z0-9]{3,20}$/;
const CSIID_RE = /^\d{1,10}$/;
const DNS_LABEL_RE = /^[a-z0-9]([-a-z0-9]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?$/;
const TOKEN_NAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9]([-a-z0-9]{0,40}[a-z0-9])?$/;

/** Path segments that are routes, not users (the legacy unversioned layout puts `:soeid` at the root). */
const RESERVED_SOEIDS = new Set(['api', 'login', 'catalog', 'healthz', 'readyz']);

/** Validate and normalise a user id. User ids end up in namespace names, so they are strictly allow-listed. */
export function parseSoeid(value: string): string {
  const soeid = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!SOEID_RE.test(soeid) || RESERVED_SOEIDS.has(soeid)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid soeid', 'INVALID_SOEID');
  }
  return soeid;
}

export function parseCsiid(value: string): string {
  const csiid = value.trim();
  if (!CSIID_RE.test(csiid)) throw new BadRequestError('csiid must be numeric', 'INVALID_CSIID');
  return csiid;
}

export function parseResourceName(value: string, what = 'resource name'): string {
  const name = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!DNS_LABEL_RE.test(name)) throw new BadRequestError(`Invalid ${what}`, 'INVALID_NAME');
  return name;
}

export function parseTokenName(value: string): string {
  const name = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!TOKEN_NAME_RE.test(name)) throw new BadRequestError('Invalid token name', 'INVALID_TOKEN_NAME');
  return name;
}

export const workspaceNamespace = (soeid: string, csiid: string): string => `${soeid}-devspaces-${csiid}`;
export const deploymentNamespace = (soeid: string, csiid: string): string =>
  `${soeid}-devspaces-deploy-${csiid}`;

export function csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace(namespace: string): string | undefined {
  return /-devspaces-(\d+)$/.exec(namespace)?.[1];
}

export function csiidFromDeploymentNamespace(namespace: string): string | undefined {
  return /-devspaces-deploy-(\d+)$/.exec(namespace)?.[1];
}

/** `https://api.<cluster>.ecs.dyn…:6443` → `<cluster>`. */
export function extractClusterName(url: string | undefined): string {
  const match = /(?:api\.|apps\.)([^.]+)\.ecs/.exec(url ?? '');
  return match?.[1] ?? 'unknown-cluster';
}

export const randomSuffix = (): string => randomBytes(2).toString('hex');

/** DNS-1123 workspace name `<stack>-<suffix>`, at most 63 characters. */
export function cleanWorkspaceName(base: string, suffix: string = randomSuffix()): string {
  const cleaned =
    base
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]+/g, '-')
      .replace(/-{2,}/g, '-')
      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
      .slice(0, 63 - suffix.length - 1)
      .replace(/-+$/g, '') || 'workspace';
  return `${cleaned}-${suffix}`;
}
