@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 6b0933997d10467ef3ec4c5fd9b8780bea7da92f71ff7b464677080536e0fd99 tests/integration/workspaces.test.ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { startIntegration, type Integration } from '../helpers/integration.js';

let t: Integration;
beforeAll(async () => {
  t = await startIntegration();
});
afterAll(() => t.close());
beforeEach(async () => {
  t.fakes.kube.namespaces.clear();
  t.fakes.kube.objects.clear();
  await t.sql('DELETE FROM metrics."devspaces-metrics"');
  t.registry.failing = false;
});

const base = '/api/v1/pp1/workspaces';
const body = { devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven', csiid: '1001' };

describe('GET /api/v1/catalog', () => {
  it('returns the registry catalog over real HTTP', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/catalog');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.map((e: { name: string }) => e.name)).toEqual(['java-maven', 'python']);
  });

  it('answers 502 when the registry is down', async () => {
    t.registry.failing = true;
    const res = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/catalog');
    expect(res.status).toBe(502);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('UPSTREAM_ERROR');
  });
});

describe('POST /api/v1/:soeid/workspaces', () => {
  it('creates the project and the workspace, and records both', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({
      namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-1001',
      csiid: '1001',
      stack: 'java-maven',
      version: '2.0.0',
      displayName: 'Java Maven',
      editor: 'Visual Studio Code',
      limitcpu: 2,
      limitmemory: 4,
      storage: 3,
    });

    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces('pp1-devspaces-1001')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(registryPaths()).toEqual(['GET /v2index/all', 'GET /devfiles/java-maven/2.0.0']);

    const rows = await t.metricsRows();
    expect(rows.map((r) => `${r.eventtype}:${r.resourcetype}`)).toEqual([
      'create:project',
      'create:workspace',
    ]);
    expect(rows[1]).toMatchObject({
      soeid: 'pp1',
      csiid: '1001',
      workspaceversion: '2.0.0',
      editorname: 'vscode',
    });
    expect(Number(rows[1]?.limitcpu)).toBe(2);
    expect(rows[1]?.status).toEqual({ failed: false, error: '', message: '' });
  });

  it('does not recreate the project for a second workspace', async () => {
    await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    const rows = await t.metricsRows();
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.resourcetype === 'project')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.resourcetype === 'workspace')).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('honours editor, stack version and repository', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send({
      ...body,
      editor: 'intellij',
      stackVersion: '1.0.0',
      repositoryUrl: 'https://github.com/acme/app.git',
      repositoryBranch: 'main',
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.version).toBe('1.0.0');
    const dw = t.fakes.kube.workspaces()[0];
    expect(dw?.spec?.template?.projects).toEqual([
      {
        name: 'app',
        git: { remotes: { origin: 'https://github.com/acme/app.git' }, checkoutFrom: { revision: 'main' } },
      },
    ]);
    expect(dw?.spec?.template?.components?.some((c) => c.name === 'che-idea-server')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('answers 404 for an unknown stack and creates nothing', async () => {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send({ ...body, devworkspaceValue: 'cobol' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'WORKSPACE_CONFIG_NOT_FOUND' });
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('answers 502 when the registry fails mid-create', async () => {
    t.registry.failing = true;
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(502);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it.each([
    ['missing stack', { csiid: '1' }],
    ['bad csiid', { ...body, csiid: 'x' }],
    ['unknown field', { ...body, sneaky: true }],
    ['http repository', { ...body, repositoryUrl: 'http://github.com/a/b' }],
  ])('answers 400 for %s', async (_l, payload) => {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(payload);
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
  });

  it('answers 409 once the running limit (2) is reached', async () => {
    await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'WORKSPACE_RUN_COUNT_EXCEEDED' });
    expect(res.body.message).toMatch(/limit is 2/);
  });

  it('answers 409 once the total limit (3) is reached', async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      t.fakes.kube.setPhase('pp1-devspaces-1001', res.body.id, 'Stopped');
    }
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('WORKSPACE_COUNT_EXCEEDED');
  });

  it('records a failed metric and answers 502 when the cluster rejects the workspace', async () => {
    t.fakes.kube.failOnce('create:DevWorkspace', Object.assign(new Error('denied'), { code: 500 }));
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(500);
    const failed = (await t.metricsRows()).find((r) => r.resourcetype === 'workspace');
    expect(failed?.status).toMatchObject({ failed: true });
  });

  it('refuses to use a namespace owned by someone else', async () => {
    t.fakes.kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'intruder', '1001');
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('NAMESPACE_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH');
  });
});

describe('workspace lifecycle', () => {
  async function created() {
    const res = await t.api('post', base).send(body);
    return res.body as { id: string; namespace: string };
  }

  it('lists and gets only the caller’s workspaces', async () => {
    const ws = await created();
    expect((await t.api('get', base)).body.map((w: { id: string }) => w.id)).toEqual([ws.id]);
    expect((await t.api('get', `${base}/${ws.id}`)).body.id).toBe(ws.id);
    expect((await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp2/workspaces')).body).toEqual([]);
    expect((await t.api('get', `/api/v1/pp2/workspaces/${ws.id}`)).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('reflects the cluster’s phase and URL', async () => {
    const ws = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(ws.namespace, ws.id, 'Running', 'https://ws.example/ide');
    const res = await t.api('get', `${base}/${ws.id}`);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({ phase: 'Running', mainUrl: 'https://ws.example/ide' });
  });

  it('stops then starts, with metrics for each', async () => {
    const ws = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(ws.namespace, ws.id, 'Running');
    t.fakes.clock.advance(3 * 60 * 1000);

    const stop = await t.api('post', `${base}/${ws.id}/stop`);
    expect(stop.status).toBe(200);
    expect(stop.body.message).toBe(`Workspace with id ${ws.id} is stopping.`);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()[0]?.spec?.started).toBe(false);

    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(ws.namespace, ws.id, 'Stopped');
    const start = await t.api('post', `${base}/${ws.id}/start`);
    expect(start.status).toBe(200);
    expect(start.body.message).toBe(`Workspace with id ${ws.id} is starting.`);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()[0]?.spec?.started).toBe(true);

    const rows = await t.metricsRows();
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.eventtype)).toEqual(['create', 'create', 'stop', 'start']);
    expect(rows[2]?.duration).toBe('3 minutes');
  });

  it('refuses to start a third running workspace', async () => {
    const a = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(a.namespace, a.id, 'Stopped');
    const b = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(b.namespace, b.id, 'Stopped');
    const c = await created();
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(c.namespace, c.id, 'Stopped');
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(a.namespace, a.id, 'Running');
    t.fakes.kube.setPhase(b.namespace, b.id, 'Running');
    const res = await t.api('post', `${base}/${c.id}/start`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
  });

  it('deletes a workspace and records its lifetime', async () => {
    const ws = await created();
    t.fakes.clock.advance(2 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const res = await t.api('delete', `${base}/${ws.id}`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.message).toBe(`Workspace with id ${ws.id} is terminating.`);
    expect(t.fakes.kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
    const last = (await t.metricsRows()).at(-1);
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ eventtype: 'delete', duration: '2 hours' });
  });

  it.each([
    ['get', ''],
    ['delete', ''],
    ['post', '/start'],
    ['post', '/stop'],
  ] as const)('answers 404 for %s %s on an unknown workspace', async (method, suffix) => {
    const res = await t.api(method, `${base}/ghost${suffix}`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.code).toBe('WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND');
  });

  it('GET /projects lists the user’s projects', async () => {
    await created();
    const res = await t.api('get', '/api/v1/pp1/projects');
    expect(res.body).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ name: 'pp1-devspaces-1001', csiid: '1001', soeid: 'pp1' }),
    ]);
  });
});

function registryPaths(): string[] {
  return t.registry.requests.slice(-2);
}

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 26529f30b509962be4d14fde5c4e27c4d5293f2b3a2d37e5cddc9b419b4f64eb tests/unit/config/env.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { loadConfig, unquote } from '../../../src/config/env.js';

const base = {
  SECRET_KEY: 'a-sufficiently-long-secret',
  ADMIN_USERNAME: 'admin',
  ADMIN_PASSWORD: 'pw',
  DEVREG_URL: 'http://registry',
  POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'false',
};

describe('loadConfig', () => {
  it('applies defaults', () => {
    const c = loadConfig(base);
    expect(c.port).toBe(3050);
    expect(c.limits).toEqual({ maxRunning: 3, maxTotal: 5 });
    expect(c.devreg.editors).toEqual({ vscode: 'che-code', intellij: 'che-idea', goland: 'che-goland' });
    expect(c.auth.expiresIn).toBe('60d');
    expect(c.postgres.enabled).toBe(false);
    expect(c.github).toEqual({ url: 'https://github.com', userSuffix: '' });
  });

  it('refuses to start without SECRET_KEY (no fallback secret)', () => {
    const { SECRET_KEY: _omit, ...rest } = base;
    expect(() => loadConfig(rest)).toThrow(/SECRET_KEY/);
  });

  it('rejects a short SECRET_KEY', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, SECRET_KEY: 'short' })).toThrow(/at least 16/);
  });

  it('lists every problem at once', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({})).toThrow(
      /SECRET_KEY[\s\S]*ADMIN_USERNAME[\s\S]*ADMIN_PASSWORD[\s\S]*DEVREG_URL/,
    );
  });

  it('treats empty variables as unset', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, PORT: '', KUBECONFIG_PATH: '' }).port).toBe(3050);
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, KUBECONFIG_PATH: '' }).cluster.kubeconfigPath).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('coerces numbers and rejects non-positive ones', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, PORT: '8080', MAX_RUNNING_WORKSPACES: '7' }).limits.maxRunning).toBe(7);
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, PORT: '0' })).toThrow(/PORT/);
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, PORT: 'abc' })).toThrow(/PORT/);
  });

  it('requires database coordinates only when Postgres is enabled', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true' })).toThrow(
      /POSTGRES_DATABASE and POSTGRES_USER/,
    );
    const ok = loadConfig({ ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true', POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'd', POSTGRES_USER: 'u' });
    expect(ok.postgres).toMatchObject({ enabled: true, database: 'd', user: 'u', port: 5432, ssl: true });
  });

  it('uses TLS to Postgres by default, as the original service always did', () => {
    const env = { ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true', POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'd', POSTGRES_USER: 'u' };
    expect(loadConfig(env).postgres).toMatchObject({ ssl: true, env: 'development', caFile: undefined });
    expect(loadConfig({ ...env, POSTGRES_SSL: 'false' }).postgres.ssl).toBe(false);
    expect(loadConfig({ ...env, NODE_ENV: 'production' }).postgres.env).toBe('production');
  });

  it('accepts explicit CA overrides', () => {
    const env = { ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true', POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'd', POSTGRES_USER: 'u' };
    const pg = loadConfig({ ...env, POSTGRES_CA_FILE: '/ca.pem', POSTGRES_CERT_DIR: '/certs' }).postgres;
    expect(pg).toMatchObject({ caFile: '/ca.pem', certDir: '/certs' });
  });

  describe('the existing Orion database environment', () => {
    // Exactly what the team exports (quotes included, as they appear when pasted from the notes file).
    const exported = {
      POSTGRES_DATABASE: 'orion_dev_metadata',
      POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: 'NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net',
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: 'secret',
      POSTGRES_PORT: "'1524'",
      POSTGRES_USER: 'app_orion_pgsql_dev',
      POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME: `'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"'`,
      NODE_ENV: 'development',
    };

    it('is understood as-is, with or without the shell quotes', () => {
      for (const env of [
        exported,
        {
          ...exported,
          POSTGRES_PORT: '1524',
          POSTGRES_DATABASE_TABLENAME: 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
        },
      ]) {
        expect(loadConfig({ ...base, POSTGRES_ENABLED: 'true', ...env }).postgres).toEqual({
          enabled: true,
          host: 'NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net',
          port: 1524,
          database: 'orion_dev_metadata',
          user: 'app_orion_pgsql_dev',
          password: 'secret',
          ssl: true,
          caFile: undefined,
          certDir: undefined,
          env: 'development',
          table: 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
        });
      }
    });

    it('defaults to Postgres being on, so these variables alone are enough', () => {
      const { POSTGRES_ENABLED: _off, ...withoutFlag } = base;
      expect(loadConfig({ ...withoutFlag, ...exported }).postgres.enabled).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  it('unquote only unwraps one matching pair', () => {
    expect(unquote("'a'")).toBe('a');
    expect(unquote('"a b"')).toBe('a b');
    expect(unquote(`'metrics."t-1"'`)).toBe('metrics."t-1"');
    for (const untouched of ["'a", 'a"', `'a"`, 'plain', "it's", '"', "''".slice(0, 1)]) {
      expect(unquote(untouched)).toBe(untouched);
    }
    expect(unquote("''")).toBe('');
  });

  it('strips a trailing slash from GITHUB_URL', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, GITHUB_URL: 'https://ghe.example.com/' }).github.url).toBe(
      'https://ghe.example.com',
    );
  });

  it('rejects an unknown LOG_LEVEL', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, LOG_LEVEL: 'loud' })).toThrow(/LOG_LEVEL/);
  });
});

import { load } from 'js-yaml';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Devfile } from '../../../src/domain/types.js';
import {
  applyEditorProfile,
  parseDevfile,
  updateDevfileYaml,
} from '../../../src/factories/devfile-updater.js';
import { BadRequestError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML } from '../../helpers/sample-devfile.js';

const sample = (): Devfile => parseDevfile(SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML);
const byName = (d: Devfile, name: string) => d.components?.find((c) => c.name === name);

describe('parseDevfile', () => {
  it('parses a devfile', () => {
    expect(sample().metadata?.name).toBe('java-maven');
  });
  it.each([
    ['invalid yaml', 'a: [unclosed'],
    ['a list', '- 1\n- 2'],
    ['a scalar', 'hello'],
    ['no components', 'schemaVersion: 2.2.0'],
    ['empty', ''],
  ])('rejects %s', (_label, text) => {
    expect(() => parseDevfile(text)).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
});

describe('applyEditorProfile', () => {
  it('leaves the devfile alone for VS Code (but returns a copy)', () => {
    const original = sample();
    const result = applyEditorProfile(original, 'vscode');
    expect(result).toEqual(original);
    expect(result).not.toBe(original);
    result.components?.pop();
    expect(original.components).toHaveLength(4);
  });

  it('never mutates its input', () => {
    const original = sample();
    const snapshot = structuredClone(original);
    applyEditorProfile(original, 'intellij');
    expect(original).toEqual(snapshot);
  });

  describe.each([
    ['intellij', 'che-idea-server', 2222, 'che-idea-server.eclipse.org'],
    ['goland', 'che-goland-server', 3400, 'che-goland-server.eclipse.org'],
  ] as const)('%s', (editor, server, port, partOf) => {
    const result = applyEditorProfile(sample(), editor);
    const tools = byName(result, 'tools');

    it('drops commands and events', () => {
      expect(result.commands).toBeUndefined();
      expect(result.events).toBeUndefined();
    });
    it('exposes the editor server endpoint on the tools container', () => {
      expect(tools?.container?.endpoints).toEqual([
        expect.objectContaining({
          name: server,
          targetPort: port,
          exposure: 'public',
          protocol: 'https',
          secure: true,
        }),
      ]);
      expect(tools?.container?.env).toBeUndefined();
      expect(tools?.attributes?.['app.kubernetes.io/part-of']).toBe(partOf);
    });
    it('moves the editor volume mount and removes venv', () => {
      expect(tools?.container?.volumeMounts).toEqual([{ name: server, path: `/${server}` }]);
      expect(byName(result, 'venv')).toBeUndefined();
      expect(byName(result, 'checode')).toBeUndefined();
      expect(byName(result, server)?.volume).toEqual({});
    });
    it('resizes the home volume', () => {
      expect(byName(result, 'home')?.volume?.size).toBe('10Gi');
    });
  });

  it('accepts a custom home volume size', () => {
    expect(byName(applyEditorProfile(sample(), 'goland', '20Gi'), 'home')?.volume?.size).toBe('20Gi');
  });

  it('tolerates a stack without a tools component', () => {
    const d: Devfile = { components: [{ name: 'other', container: { image: 'x' } }] };
    expect(applyEditorProfile(d, 'intellij').components).toEqual(d.components);
  });

  it('tolerates a tools container without volume mounts', () => {
    const d: Devfile = { components: [{ name: 'tools', container: { image: 'x' } }] };
    const tools = byName(applyEditorProfile(d, 'intellij'), 'tools');
    expect(tools?.container?.endpoints).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe('updateDevfileYaml', () => {
  it('round-trips through YAML', () => {
    const out = load(updateDevfileYaml(SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML, 'goland')) as Devfile;
    expect(byName(out, 'che-goland-server')).toBeDefined();
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Devfile } from '../../../src/domain/types.js';
import { buildLogRecord } from '../../../src/factories/log-factory.js';
import { buildDevWorkspaceObject, WORKSPACE_LABELS } from '../../../src/factories/workspace-factory.js';
import { parseDevfile } from '../../../src/factories/devfile-updater.js';
import { SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML } from '../../helpers/sample-devfile.js';

const args = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof buildDevWorkspaceObject>[0]> = {}) => ({
  name: 'java-maven-ab12',
  namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-1',
  csiid: '1',
  stack: 'java-maven',
  stackVersion: '2.0.0',
  devfile: parseDevfile(SAMPLE_DEVFILE_YAML),
  editor: 'vscode' as const,
  editorId: 'che-code',
  devSpacesServiceFqdn: 'https://devspaces.test',
  ...over,
});

describe('buildDevWorkspaceObject', () => {

      [WORKSPACE_LABELS.csiid]: '1',
      [WORKSPACE_LABELS.stack]: 'java-maven',
      [WORKSPACE_LABELS.managedBy]: 'workspace-mgr',
    });
  });

    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(
      args({ repositoryUrl: 'https://github.com/acme/My_Repo.git', repositoryBranch: 'develop' }),

    expect(dw.spec?.template).not.toHaveProperty('commands');

  });

    const dw = buildDevWorkspaceObject(args({ devfile: { components: [] } }));
    expect(dw.metadata?.annotations?.workspaceDisplayName).toBe('java-maven');
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl df9a1b7ab9f68e6c1cf0fac170a9cbc1a2ecc6af89b72fda2cc11786d717f400 tests/unit/http/auth.test.ts
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from 'express';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  authenticate,
  authorizeSoeid,
  checkAdminCredentials,
  getPrincipal,
  safeEqual,
  signToken,
} from '../../../src/http/auth.js';
import { ForbiddenError, UnauthorizedError, BadRequestError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';

const auth = {
  secretKey: 'unit-test-secret-0123456789',
  adminUsername: 'admin',
  adminPassword: 'pw',
  expiresIn: '1h',
};

function run(handler: (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => void, req: Partial<Request>) {
  const res = { locals: {} } as Response;
  let error: unknown;
  let called = false;
  handler({ headers: {}, params: {}, ...req } as Request, res, ((e?: unknown) => {
    called = true;
    error = e;
  }) as NextFunction);
  return { res, error, called };
}

describe('safeEqual / checkAdminCredentials', () => {
  it('compares strings of any length', () => {
    expect(safeEqual('a', 'a')).toBe(true);
    expect(safeEqual('a', 'b')).toBe(false);
    expect(safeEqual('a', 'aa')).toBe(false);
    expect(safeEqual('', '')).toBe(true);
  });
  it('requires both username and password', () => {
    expect(checkAdminCredentials(auth, 'admin', 'pw')).toBe(true);
    expect(checkAdminCredentials(auth, 'admin', 'x')).toBe(false);
    expect(checkAdminCredentials(auth, 'x', 'pw')).toBe(false);
    expect(checkAdminCredentials(auth, '', '')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('signToken', () => {
  it('issues an HS256 token with subject and role', () => {
    const token = signToken(auth, { soeid: 'admin', role: 'admin' });
    const decoded = jwt.verify(token, auth.secretKey, { complete: true });
    expect(decoded.header.alg).toBe('HS256');
    expect(decoded.payload).toMatchObject({ sub: 'admin', role: 'admin' });
  });
});

describe('authenticate', () => {
  const mw = authenticate(auth);
  const bearer = (token: string) => ({ headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });

  it('accepts a valid token and exposes the principal', () => {
    const { res, error } = run(mw, bearer(signToken(auth, { soeid: 'admin', role: 'admin' })));
    expect(error).toBeUndefined();
    expect(getPrincipal(res)).toEqual({ soeid: 'admin', role: 'admin' });
  });

  it('treats anything but role=admin as a plain user', () => {
    const token = jwt.sign({ role: 'root' }, auth.secretKey, { subject: 'pp1' });
    expect(getPrincipal(run(mw, bearer(token)).res).role).toBe('user');
  });

  it.each([
    ['no header', {}],
    ['wrong scheme', { headers: { authorization: 'Basic abc' } }],
    ['no token', { headers: { authorization: 'Bearer' } }],
    ['garbage', bearer('not.a.jwt')],
    ['wrong secret', bearer(jwt.sign({}, 'another-secret-another-secret', { subject: 'x' }))],
    ['expired', bearer(jwt.sign({}, auth.secretKey, { subject: 'x', expiresIn: -10 }))],
    ['no subject', bearer(jwt.sign({ role: 'admin' }, auth.secretKey))],
    [
      'alg none',
      bearer(
        `${Buffer.from('{"alg":"none"}').toString('base64url')}.${Buffer.from('{"sub":"x"}').toString('base64url')}.`,
      ),
    ],
    ['other algorithm', bearer(jwt.sign({}, auth.secretKey, { subject: 'x', algorithm: 'HS512' }))],
  ] as const)('rejects %s with 401', (_label, req) => {
    const { error } = run(mw, req as Partial<Request>);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedError);
  });
});

describe('authorizeSoeid', () => {
  const mw = authorizeSoeid();
  const withPrincipal = (principal: unknown, soeid: string) => {
    const res = { locals: { principal } } as unknown as Response;
    let error: unknown;
    mw({ params: { soeid } } as unknown as Request, res, ((e?: unknown) => (error = e)) as NextFunction);
    return { res, error };
  };

  it('lets an admin act for any valid user and normalises the id', () => {
    const { res, error } = withPrincipal({ soeid: 'admin', role: 'admin' }, 'PP58386');
    expect(error).toBeUndefined();
    expect(res.locals.soeid).toBe('pp58386');
  });
  it('lets a user act for themselves only', () => {
    expect(withPrincipal({ soeid: 'PP1', role: 'user' }, 'pp1').error).toBeUndefined();
    expect(withPrincipal({ soeid: 'pp1', role: 'user' }, 'pp2').error).toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
  });
  it('rejects malformed and reserved ids', () => {
    for (const bad of ['a/b', '..', 'api', 'login', '']) {
      expect(withPrincipal({ soeid: 'admin', role: 'admin' }, bad).error).toBeInstanceOf(BadRequestError);
    }
  });
  it('requires an authenticated principal', () => {
    expect(withPrincipal(undefined, 'pp1').error).toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedError);
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 203f51c5a0f978617f7885186209652ab7fcbf6cc1103c8058f9bbdbdc3924d5 tests/unit/http/errors-schemas.test.ts
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from 'express';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { asyncHandler, createErrorHandler, errorBody, notFoundHandler } from '../../../src/http/errors.js';
import {
  createWorkspaceSchema,
  gitConfigBodySchema,
  loginSchema,
  parse,
  patBodySchema,
} from '../../../src/http/schemas.js';
import { BadRequestError, ConflictError, UpstreamError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { createLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';

function fakeRes() {
  const res = {
    locals: { requestId: 'rid' },
    headersSent: false,
    statusCode: 200,
    body: undefined as unknown,
    status(code: number) {
      this.statusCode = code;
      return this;
    },
    json(b: unknown) {
      this.body = b;
      return this;
    },
  };
  return res;
}

function handle(err: unknown) {
  const logs: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  const handler = createErrorHandler(createLogger('debug', (l) => logs.push(JSON.parse(l))));
  const res = fakeRes();
  let forwarded: unknown;
  handler(
    err,
    { path: '/x' } as Request,
    res as unknown as Response,
    ((e?: unknown) => (forwarded = e)) as NextFunction,
  );
  return { res, logs, forwarded };
}

describe('error handler', () => {
  it('maps AppErrors to their status and the standard body', () => {
    const { res } = handle(new ConflictError('taken', 'PAT_EXISTS'));
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect(res.body).toEqual({ error: 'taken', message: 'taken', code: 'PAT_EXISTS' });
  });

  it('logs 5xx AppErrors but not 4xx', () => {
    expect(handle(new UpstreamError('cluster down')).logs).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(handle(new BadRequestError('bad')).logs).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('maps body-parser failures', () => {
    expect(handle(Object.assign(new Error('x'), { type: 'entity.parse.failed' })).res.statusCode).toBe(400);
    expect(handle(Object.assign(new Error('x'), { type: 'entity.too.large' })).res.statusCode).toBe(413);
  });

  it('hides internals of unexpected errors', () => {
    const { res, logs } = handle(new Error('db password is hunter2'));
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(500);
    expect(res.body).toEqual({
      error: 'Internal server error',
      message: 'Internal server error',
      code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR',
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(res.body)).not.toContain('hunter2');
    expect(logs[0]).toMatchObject({ event: 'request.crashed', requestId: 'rid' });
  });

  it('defers to express when headers are already sent', () => {
    const logs: string[] = [];
    const handler = createErrorHandler(createLogger('debug', (l) => logs.push(l)));
    const res = { ...fakeRes(), headersSent: true };
    let forwarded: unknown;
    const err = new Error('late');
    handler(
      err,
      {} as Request,
      res as unknown as Response,
      ((e?: unknown) => (forwarded = e)) as NextFunction,
    );
    expect(forwarded).toBe(err);
  });

  it('404 handler names the route', () => {
    const res = fakeRes();
    notFoundHandler(
      { method: 'GET', path: '/nope' } as Request,
      res as unknown as Response,
      (() => undefined) as NextFunction,
    );
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body).toEqual(errorBody('Route not found: GET /nope', 'ROUTE_NOT_FOUND'));
  });
});

describe('asyncHandler', () => {
  it('forwards rejections to next()', async () => {
    const boom = new Error('async boom');
    let forwarded: unknown;
    asyncHandler(async () => {
      throw boom;
    })({} as Request, {} as Response, ((e?: unknown) => (forwarded = e)) as NextFunction);
    await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r));
    expect(forwarded).toBe(boom);
  });
});

describe('createWorkspaceSchema', () => {
  const ok = { devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven', csiid: '177398' };

  it('applies defaults and accepts numeric csiid', () => {
    expect(parse(createWorkspaceSchema, { ...ok, csiid: 177398 })).toEqual({ ...ok, editor: 'vscode' });
  });
  it('accepts all optional fields', () => {
    const body = {
      ...ok,
      editor: 'goland',
      stackVersion: '2.0.0',
      repositoryUrl: 'https://github.com/acme/app.git',
      repositoryBranch: 'feature/x-1',
    };
    expect(parse(createWorkspaceSchema, body)).toMatchObject(body);
  });
  it.each([
    ['missing stack', { csiid: '1' }],
    ['non-numeric csiid', { ...ok, csiid: 'abc' }],
    ['unknown editor', { ...ok, editor: 'emacs' }],
    ['stack with slash', { ...ok, devworkspaceValue: '../etc' }],
    ['http repo url', { ...ok, repositoryUrl: 'http://github.com/a/b' }],
    ['ssh repo url', { ...ok, repositoryUrl: 'git@github.com:a/b.git' }],
    ['bad branch', { ...ok, repositoryBranch: 'a b;rm' }],
    ['unknown property', { ...ok, admin: true }],
    ['empty body', undefined],
  ])('rejects %s with a 400 listing the problem', (_l, body) => {
    expect(() => parse(createWorkspaceSchema, body)).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
});

describe('other schemas', () => {
  it('login needs both fields and nothing else', () => {
    expect(parse(loginSchema, { username: 'a', password: 'b' })).toEqual({ username: 'a', password: 'b' });
    expect(() => parse(loginSchema, { username: 'a' })).toThrow(BadRequestError);
    expect(() => parse(loginSchema, { username: 'a', password: 'b', extra: 1 })).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
  it('pat and gitconfig bodies', () => {
    expect(parse(patBodySchema, { csiid: 5, token: 't' })).toEqual({ csiid: '5', token: 't' });
    expect(() => parse(patBodySchema, { csiid: '5' })).toThrow(BadRequestError);
    expect(parse(gitConfigBodySchema, { csiid: '5', content: 'x' }).content).toBe('x');
    expect(() => parse(gitConfigBodySchema, { csiid: '5', content: '' })).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
  it('error messages name the field', () => {
    try {
      parse(createWorkspaceSchema, { devworkspaceValue: 'x', csiid: 'abc' });
      expect.unreachable();
    } catch (e) {
      expect((e as Error).message).toContain('csiid');
      expect((e as BadRequestError).code).toBe('VALIDATION_ERROR');
    }
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 95b9a23fcee38b89856461917cacf3e788c56d3024d132093b02b72d8fd89be4 tests/unit/infra/DevfileRegistryClient.test.ts
import axios, { AxiosError, type AxiosInstance, type InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  DevfileRegistryClient,
  createRegistryHttp,
} from '../../../src/infra/devreg/DevfileRegistryClient.js';
import { UpstreamError, WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';

type Handler = (url: string, config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => { status: number; data: unknown };

function http(handler: Handler): { http: AxiosInstance; urls: string[] } {
  const urls: string[] = [];
  const instance = axios.create({
    baseURL: 'http://registry.test',
    validateStatus: (s) => s >= 200 && s < 300,
    adapter: async (config) => {
      const url = config.url ?? '';
      urls.push(url);
      const { status, data } = handler(url, config as InternalAxiosRequestConfig);
      const response = {
        data,
        status,
        statusText: String(status),
        headers: {},
        config: config as InternalAxiosRequestConfig,
      };
      if (status >= 400)
        throw new AxiosError(
          `status ${status}`,
          'ERR_BAD_RESPONSE',
          config as InternalAxiosRequestConfig,
          {},
          response,
        );
      return response;
    },
  });
  return { http: instance, urls };
}

const catalog = [
  { name: 'java', versions: [{ version: '1.0.0' }, { version: '2.0.0', default: true }] },
  { name: 'py', versions: [{ version: '3.1.0' }] },
  { name: 'empty', versions: [] },
];

const registry = (devfile = 'schemaVersion: 2.2.0') =>
  http((url) => (url === 'v2index/all' ? { status: 200, data: catalog } : { status: 200, data: devfile }));

describe('getCatalog', () => {
  it('returns the registry index', async () => {
    expect(await new DevfileRegistryClient(registry().http).getCatalog()).toEqual(catalog);
  });
  it('wraps failures as UpstreamError', async () => {
    const client = new DevfileRegistryClient(http(() => ({ status: 503, data: '' })).http);
    await expect(client.getCatalog()).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
  });
  it('rejects a non-list index', async () => {
    const client = new DevfileRegistryClient(http(() => ({ status: 200, data: { not: 'a list' } })).http);
    await expect(client.getCatalog()).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
  });
});

describe('getDevfile', () => {
  it('picks the default version and fetches that devfile', async () => {
    const { http: h, urls } = registry('yaml-body');
    expect(await new DevfileRegistryClient(h).getDevfile('java')).toEqual({
      yaml: 'yaml-body',
      version: '2.0.0',
    });
    expect(urls).toEqual(['v2index/all', 'devfiles/java/2.0.0']);
  });
  it('honours a requested version', async () => {
    const { http: h, urls } = registry();
    expect((await new DevfileRegistryClient(h).getDevfile('java', '1.0.0')).version).toBe('1.0.0');
    expect(urls.at(-1)).toBe('devfiles/java/1.0.0');
  });
  it('falls back to the first version when none is marked default', async () => {
    expect((await new DevfileRegistryClient(registry().http).getDevfile('py')).version).toBe('3.1.0');
  });
  it('404s for an unknown stack, unknown version, or versionless stack', async () => {
    const client = new DevfileRegistryClient(registry().http);
    await expect(client.getDevfile('cobol')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError);
    await expect(client.getDevfile('java', '9.9.9')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError);
    await expect(client.getDevfile('empty')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError);
  });
  it('URL-encodes stack and version', async () => {
    const { http: h, urls } = http((url) =>
      url === 'v2index/all'
        ? { status: 200, data: [{ name: 'a/b', versions: [{ version: '1 0' }] }] }
        : { status: 200, data: 'y' },
    );
    await new DevfileRegistryClient(h).getDevfile('a/b');
    expect(urls.at(-1)).toBe('devfiles/a%2Fb/1%200');
  });
  it('wraps a failing devfile download as UpstreamError', async () => {
    const h = http((url) =>
      url === 'v2index/all' ? { status: 200, data: catalog } : { status: 500, data: '' },
    ).http;
    await expect(new DevfileRegistryClient(h).getDevfile('java')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
  });
});

describe('createRegistryHttp', () => {
  it('sets base URL and a timeout', () => {
    const h = createRegistryHttp('http://x');
    expect(h.defaults.baseURL).toBe('http://x');
    expect(h.defaults.timeout).toBe(10_000);
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { EmailNotifier, NoopNotifier, type FetchFn } from '../../../src/infra/email/EmailNotifier.js';
import {
  clusterLinks,
  deletedNamespacesEmailBody,
  escapeHtml,
  existingNamespaceEmailBody,
  newNamespaceEmailBody,
} from '../../../src/infra/email/email-templates.js';
import { GraphqlUserDirectory, NullUserDirectory } from '../../../src/infra/email/GraphqlUserDirectory.js';
import { createLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';

const config = {
  url: 'http://mail.test/',
  username: 'svc',
  password: 'pw',
  sender: 'noreply@example.com',
  cc: 'team@example.com',
  emailDomain: 'example.com',
};
const API = 'https://api.prod1.ecs.dyn.example.net:6443';

function setup(opts: { status?: number; fail?: boolean; name?: string; cfg?: Partial<typeof config> } = {}) {
  const calls: { url: string; init: RequestInit }[] = [];
  const logs: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  const fetchFn: FetchFn = async (url, init) => {
    calls.push({ url, init });
    if (opts.fail) throw new Error('network down');
    return new Response('{}', { status: opts.status ?? 200 });
  };
  const directory = new (class {
    async firstName() {
      return opts.name;
    }
  })();
  const logger = createLogger('debug', (l) => logs.push(JSON.parse(l)));
  const notifier = new EmailNotifier({ ...config, ...opts.cfg }, API, directory, logger, fetchFn);
  const body = () => JSON.parse(String(calls[0]?.init.body)) as Record<string, string>;
  return { notifier, calls, logs, body };
}

describe('EmailNotifier', () => {
  it('posts a new-namespace email to the mail service with basic auth', async () => {
    const { notifier, calls, body } = setup({ name: 'Pat' });
    await notifier.namespaceCreated({ soeid: 'pp1', namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-deploy-1' });
    expect(calls[0]?.url).toBe('http://mail.test/send-mail/v2');
    expect((calls[0]?.init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization).toBe(
      `Basic ${Buffer.from('svc:pw').toString('base64')}`,
    );
    expect(body()).toMatchObject({
      sender: 'noreply@example.com',
      receiver: 'pp1@example.com',
      receiverName: 'PP1',
      cc: 'team@example.com',
      subject: 'Success! Your DevSpaces Deployment Project is Ready!',
      isHtml: true,
    });
    expect(body().emailMessage).toContain('Dear Pat,');
    expect(body().emailMessage).toContain('pp1-devspaces-deploy-1');
    expect(body().emailMessage).toContain(
      'https://oauth-openshift.apps.prod1.ecs.dyn.example.net/oauth/token/request',
    );
  });

  it('greets by soeid when the directory does not know the person', async () => {
    const { notifier, body } = setup();
    await notifier.namespaceCreated({ soeid: 'pp1', namespace: 'n' });
    expect(body().emailMessage).toContain('Dear User PP1,');
  });

  it('sends the already-exists and deleted emails', async () => {
    const a = setup();
    await a.notifier.namespaceAlreadyExists({ soeid: 'pp1', newNamespace: 'new', existingNamespace: 'old' });
    expect(a.body().subject).toBe('Important: Your DevSpaces Deployment Project Already Exists');
    expect(a.body().emailMessage).toContain('oc project old');

    const d = setup();
    await d.notifier.namespacesDeleted({ soeid: 'pp1', namespaces: ['a', 'b'] });
    expect(d.body().subject).toBe('Confirmation: DevSpaces Deployment Project(s) Deleted');
    expect(d.body().emailMessage).toContain('<code>a</code>');
  });

  it('sends nothing for an empty deletion list', async () => {
    const { notifier, calls } = setup();
    await notifier.namespacesDeleted({ soeid: 'pp1', namespaces: [] });
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('is disabled when no mail URL is configured', async () => {
    const { notifier, calls } = setup({ cfg: { url: undefined } as never });
    await notifier.namespaceCreated({ soeid: 'pp1', namespace: 'n' });
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('never throws: logs a failed send and a non-2xx answer', async () => {
    const boom = setup({ fail: true });
    await expect(boom.notifier.namespaceCreated({ soeid: 'pp1', namespace: 'n' })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(boom.logs).toContainEqual(
      expect.objectContaining({ event: 'email.failed', error: 'network down' }),
    );

    const bad = setup({ status: 500 });
    await expect(bad.notifier.namespaceCreated({ soeid: 'pp1', namespace: 'n' })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(bad.logs).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({ event: 'email.failed' }));
  });

  it('never logs the mail credentials', async () => {
    const { notifier, logs } = setup({ fail: true });
    await notifier.namespaceCreated({ soeid: 'pp1', namespace: 'n' });
    expect(JSON.stringify(logs)).not.toContain('pw');
  });
});

        firstName: evil,
        soeid: 'x',
        newNamespace: evil,
        existingNamespace: evil,

        recipient: evil,

      deletedNamespacesEmailBody({ firstName: evil, soeid: 'x', namespaces: [evil], recipient: evil }),

      expect(html).toContain('&lt;script&gt;');

    });

describe('GraphqlUserDirectory', () => {
  const ok =
    (data: unknown): FetchFn =>
    async () =>
      new Response(JSON.stringify(data), { status: 200 });

  it('returns the first name using GraphQL variables (no string interpolation)', async () => {
    let sent: { query: string; variables: { id: string } } | undefined;
    const dir = new GraphqlUserDirectory('http://dir.test', undefined, async (_u, init) => {
      sent = JSON.parse(String(init.body));
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ data: { users: [{ firstName: 'Pat' }] } }), { status: 200 });
    });
    expect(await dir.firstName('pp1')).toBe('Pat');
    expect(sent?.variables.id).toBe('PP1');
    expect(sent?.query).not.toContain('PP1');
  });

  it.each([
    ['no users', { data: { users: [] } }],
    ['no data', {}],
    ['blank name', { data: { users: [{ firstName: '' }] } }],
  ])('returns undefined for %s', async (_l, payload) => {
    expect(
      await new GraphqlUserDirectory('http://d', undefined, ok(payload)).firstName('pp1'),
    ).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('returns undefined (and warns) on errors', async () => {
    const logs: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
    const logger = createLogger('debug', (l) => logs.push(JSON.parse(l)));
    const down = new GraphqlUserDirectory('http://d', logger, async () => {
      throw new Error('boom');
    });
    expect(await down.firstName('pp1')).toBeUndefined();
    const bad = new GraphqlUserDirectory('http://d', logger, async () => new Response('', { status: 500 }));
    expect(await bad.firstName('pp1')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(logs.filter((l) => l.event === 'userdirectory.failed')).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('NullUserDirectory knows nobody', async () => {
    expect(await new NullUserDirectory().firstName()).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

import { CoreV1Api, KubeConfig, KubernetesObjectApi } from '@kubernetes/client-node';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { KubeClient, loadKubeConfig } from '../../../src/infra/kube/KubeClient.js';
import { ConflictError, ForbiddenError, NotFoundError, UpstreamError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';

const apiError = (code: number) =>
  Object.assign(new Error(`HTTP ${code}`), { code, body: { code, message: 'k8s says no' } });

function client(): KubeClient {
  const kc = new KubeConfig();
  kc.loadFromOptions({
    clusters: [{ name: 'c', server: 'https://localhost:6443', skipTLSVerify: true }],
    users: [{ name: 'u', token: 'test-token' }],
    contexts: [{ name: 'x', cluster: 'c', user: 'u' }],
    currentContext: 'x',
  });
  return new KubeClient(kc);
}

describe('namespaces', () => {
  it('lists with a label selector', async () => {
    const spy = vi
      .spyOn(CoreV1Api.prototype, 'listNamespace')
      .mockResolvedValue({ items: [{ metadata: { name: 'a' } }] } as never);
    expect(await client().listNamespaces('k=v')).toEqual([{ metadata: { name: 'a' } }]);
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ labelSelector: 'k=v' });
  });

  it('getNamespace returns null on 404 and translates other errors', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(CoreV1Api.prototype, 'readNamespace');
    spy.mockRejectedValueOnce(apiError(404));
    expect(await client().getNamespace('x')).toBeNull();
    spy.mockRejectedValueOnce(apiError(403));
    await expect(client().getNamespace('x')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
    spy.mockResolvedValueOnce({ metadata: { name: 'x' } } as never);
    expect(await client().getNamespace('x')).toEqual({ metadata: { name: 'x' } });
  });

  it('createNamespace passes the body and maps conflicts', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(CoreV1Api.prototype, 'createNamespace');
    spy.mockResolvedValueOnce({ metadata: { name: 'n' } } as never);
    await client().createNamespace({ metadata: { name: 'n' } });
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ body: { metadata: { name: 'n' } } });
    spy.mockRejectedValueOnce(apiError(409));
    await expect(client().createNamespace({ metadata: { name: 'n' } })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
  });

  it('deleteNamespace is false when already gone', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(CoreV1Api.prototype, 'deleteNamespace');
    spy.mockResolvedValueOnce({} as never);
    expect(await client().deleteNamespace('n')).toBe(true);
    spy.mockRejectedValueOnce(apiError(404));
    expect(await client().deleteNamespace('n')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('ping calls the API discovery endpoint and maps failures', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(CoreV1Api.prototype, 'getAPIResources');
    spy.mockResolvedValueOnce({} as never);
    await expect(client().ping()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    spy.mockRejectedValueOnce(apiError(500));
    await expect(client().ping()).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
  });

    spy.mockRejectedValueOnce(apiError(404));
    expect(await client().delete('Secret', 'ns', 's')).toBe(false);
  });

      .spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'patch')
      .mockResolvedValue({ spec: { started: false } } as never);
    await client().setWorkspaceStarted('ns', 'ws', false);
    expect(spy.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'DevWorkspace',
      metadata: { name: 'ws', namespace: 'ns' },
      spec: { started: false },
    });
    expect(spy.mock.calls[0]?.[5]).toBe('application/merge-patch+json');
  });

  it('wraps unexpected failures as UpstreamError', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'create').mockRejectedValue(apiError(500));
    await expect(client().create('Secret', 'ns', { metadata: { name: 's' } })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
      UpstreamError,
    );
  });
});

describe('apply', () => {
  it('creates when nothing exists', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'read').mockRejectedValue(apiError(404));
    const create = vi
      .spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'create')
      .mockResolvedValue({ metadata: { name: 'q' } } as never);
    await client().apply('ResourceQuota', 'ns', { metadata: { name: 'q' } });
    expect(create).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('replaces with the existing resourceVersion when the object exists', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'read').mockResolvedValue({
      metadata: { name: 'q', resourceVersion: '77' },
    } as never);
    const replace = vi
      .spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'replace')
      .mockResolvedValue({ metadata: { name: 'q' } } as never);
    const create = vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'create');
    await client().apply('ResourceQuota', 'ns', { metadata: { name: 'q' } });
    expect(create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(replace.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toMatchObject({
      metadata: { name: 'q', namespace: 'ns', resourceVersion: '77' },
    });
  });

  it('recovers from losing a creation race by replacing the winner’s object', async () => {
    const read = vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'read');
    read.mockRejectedValueOnce(apiError(404));
    read.mockResolvedValueOnce({ metadata: { name: 'q', resourceVersion: '5' } } as never);
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'create').mockRejectedValue(apiError(409));
    const replace = vi
      .spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'replace')
      .mockResolvedValue({ metadata: { name: 'q' } } as never);
    await client().apply('ResourceQuota', 'ns', { metadata: { name: 'q' } });
    expect(replace).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('gives up with a Conflict when the race cannot be resolved', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'read').mockRejectedValue(apiError(404));
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'create').mockRejectedValue(apiError(409));
    await expect(client().apply('ResourceQuota', 'ns', { metadata: { name: 'q' } })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
      ConflictError,
    );
  });

  it('surfaces other creation errors', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'read').mockRejectedValue(apiError(404));
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'create').mockRejectedValue(apiError(403));
    await expect(client().apply('Secret', 'ns', { metadata: { name: 's' } })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
      ForbiddenError,
    );
  });
});

describe('loadKubeConfig', () => {
  it('fails clearly for a missing kubeconfig file', () => {
    expect(() => loadKubeConfig('/no/such/kubeconfig')).toThrow();
  });
  it('loads default config otherwise without throwing', () => {
    expect(() => loadKubeConfig()).not.toThrow();
  });
  it('NotFoundError is what a translated 404 looks like', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(KubernetesObjectApi.prototype, 'patch').mockRejectedValue(apiError(404));
    await expect(client().setWorkspaceStarted('ns', 'ws', true)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 7950d40a2c576eafd1951a6c482533cc368d6a2e3ce312fc38d56a2cf50d8d56 tests/unit/infra/MetricsRepository.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { AppConfig } from '../../../src/config/env.js';
import type { MetricsRecord } from '../../../src/domain/types.js';
import {
  MetricsRepository,
  NoopMetricsStore,
  assertSafeTableName,
  candidateColumns,
  createPgPool,
  formatUtc,
  parseTableName,
} from '../../../src/infra/db/MetricsRepository.js';
import { buildSslOptions, resolveCaPath } from '../../../src/infra/db/pg-ssl.js';
import { createLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';

type PgConfig = AppConfig['postgres'];

const record: MetricsRecord = {
  timestamp: new Date('2026-01-02T03:04:05.678Z'),
  csiid: '1001',
  soeid: 'pp1',
  eventtype: 'create',
  resourcename: 'ws',
  resourcetype: 'workspace',
  status: { failed: false, error: '', message: '' },
  useraction: 'user',
  limitcpu: 2,
  limitmemory: 4,
  requestcpu: 0.5,
  requestmemory: 1,
  duration: '0 seconds',
  storage: 3,
  workspaceversion: '2.0.0',
  editorname: 'vscode',
};

const ALL = [
  'id',
  'timestamp',
  'csiid',
  'soeid',
  'eventtype',
  'resourcename',
  'resourcetype',
  'status',
  'limitcpu',
  'limitmemory',
  'requestcpu',
  'requestmemory',
  'duration',
  'storage',
  'useraction',
  'workspaceversion',
  'editorname',
  'errordetail',
];

/** The shape the ORIGINAL service wrote (no workspaceversion / editorname). */
const LEGACY = ALL.filter((c) => c !== 'workspaceversion' && c !== 'editorname');

type Path = 'ok' | 'empty' | 'error';

/** `catalog` = information_schema lookup, `select` = `SELECT * … LIMIT 0` metadata lookup. */
function fakeDb(
  columns: string[] | Error | 'none',
  paths: { catalog: Path; select: Path } = { catalog: 'ok', select: 'ok' },
) {
  const queries: { sql: string; values?: unknown[] }[] = [];
  const names = Array.isArray(columns) ? columns : [];
  const query = vi.fn(async (sql: string, values?: unknown[]) => {
    queries.push({ sql, values });
    if (sql.includes('information_schema.columns')) {
      if (columns instanceof Error || paths.catalog === 'error') throw new Error('permission denied');
      return { rows: paths.catalog === 'ok' ? names.map((c) => ({ column_name: c })) : [] };
    }
    if (/LIMIT 0$/.test(sql)) {
      if (columns instanceof Error || paths.select === 'error') throw new Error('permission denied');
      return { rows: [], fields: paths.select === 'ok' ? names.map((name) => ({ name })) : [] };
    }
    return {};
  });
  const insertQuery = () => queries.find((q) => q.sql.startsWith('INSERT'));
  return { db: { query, end: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) }, queries, insertQuery };
}

function logs() {
  const lines: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  return { lines, logger: createLogger('debug', (l) => lines.push(JSON.parse(l))) };
}

describe('MetricsRepository.insert', () => {
  it('inserts every field into a table that has all columns, fully parameterised', async () => {
    const { db, insertQuery } = fakeDb(ALL);
    await new MetricsRepository(db).insert({ ...record, resourcename: "x'); DROP TABLE y;--" });
    const q = insertQuery();
    expect(q?.sql).toMatch(/^INSERT INTO metrics\."devspaces-metrics" \("timestamp", "csiid"/);
    expect(q?.sql).not.toContain('DROP TABLE');
    expect(q?.values).toHaveLength(17);
    expect(q?.values).toContain("x'); DROP TABLE y;--");
  });

  it('writes the timestamp as UTC wall-clock text, like the original service', async () => {
    const { db, insertQuery } = fakeDb(ALL);
    await new MetricsRepository(db).insert(record);
    expect(insertQuery()?.values?.[0]).toBe('2026-01-02 03:04:05.678');
    expect(formatUtc(record.timestamp)).toBe('2026-01-02 03:04:05.678');
  });

  it('adapts to a table that predates some columns (legacy shape: no workspaceversion/editorname)', async () => {
    const { db, insertQuery } = fakeDb(LEGACY);
    const { lines, logger } = logs();
    const repo = new MetricsRepository(db, 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"', logger);
    await repo.insert(record);
    await repo.insert(record);
    const sql = insertQuery()?.sql ?? '';
    expect(sql).toContain('INSERT INTO metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"');
    expect(sql).not.toContain('workspaceversion');
    expect(sql).not.toContain('editorname');
    expect(sql).toContain('"errordetail"');
    // told once, not on every insert
    expect(lines.filter((l) => l.event === 'metrics.columns_missing')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(lines.find((l) => l.event === 'metrics.columns_missing')?.missing).toBe(
      'workspaceversion,editorname',
    );
  });

  it('looks the columns up once per process, not per insert', async () => {
    const { db, queries } = fakeDb(ALL);
    const repo = new MetricsRepository(db);
    await repo.insert(record);
    await repo.insert(record);
    await repo.insert(record);
    expect(queries.filter((q) => q.sql.includes('information_schema'))).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(queries.filter((q) => q.sql.startsWith('INSERT'))).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it('matches the table by schema and unquoted name', async () => {
    const { db, queries } = fakeDb(ALL);
    await new MetricsRepository(db, 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"').insert(record);
    expect(queries[0]?.values).toEqual(['metrics', 'devspaces-metrics-dev']);
  });

  it('looks in the current schema when the table name is unqualified', async () => {
    const { db, queries } = fakeDb(ALL);
    await new MetricsRepository(db, 'devspaces_metrics').insert(record);
    expect(queries[0]?.sql).toContain('current_schema()');
    expect(queries[0]?.values).toEqual(['devspaces_metrics']);
  });

  it('reads the columns from an empty SELECT when the catalog shows nothing (restricted information_schema)', async () => {
    const { db, insertQuery } = fakeDb(LEGACY, { catalog: 'empty', select: 'ok' });
    const { lines, logger } = logs();
    await new MetricsRepository(db, 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"', logger).insert(record);
    expect(insertQuery()?.sql).not.toContain('workspaceversion');
    expect(lines.some((l) => l.event === 'metrics.columns_unknown')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('also uses the empty SELECT when the catalog query itself fails', async () => {
    const { db, insertQuery } = fakeDb(LEGACY, { catalog: 'error', select: 'ok' });
    await new MetricsRepository(db).insert(record);
    expect(insertQuery()?.sql).not.toContain('editorname');
  });

  it('uses the catalog without needing SELECT rights on the table', async () => {
    const { db, queries, insertQuery } = fakeDb(LEGACY, { catalog: 'ok', select: 'error' });
    await new MetricsRepository(db).insert(record);
    expect(queries.some((q) => /LIMIT 0$/.test(q.sql))).toBe(false);
    expect(insertQuery()?.sql).not.toContain('workspaceversion');
  });

  it('stores the error text in errordetail only for failures', async () => {
    expect(candidateColumns(record).errordetail).toBeNull();
    const failed = { ...record, status: { failed: true, error: 'quota exceeded', message: '' } };
    expect(candidateColumns(failed).errordetail).toBe('quota exceeded');
    expect(candidateColumns(failed).status).toBe('{"failed":true,"error":"quota exceeded","message":""}');
  });

  it('falls back to all fields (and warns) when the column list cannot be read, then retries discovery', async () => {
    const { db, queries } = fakeDb(new Error('permission denied for information_schema'));
    const { lines, logger } = logs();
    const repo = new MetricsRepository(db, undefined, logger);
    await repo.insert(record);
    await repo.insert(record);
    expect(lines.filter((l) => l.event === 'metrics.columns_unknown')).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(String(lines[0]?.errors)).toContain('information_schema');
    expect(queries.filter((q) => q.sql.includes('information_schema'))).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(queries.filter((q) => q.sql.startsWith('INSERT'))[0]?.values).toHaveLength(17);
  });

  it('falls back to all fields (and warns) when the table is not visible at all', async () => {
    const { db, insertQuery } = fakeDb('none');
    const { lines, logger } = logs();
    await new MetricsRepository(db, undefined, logger).insert(record);
    expect(insertQuery()?.values).toHaveLength(17);
    expect(lines.some((l) => l.event === 'metrics.columns_unknown')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('fails clearly when the table shares no column with the service', async () => {
    const { db } = fakeDb(['foo', 'bar']);
    await expect(new MetricsRepository(db).insert(record)).rejects.toThrow(
      /none of the expected metrics columns/,
    );
  });

  it('propagates insert errors to the caller (the recorder decides what to do)', async () => {
    const query = vi.fn(async (sql: string) => {
      if (sql.startsWith('INSERT')) throw new Error('relation does not exist');
      return { rows: ALL.map((c) => ({ column_name: c })) };
    });
    await expect(new MetricsRepository({ query, end: vi.fn() }).insert(record)).rejects.toThrow(
      'relation does not exist',
    );
  });

  it('NEVER issues DDL or anything but INSERT and SELECT', async () => {
    for (const columns of [ALL, LEGACY, new Error('x'), 'none' as const]) {
      const { db, queries } = fakeDb(columns);
      const repo = new MetricsRepository(db, 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"');
      await repo.insert(record);
      await repo.ping();
      for (const { sql } of queries) {
        expect(sql.trim()).toMatch(/^(INSERT|SELECT)\b/i);
        expect(sql).not.toMatch(/\b(CREATE|ALTER|DROP|TRUNCATE|DELETE|UPDATE|GRANT)\b/i);
      }
    }
  });

  it('pings and closes', async () => {
    const { db } = fakeDb(ALL);
    const repo = new MetricsRepository(db);
    await repo.ping();
    await repo.close();
    expect(db.query).toHaveBeenCalledWith('SELECT 1');
    expect(db.end).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('table names', () => {
  it.each([
    'metrics."devspaces-metrics"',
    'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
    'devspaces_metrics',
    '"a-b"."c-d"',
    'public.t',
  ])('accepts %s', (name) => expect(assertSafeTableName(name)).toBe(name));
  it.each(['t; DROP TABLE x', 'a b', '', 'a.b.c', 'a"b', "t'--"])('rejects %j', (name) => {
    expect(() => assertSafeTableName(name)).toThrow(/Unsafe/);
  });
  it('is enforced by the constructor', () => {
    expect(() => new MetricsRepository({ query: vi.fn(), end: vi.fn() }, 'x; drop')).toThrow();
  });
  it('parseTableName', () => {
    expect(parseTableName('metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"')).toEqual({
      schema: 'metrics',
      name: 'devspaces-metrics-dev',
    });
    expect(parseTableName('plain_table')).toEqual({ name: 'plain_table' });
    expect(parseTableName('"a-b"."c-d"')).toEqual({ schema: 'a-b', name: 'c-d' });
  });
});

const pgConfig = (over: Partial<PgConfig> = {}): PgConfig => ({
  enabled: true,
  host: 'NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net',
  port: 1524,
  database: 'orion_dev_metadata',
  user: 'app_orion_pgsql_dev',
  password: 'pw',
  ssl: true,
  env: 'development',
  table: 'metrics."devspaces-metrics-dev"',
  ...over,
});

describe('TLS to the existing database (same rules as the original service)', () => {
  it('picks the CA file from NODE_ENV', () => {
    expect(resolveCaPath(pgConfig({ env: 'development' }), '/work')).toMatch(
      /[\\/]work[\\/]certs[\\/]dev\.pem$/,
    );
    expect(resolveCaPath(pgConfig({ env: 'uat' }))).toMatch(/[\\/]app[\\/]backend[\\/]cert[\\/]uat\.pem$/);
    expect(resolveCaPath(pgConfig({ env: 'production' }))).toMatch(
      /[\\/]app[\\/]backend[\\/]cert[\\/]prod\.pem$/,
    );
  });

  it('falls back to dev.pem for an unknown NODE_ENV, in the container directory', () => {
    expect(resolveCaPath(pgConfig({ env: 'staging' }))).toMatch(
      /[\\/]app[\\/]backend[\\/]cert[\\/]dev\.pem$/,
    );
  });

  it('lets POSTGRES_CERT_DIR and POSTGRES_CA_FILE override the location', () => {
    expect(resolveCaPath(pgConfig({ env: 'uat', certDir: '/mnt/certs' }))).toMatch(
      /[\\/]mnt[\\/]certs[\\/]uat\.pem$/,
    );
    expect(resolveCaPath(pgConfig({ caFile: '/x/custom.pem', certDir: '/ignored' }))).toBe('/x/custom.pem');
  });

  it('development: verifies the certificate but skips the hostname check', () => {
    const ssl = buildSslOptions(pgConfig({ env: 'development' }), () => 'CA-DEV');
    expect(ssl).toMatchObject({ rejectUnauthorized: true, ca: 'CA-DEV' });
    expect((ssl as { checkServerIdentity?: () => unknown }).checkServerIdentity?.()).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('uat/production: full verification including the hostname', () => {
    for (const env of ['uat', 'production']) {
      const ssl = buildSslOptions(pgConfig({ env }), () => 'CA');
      expect(ssl).toEqual({ rejectUnauthorized: true, ca: 'CA' });
    }
  });

  it('can be switched off for a local database', () => {
    expect(buildSslOptions(pgConfig({ ssl: false }), () => 'never read')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('names the file and the fix when the CA cannot be read', () => {
    const read = () => {
      throw new Error('ENOENT');
    };
    expect(() => buildSslOptions(pgConfig({ env: 'uat' }), read)).toThrow(
      /uat\.pem.*NODE_ENV=uat.*POSTGRES_CA_FILE/s,
    );
  });
});

describe('createPgPool', () => {
  it('builds a pool from the environment-style settings without connecting', async () => {
    const pool = createPgPool(pgConfig({ ssl: false }));
    expect(pool.options).toMatchObject({
      host: 'NAORI01DPS_INSTANCE1.pgaas.dyn.nsroot.net',
      port: 1524,
      database: 'orion_dev_metadata',
      user: 'app_orion_pgsql_dev',
      max: 5,
    });
    await pool.end();
  });

  it('survives idle-client errors instead of crashing the process', async () => {
    const { lines, logger } = logs();
    const pool = createPgPool(pgConfig({ ssl: false }), logger);
    expect(() =>
      pool.emit('error', new Error('terminating connection due to administrator command')),
    ).not.toThrow();
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({ event: 'postgres.pool_error' });
    await pool.end();
  });

  it('fails fast when TLS is on but the CA file is missing', () => {
    expect(() => createPgPool(pgConfig({ caFile: '/definitely/not/here.pem' }))).toThrow(/CA certificate/);
  });
});

  it('accepts everything', async () => {
    const s = new NoopMetricsStore();
    await expect(Promise.all([s.insert(), s.ping(), s.close()])).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl f285488c7aa0e5370a3fd8c81543cccea69f4d972d383d29735f930b734fabd3 tests/unit/scripts/bundle-format.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  BUNDLE_MAGIC,
  createBundle,
  isExcluded,
  isSafeRelativePath,
  isTextBuffer,
  parseBundle,
  sha256,
} from '../../../scripts/lib/bundle-format.mjs';

const file = (path: string, content: string | Buffer) => ({
  path,
  content: Buffer.isBuffer(content) ? content : Buffer.from(content),
});

const roundTrip = (files: { path: string; content: Buffer }[]) => parseBundle(createBundle(files));

describe('round trip', () => {
  it('restores text files byte for byte', () => {
    const files = [
      file('src/a.ts', 'export const a = 1;\n'),
      file('no-final-newline.txt', 'abc'),
      file('crlf.txt', 'line1\r\nline2\r\n'),
      file('empty.txt', ''),
      file('unicode.md', 'héllo — 日本語 🚀\n'),
      file('bom.txt', Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([0xef, 0xbb, 0xbf]), Buffer.from('with bom\n')])),
      file('blank-lines.txt', '\n\n\ntext\n\n\n'),
      file('deep/dir/with space/file.json', '{"a":1}'),
    ];
    const { files: out } = roundTrip(files);
    expect(out.map((f) => f.path)).toEqual(files.map((f) => f.path));
    out.forEach((f, i) => expect(f.content.equals(files[i]!.content)).toBe(true));
  });

  it('stores binary files as base64 and restores them exactly', () => {
    const png = Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a, 0x00, 0x00, 0xff, 0xfe, 0x80]);
    const big = Buffer.alloc(10_000, 0xab);
    const bundle = createBundle([file('logo.png', png), file('blob.bin', big)]);
    expect(bundle).toContain('"enc":"base64"');
    const { files } = parseBundle(bundle);
    expect(files[0]?.content.equals(png)).toBe(true);
    expect(files[1]?.content.equals(big)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('is not confused by file content that looks like bundle markers', () => {
    const evil = [
      '@@@ END deadbeefdeadbeefdeadbeef @@@',
      '@@@ FILE deadbeefdeadbeefdeadbeef {"path":"../../etc/passwd"} @@@',
      BUNDLE_MAGIC,
      'boundary: 00000000000000000000000000',
    ].join('\n');
    const { files } = roundTrip([file('evil.txt', evil), file('after.txt', 'still here')]);
    expect(files).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(files[0]?.content.toString()).toBe(evil);
    expect(files[1]?.path).toBe('after.txt');
  });

  it('records generation time and note', () => {
    const text = createBundle([file('a', 'x')], {
      generatedAt: new Date('2026-01-02T03:04:05Z'),
      note: 'line1\nline2',
    });
    const { meta } = parseBundle(text);
    expect(meta).toMatchObject({ generated: '2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z', files: 1, note: 'line1 line2' });
  });

  it('handles a bundle with no files', () => {
    expect(roundTrip([]).files).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('repairs a bundle whose LF line endings were all converted to CRLF, exactly', () => {
    const files = [
      file('lf.txt', 'one\ntwo\n'),
      file('crlf.txt', 'one\r\ntwo\r\n'),
      file('none.txt', 'no newline'),
      file('bin.dat', Buffer.from([0, 13, 10, 255])),
    ];
    const converted = createBundle(files).replace(/\n/g, '\r\n'); // naive converter: every LF → CRLF
    const parsed = parseBundle(converted);
    expect(parsed.meta.lineEndingsRepaired).toBe(true);
    parsed.files.forEach((f, i) => expect(f.content.equals(files[i]!.content), f.path).toBe(true));
  });

  it('does not report a repair for an untouched bundle', () => {
    expect(roundTrip([file('a', 'x\n')]).meta.lineEndingsRepaired).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('refuses rather than guess when the damage cannot be verified', () => {
    // A "smart" converter leaves existing CRLF alone and only fixes bare LF: the CRLF file no longer matches.
    const text = createBundle([file('crlf.txt', 'a\r\nb\r\n')]).replace(/\r?\n/g, '\r\n');
    expect(() => parseBundle(text)).toThrow(/mismatch/);
  });
});

describe('integrity checks', () => {
  const good = createBundle([file('a.txt', 'hello world\n'), file('b.txt', 'second\n')]);

  it('rejects text that is not a bundle', () => {
    expect(() => parseBundle('hello')).toThrow(/Not a workspace-mgr bundle/);
    expect(() => parseBundle('')).toThrow(/Not a workspace-mgr bundle/);
  });

  it('detects tampered content (same length) via checksum', () => {
    expect(() => parseBundle(good.replace('hello world', 'hellO world'))).toThrow(
      /Checksum mismatch for a\.txt/,
    );
  });

  it('detects tampered length', () => {
    expect(() => parseBundle(good.replace('hello world', 'hello'))).toThrow(/Size mismatch for a\.txt/);
  });

  it('detects a truncated bundle', () => {
    expect(() => parseBundle(good.slice(0, good.lastIndexOf('@@@ FILE')))).toThrow(
      /declares 2 files but contains 1/,
    );
    expect(() => parseBundle(good.slice(0, good.length - 40))).toThrow(/Missing end marker for b\.txt/);
  });

  it('detects a missing file count or boundary', () => {
    expect(() => parseBundle(good.replace(/^files: .*$/m, 'files: nope'))).toThrow(/file count/);
    expect(() => parseBundle(good.replace(/^boundary: .*$/m, 'boundary: x'))).toThrow(/boundary/);
  });

  it('rejects unsafe paths even in a hand-crafted bundle', () => {
    for (const bad of ['../outside.txt', '/abs.txt', 'C:/win.txt', 'a/../../b', 'a\\b']) {
      const body = 'x';
      const boundary = 'a'.repeat(24);
      const meta = JSON.stringify({ path: bad, enc: 'utf8', bytes: 1, sha256: sha256(Buffer.from(body)) });
      const text = `${BUNDLE_MAGIC}\ngenerated: now\nboundary: ${boundary}\nfiles: 1\n\n@@@ FILE ${boundary} ${meta} @@@\n${body}\n@@@ END ${boundary} @@@\n`;
      expect(() => parseBundle(text), bad).toThrow(/Unsafe path/);
    }
  });

  it('rejects duplicate paths and unknown encodings', () => {
    const boundary = 'b'.repeat(24);
    const entry = (path: string, enc = 'utf8') =>
      `@@@ FILE ${boundary} ${JSON.stringify({ path, enc, bytes: 1, sha256: sha256(Buffer.from('x')) })} @@@\nx\n@@@ END ${boundary} @@@\n`;
    const head = (n: number) => `${BUNDLE_MAGIC}\ngenerated: now\nboundary: ${boundary}\nfiles: ${n}\n\n`;
    expect(() => parseBundle(head(2) + entry('a') + entry('a'))).toThrow(/Duplicate path/);
    expect(() => parseBundle(head(1) + entry('a', 'rot13'))).toThrow(/Unknown encoding/);
  });

  it('rejects unreadable file headers', () => {
    const boundary = 'c'.repeat(24);
    const text = `${BUNDLE_MAGIC}\ngenerated: now\nboundary: ${boundary}\nfiles: 1\n\n@@@ FILE ${boundary} {not json @@@\nx\n@@@ END ${boundary} @@@\n`;
    expect(() => parseBundle(text)).toThrow(/Unreadable file header/);
    const noEnd = `${BUNDLE_MAGIC}\ngenerated: now\nboundary: ${boundary}\nfiles: 1\n\n@@@ FILE ${boundary} {"path":"a"}`;
    expect(() => parseBundle(noEnd)).toThrow(/Malformed file header/);
  });
});

describe('createBundle guards', () => {
  it('refuses unsafe and duplicate paths', () => {
    expect(() => createBundle([file('../x', 'a')])).toThrow(/unsafe/i);
    expect(() => createBundle([file('a', 'x'), file('a', 'y')])).toThrow(/Duplicate/);
  });
});

describe('helpers', () => {
  it('isSafeRelativePath', () => {
    for (const ok of ['a', 'a/b.txt', 'a/b/c/d.e', '.env.example', 'dir with space/x']) {
      expect(isSafeRelativePath(ok), ok).toBe(true);
    }
    for (const bad of [
      '',
      '.',
      '..',
      '../a',
      'a/..',
      'a/../b',
      './a',
      'a//b',
      '/a',
      '\\a',
      'C:\\a',
      'C:a',
      'a\\b',
      'a\0b',
      42,
      null,
    ]) {
      expect(isSafeRelativePath(bad), String(bad)).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it('isTextBuffer', () => {
    expect(isTextBuffer(Buffer.from('plain'))).toBe(true);
    expect(isTextBuffer(Buffer.from('日本語'))).toBe(true);
    expect(isTextBuffer(Buffer.from([0x61, 0x00, 0x62]))).toBe(false);
    expect(isTextBuffer(Buffer.from([0xff, 0xfe, 0xfd]))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('isExcluded keeps secrets, dependencies and build output out of bundles', () => {
    for (const out of [
      'node_modules/x/index.js',
      'a/node_modules/x',
      'dist/index.js',
      'coverage/lcov.info',
      '.git/config',
      '.env',
      '.env.local',
      '.env.production',
      'workspace-mgr.bundle.txt',
      'other.bundle.txt',
      'npm-debug.log',
      '.DS_Store',
      '.vscode/settings.json',
    ]) {
      expect(isExcluded(out), out).toBe(true);
    }
    for (const kept of [
      'src/index.ts',
      '.env.example',
      'package.json',
      'docs/CHECKLIST.md',
      'legacy/backend/src/t.ts',
      'distillery.ts',
    ]) {
      expect(isExcluded(kept), kept).toBe(false);
    }
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl a1e0c3c43471ea4d922d19a4c565e16915364616aab6f887be4fa8814dfdccca tests/unit/services/DeploymentNamespaceService.test.ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { DeploymentNamespaceService } from '../../../src/services/DeploymentNamespaceService.js';
import { MetricsRecorder } from '../../../src/services/MetricsRecorder.js';
import { UpstreamError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { FakeClock, FakeMetricsStore, FakeNotifier } from '../../helpers/fakes.js';
import { InMemoryKube } from '../../helpers/InMemoryKube.js';

let kube: InMemoryKube;
let metrics: FakeMetricsStore;
let notifier: FakeNotifier;
let service: DeploymentNamespaceService;
const name = 'pp1-devspaces-deploy-1001';

beforeEach(() => {
  kube = new InMemoryKube();
  metrics = new FakeMetricsStore();
  notifier = new FakeNotifier();
  service = new DeploymentNamespaceService(
    kube,
    new MetricsRecorder(metrics, new FakeClock(), silentLogger),
    notifier,
    'devspaces-support-team',
    silentLogger,
  );
});

describe('ensure', () => {
  it('creates the project, quotas, limit range and RBAC, then emails the user', async () => {
    expect(await service.ensure('pp1', '1001')).toEqual({ exists: false, name });

    expect(kube.namespaces.get(name)?.metadata?.annotations).toMatchObject({
      'che.eclipse.org/username': 'pp1',
      'openshift.io/requester': 'devspaces-support-team',
    });
    const keys = [...kube.objects.keys()];
    expect(keys).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining([
        `ResourceQuota/${name}/compute-resources`,
        `ResourceQuota/${name}/storage-quota`,
        `LimitRange/${name}/default-limits`,
        `Role/${name}/devspaces-builder-role`,
        `Role/${name}/devspaces-deployer-role`,
        `RoleBinding/${name}/devspaces-builder-role-pp1-binding`,
        `RoleBinding/${name}/devspaces-deployer-role-pp1-binding`,
      ]),
    );
    expect(kube.objects.get(`RoleBinding/${name}/devspaces-builder-role-pp1-binding`)?.subjects).toEqual([
      { kind: 'User', name: 'pp1', apiGroup: 'rbac.authorization.k8s.io' },
      { kind: 'ServiceAccount', name: 'default', namespace: name },
    ]);
    expect(notifier.created).toEqual([{ soeid: 'pp1', namespace: name }]);
    expect(metrics.records[0]).toMatchObject({
      eventtype: 'create',
      resourcetype: 'deployment_namespace',
      resourcename: name,
      csiid: '1001',
    });
  });

  it('uses the name the platform actually gave the project', async () => {
    const original = kube.createProject.bind(kube);
    kube.createProject = async (body) => {
      const created = await original(body);
      return { ...created, metadata: { ...created.metadata, name: created.metadata?.name } };
    };
    expect((await service.ensure('pp1', '1001')).name).toBe(name);
  });

  it('returns the existing project and emails an “already exists” notice', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace(name, 'pp1');
    expect(await service.ensure('pp1', '1001')).toEqual({ exists: true, name });
    expect(notifier.existing).toEqual([{ soeid: 'pp1', newNamespace: name, existingNamespace: name }]);
    expect(notifier.created).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(kube.calls).not.toContain('createProject');
  });

  it('records the failure, sends no email, and rethrows when provisioning fails', async () => {
    kube.failOnce('apply:ResourceQuota', new UpstreamError('quota denied'));
    await expect(service.ensure('pp1', '1001')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
    expect(notifier.created).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(metrics.records[0]?.status).toMatchObject({ failed: true, error: 'quota denied' });
  });
});

describe('list', () => {
  it('matches only the user’s deployment namespaces', async () => {
    for (const n of [
      'pp1-devspaces-deploy-1',
      'pp1-devspaces-deploy-22',

describe('delete', () => {
  it('reports nothing to do when the user has no deployment namespaces', async () => {
    expect(await service.delete('pp1')).toEqual({
      exists: false,
      deleted: [],
      failed: [],

    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-deploy-2', 'pp1');
    const result = await service.delete('pp1');

    expect(notifier.deleted[0]?.namespaces).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('keeps going after a failure and reports both outcomes', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-deploy-1', 'pp1');
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-deploy-2', 'pp1');
    kube.failOnce('deleteNamespace', new UpstreamError('finalizer stuck'));
    const result = await service.delete('pp1');
    expect(result.deleted).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result.failed).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result.message).toMatch(/Deleted 1.*Failed to delete 1/);
    expect(metrics.records.filter((r) => r.status.failed)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('sends no email when nothing was deleted', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-deploy-1', 'pp1');
    kube.failOnce('deleteNamespace', new UpstreamError('nope'));
    const result = await service.delete('pp1');
    expect(result.exists).toBe(false);
    expect(notifier.deleted).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
