@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl cf38e80396f33038c133fd705f992a650c5fc72e6441bdb71f496ab09a5c5489 tests/unit/services/GitConfigService.test.ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { GitConfigService, GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP } from '../../../src/services/GitConfigService.js';
import { MetricsRecorder } from '../../../src/services/MetricsRecorder.js';
import { NamespaceService } from '../../../src/services/NamespaceService.js';
import { BadRequestError, NotFoundError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { FakeClock, FakeMetricsStore } from '../../helpers/fakes.js';
import { InMemoryKube } from '../../helpers/InMemoryKube.js';

let kube: InMemoryKube;
let metrics: FakeMetricsStore;
let service: GitConfigService;
const ns = 'pp1-devspaces-1001';
const content = '[user]\n  name = Pat\n  email = pat@example.com\n';

beforeEach(() => {
  kube = new InMemoryKube();
  metrics = new FakeMetricsStore();
  const recorder = new MetricsRecorder(metrics, new FakeClock(), silentLogger);
  service = new GitConfigService(kube, new NamespaceService(kube, recorder, { emailDomain: 'x' }), recorder);
  kube.seedNamespace(ns, 'pp1', '1001');
});

describe('GitConfigService', () => {
  it('creates a ConfigMap mounted at /home/user/.gitconfig', async () => {
    const file = await service.upsert('pp1', '1001', content);
    expect(file).toEqual({ name: GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP, namespace: ns, content });
    const cm = kube.objects.get(`ConfigMap/${ns}/${GITCONFIG_CONFIGMAP}`);
    expect(cm?.data).toEqual({ '.gitconfig': content });
    expect(cm?.metadata?.annotations).toMatchObject({
      'controller.devfile.io/mount-as': 'subpath',
      'controller.devfile.io/mount-path': '/home/user',
    });
    expect(cm?.metadata?.labels).toMatchObject({ 'controller.devfile.io/mount-to-devworkspace': 'true' });
  });

  it('records create the first time and update afterwards', async () => {
    await service.upsert('pp1', '1001', content);
    await service.upsert('pp1', '1001', `${content}[core]\n  autocrlf = false\n`);
    expect(metrics.records.map((r) => r.eventtype)).toEqual(['create', 'update']);
    expect(metrics.records.every((r) => r.resourcetype === 'gitconfig')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('views the stored content', async () => {
    await service.upsert('pp1', '1001', content);
    expect((await service.view('pp1', '1001')).content).toBe(content);
  });

  it('404s when there is no git config', async () => {
    await expect(service.view('pp1', '1001')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });

  it('rejects empty or oversized content without touching the cluster', async () => {
    await expect(service.upsert('pp1', '1001', '   \n')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(BadRequestError);
    await expect(service.upsert('pp1', '1001', 'x'.repeat(65 * 1024))).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
      BadRequestError,
    );
    expect(kube.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('needs an existing project', async () => {
    await expect(service.upsert('pp1', '9999', content)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });

  it('deletes, reporting whether anything was there', async () => {
    await service.upsert('pp1', '1001', content);
    expect(await service.delete('pp1', '1001')).toBe(true);
    expect(await service.delete('pp1', '1001')).toBe(false);
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl cd03a7146f9ed98fbb65ef77efe5ec6cef4310ec0a0ae6d928c2e72a7d23807f tests/unit/services/MetricsRecorder.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { MetricsRecorder } from '../../../src/services/MetricsRecorder.js';
import { createLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { FakeClock, FakeMetricsStore } from '../../helpers/fakes.js';

const base = { soeid: 'pp1', eventtype: 'create', resourcetype: 'workspace' } as const;

function setup(timeoutMs = 50) {
  const store = new FakeMetricsStore();
  const logs: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  const logger = createLogger('debug', (l) => logs.push(JSON.parse(l)));
  const clock = new FakeClock();
  return { store, logs, clock, recorder: new MetricsRecorder(store, clock, logger, timeoutMs) };
}

describe('MetricsRecorder.record', () => {
  it('stores a record stamped by the clock', async () => {
    const { store, recorder, clock } = setup();
    await recorder.record({ ...base, resourcename: 'ws1' });
    expect(store.records).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(store.records[0]).toMatchObject({ soeid: 'pp1', resourcename: 'ws1', timestamp: clock.now() });
  });

  it('never throws when the database fails, and logs it', async () => {
    const { store, recorder, logs } = setup();
    store.failWith = new Error('connection refused');
    await expect(recorder.record(base)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(logs).toContainEqual(
      expect.objectContaining({ event: 'metrics.insert_failed', error: 'connection refused' }),
    );
  });

  it('gives up on a hung database after the timeout', async () => {
    const { store, recorder, logs } = setup(20);
    store.delayMs = 500;
    const started = Date.now();
    await recorder.record(base);
    expect(Date.now() - started).toBeLessThan(400);
    expect(logs.some((l) => String(l.error).includes('timed out'))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('MetricsRecorder.track', () => {
  it('records success and returns the result', async () => {
    const { store, recorder } = setup();
    const result = await recorder.track(
      base,
      async () => 42,
      (n) => ({ storage: n }),
    );
    expect(result).toBe(42);
    expect(store.records[0]).toMatchObject({ storage: 42, status: { failed: false } });
  });

  it('records failure with the error text, then rethrows the original error', async () => {
    const { store, recorder } = setup();
    const boom = new Error('quota exceeded');
    await expect(recorder.track(base, async () => Promise.reject(boom))).rejects.toBe(boom);
    expect(store.records[0]?.status).toEqual({ failed: true, error: 'quota exceeded', message: '' });
  });

  it('still returns the result when recording fails', async () => {
    const { store, recorder } = setup();
    store.failWith = new Error('db down');
    await expect(recorder.track(base, async () => 'ok')).resolves.toBe('ok');
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl a9c2b2141163d299681d6350a45906c4a5c46799d68105db157459c68b95d829 tests/unit/services/NamespaceService.test.ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { NamespaceService } from '../../../src/services/NamespaceService.js';
import { MetricsRecorder } from '../../../src/services/MetricsRecorder.js';
import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, UpstreamError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { FakeClock, FakeMetricsStore } from '../../helpers/fakes.js';
import { InMemoryKube } from '../../helpers/InMemoryKube.js';

let kube: InMemoryKube;
let metrics: FakeMetricsStore;

const make = (proxy?: string) =>
  new NamespaceService(
    kube,
    new MetricsRecorder(metrics, new FakeClock(), silentLogger),
    { workspaceProxyUrl: proxy, emailDomain: 'example.com' },
    silentLogger,
  );

beforeEach(() => {
  kube = new InMemoryKube();
  metrics = new FakeMetricsStore();
});

describe('ensure', () => {
  it('creates and provisions a namespace that does not exist', async () => {
    const result = await make().ensure('pp1', '1001');
    expect(result).toEqual({ name: 'pp1-devspaces-1001', created: true });

    const ns = kube.namespaces.get('pp1-devspaces-1001');
    expect(ns?.metadata?.labels).toMatchObject({ 'app.kubernetes.io/component': 'workspaces-namespace' });
    expect(ns?.metadata?.annotations).toEqual({ 'che.eclipse.org/username': 'pp1' });

    const kinds = [...kube.objects.keys()].map((k) => k.split('/')[0]);
    expect(kinds).toContain('Role');
    expect(kinds).toContain('RoleBinding');
    expect(kinds).toContain('ResourceQuota');
    expect(kinds).toContain('LimitRange');
    expect(kinds.filter((k) => k === 'NetworkPolicy')).toHaveLength(5);
  });

  it('binds the user to the user role', async () => {
    await make().ensure('pp1', '1001');
    const binding = kube.objects.get('RoleBinding/pp1-devspaces-1001/devspaces-project-access-pp1');
    expect(binding).toMatchObject({
      subjects: [{ kind: 'User', name: 'pp1' }],
      roleRef: { kind: 'Role', name: 'devspaces-user-role' },
    });
  });

  it('records a successful project create', async () => {
    await make().ensure('pp1', '1001');
    expect(metrics.records).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(metrics.records[0]).toMatchObject({
      eventtype: 'create',
      resourcetype: 'project',
      resourcename: 'pp1-devspaces-1001',
      csiid: '1001',
    });
  });

  it('returns an existing namespace without touching it', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'pp1', '1001');
    const result = await make().ensure('pp1', '1001');
    expect(result).toEqual({ name: 'pp1-devspaces-1001', created: false });
    expect(kube.calls).not.toContain('createNamespace');
    expect(metrics.records).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('refuses a namespace that belongs to someone else', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'intruder', '1001');
    await expect(make().ensure('pp1', '1001')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
  });

  it('adopts the namespace when it loses a creation race', async () => {
    const service = make();
    const create = kube.createNamespace.bind(kube);
    kube.createNamespace = async (body) => {
      kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'pp1', '1001');
      return create(body);
    };
    expect(await service.ensure('pp1', '1001')).toEqual({ name: 'pp1-devspaces-1001', created: false });
    expect(kube.objects.size).toBe(0); // the winner provisions; we did not
  });

  it('rolls the namespace back and records the failure when provisioning fails', async () => {
    kube.failOnce('apply:ResourceQuota', new UpstreamError('quota api down'));
    await expect(make().ensure('pp1', '1001')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
    expect(kube.namespaces.has('pp1-devspaces-1001')).toBe(false);
    expect(metrics.records[0]?.status).toMatchObject({ failed: true, error: 'quota api down' });
  });

  it('propagates a namespace creation failure that is not a race', async () => {
    kube.failOnce('createNamespace', new UpstreamError('api down'));
    await expect(make().ensure('pp1', '1001')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
  });

  it('adds proxy and git ConfigMaps when a proxy is configured', async () => {
    await make('http://proxy.example:8080').ensure('pp1', '1001');
    const env = kube.objects.get('ConfigMap/pp1-devspaces-1001/proxy-env-vars-ws-mgr');
    expect(env?.data).toMatchObject({
      HTTP_PROXY: 'http://proxy.example:8080',
      https_proxy: 'http://proxy.example:8080',
    });
    const git = kube.objects.get(
      'ConfigMap/pp1-devspaces-1001/workspace-userdata-gitconfig-configmap-ws-mgr',
    );
    expect(String((git?.data as Record<string, string>).gitconfig)).toContain('email = pp1@example.com');
  });

  it('adds no ConfigMaps without a proxy', async () => {
    await make().ensure('pp1', '1001');
    expect([...kube.objects.keys()].some((k) => k.startsWith('ConfigMap/'))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('require', () => {
  it('returns the name of an existing namespace', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'pp1');
    expect(await make().require('pp1', '1001')).toBe('pp1-devspaces-1001');
  });
  it('throws NotFound when missing and never creates', async () => {
    await expect(make().require('pp1', '1001')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
    expect(kube.namespaces.size).toBe(0);
  });
  it('throws Conflict for another user’s namespace', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1001', 'other');
    await expect(make().require('pp1', '1001')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
  });
});

describe('listForUser / listProjects', () => {
  it('returns only the user’s workspace namespaces', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1', 'pp1', '1');
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-2', 'pp1', '2');
    kube.seedNamespace('pp2-devspaces-1', 'pp2', '1');
    kube.namespaces.set('pp1-unrelated', {
      metadata: { name: 'pp1-unrelated', annotations: { 'che.eclipse.org/username': 'pp1' } },
    });

    const list = await make().listForUser('pp1');
    expect(list.map((n) => n.metadata?.name).sort()).toEqual(['pp1-devspaces-1', 'pp1-devspaces-2']);
  });

  it('describes projects with csiid and creation time', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-7', 'pp1', '7');
    const [project] = await make().listProjects('pp1');
    expect(project).toMatchObject({ name: 'pp1-devspaces-7', csiid: '7', soeid: 'pp1' });
    expect(project?.createdAt).toMatch(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/);
  });

  it('derives the csiid from the name when the label is missing', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-7', 'pp1', '7');
    const ns = kube.namespaces.get('pp1-devspaces-7');
    delete ns?.metadata?.labels?.['devspaces.citi.com/csiid'];
    expect((await make().listProjects('pp1'))[0]?.csiid).toBe('7');
  });
});

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { KubeObject } from '../../../src/domain/types.js';
import { MetricsRecorder } from '../../../src/services/MetricsRecorder.js';
import { NamespaceService } from '../../../src/services/NamespaceService.js';
import { PatService, assertValidGithubToken, maskToken } from '../../../src/services/PatService.js';
import { BadRequestError, ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { FakeClock, FakeMetricsStore } from '../../helpers/fakes.js';
import { InMemoryKube } from '../../helpers/InMemoryKube.js';
import { SAMPLE_PAT } from '../../helpers/sample-devfile.js';

let kube: InMemoryKube;
let metrics: FakeMetricsStore;
let service: PatService;
const ns = 'pp1-devspaces-1001';

beforeEach(() => {
  kube = new InMemoryKube();
  metrics = new FakeMetricsStore();
  const recorder = new MetricsRecorder(metrics, new FakeClock(), silentLogger);
  const namespaces = new NamespaceService(kube, recorder, { emailDomain: 'example.com' }, silentLogger);
  service = new PatService(kube, namespaces, recorder, { url: 'https://github.com', userSuffix: '_citi' });
  kube.seedNamespace(ns, 'pp1', '1001');
});

const secret = (name: string) =>
  kube.objects.get(`Secret/${ns}/git-credentials-secret-${name}`) as KubeObject;
const decoded = (name: string) =>
  Buffer.from((secret(name).data as Record<string, string>).credentials ?? '', 'base64').toString();

describe('token validation', () => {
  it('accepts a classic PAT', () => expect(() => assertValidGithubToken(SAMPLE_PAT)).not.toThrow());
  it.each(['', 'ghp_short', `gho_${'a'.repeat(36)}`, `ghp_${'a'.repeat(37)}`, `ghp_${'!'.repeat(36)}`])(
    'rejects %j',
    (token) => expect(() => assertValidGithubToken(token)).toThrow(BadRequestError),
  );
  it('masks tokens', () => {
    expect(maskToken(SAMPLE_PAT)).toBe(`gh…${SAMPLE_PAT.slice(-3)}`);
    expect(maskToken('abc')).toBe('***');
  });
});

describe('create', () => {
  it('stores credentials the way Dev Spaces mounts them', async () => {
    const summary = await service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT);
    expect(summary).toMatchObject({ namespace: ns, name: 'work' });
    expect(decoded('work')).toBe(`https://pp1_citi:${SAMPLE_PAT}@github.com`);
    expect(secret('work').metadata?.labels).toMatchObject({ 'controller.devfile.io/git-credential': 'true' });
    expect(secret('work').metadata?.annotations).toMatchObject({
      'che.eclipse.org/mount-path': '/.git-credentials',
    });
  });

  it('uses the plain soeid when no suffix is configured', async () => {
    const recorder = new MetricsRecorder(metrics, new FakeClock(), silentLogger);
    const plain = new PatService(kube, new NamespaceService(kube, recorder, { emailDomain: 'x' }), recorder, {
      url: 'https://ghe.example.com',
      userSuffix: '',
    });
    await plain.create('pp1', '1001', 'ghe', SAMPLE_PAT);
    expect(decoded('ghe')).toBe(`https://pp1:${SAMPLE_PAT}@ghe.example.com`);
  });

  it('records a create metric that never contains the token', async () => {
    await service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT);
    expect(metrics.records[0]).toMatchObject({
      eventtype: 'create',
      resourcetype: 'pat',
      resourcename: 'git-credentials-secret-work',
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(metrics.records)).not.toContain(SAMPLE_PAT);
  });

  it('conflicts when the name is taken', async () => {
    await service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT);
    await expect(service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
  });

  it('validates before touching the cluster', async () => {
    await expect(service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', 'bad')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(BadRequestError);
    expect(kube.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('needs an existing project', async () => {
    await expect(service.create('pp1', '9999', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });
});

describe('view', () => {
  it('returns the token masked, never in full', async () => {
    await service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT);
    const view = await service.view('pp1', '1001', 'work');
    expect(view.token).toBe(maskToken(SAMPLE_PAT));
    expect(JSON.stringify(view)).not.toContain(SAMPLE_PAT);
    expect(view).toMatchObject({ name: 'work', namespace: ns, scmUrl: 'https://github.com' });
  });
  it('flags unrecognised secret contents', async () => {
    await service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT);
    (secret('work').data as Record<string, string>).credentials = Buffer.from('garbage').toString('base64');
    expect((await service.view('pp1', '1001', 'work')).token).toBe('[unrecognized format]');
  });
  it('404s for an unknown token', async () => {
    await expect(service.view('pp1', '1001', 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });
});

describe('update', () => {
  it('replaces the token', async () => {
    await service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT);
    const next = `ghp_${'Z'.repeat(36)}`;
    await service.update('pp1', '1001', 'work', next);
    expect(decoded('work')).toContain(next);
    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ eventtype: 'update', resourcetype: 'pat' });
  });
  it('404s for an unknown token and rejects bad tokens', async () => {
    await expect(service.update('pp1', '1001', 'nope', SAMPLE_PAT)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
    await expect(service.update('pp1', '1001', 'nope', 'bad')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(BadRequestError);
  });
});

describe('delete', () => {
  it('deletes and reports true', async () => {
    await service.create('pp1', '1001', 'work', SAMPLE_PAT);
    expect(await service.delete('pp1', '1001', 'work')).toBe(true);
    expect(kube.objects.size).toBe(0);
    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ eventtype: 'delete', resourcetype: 'pat' });
  });
  it('is a success when already gone (false)', async () => {
    expect(await service.delete('pp1', '1001', 'ghost')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('list', () => {
  it('lists the user’s PAT secrets across namespaces, ignoring other secrets', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace('pp1-devspaces-1002', 'pp1', '1002');
    await service.create('pp1', '1001', 'one', SAMPLE_PAT);
    await service.create('pp1', '1002', 'two', SAMPLE_PAT);
    await kube.create('Secret', ns, {
      metadata: { name: 'other-secret', labels: { 'controller.devfile.io/git-credential': 'true' } },
    });
    await kube.create('Secret', ns, { metadata: { name: 'git-credentials-secret-unlabelled' } });
    const list = await service.list('pp1');
    expect(list.map((p) => `${p.namespace}/${p.name}`).sort()).toEqual([
      'pp1-devspaces-1001/one',
      'pp1-devspaces-1002/two',
    ]);
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 354e4e259817a69362e9ef6764e537f2708c9161081b4909d0fe2525f875c326 tests/unit/services/WorkspaceService.test.ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { CreateWorkspaceInput } from '../../../src/domain/types.js';
import { MetricsRecorder } from '../../../src/services/MetricsRecorder.js';
import { NamespaceService } from '../../../src/services/NamespaceService.js';
import { WorkspaceService, isActive, toWorkspace } from '../../../src/services/WorkspaceService.js';
import {
  NotFoundError,
  UpstreamError,
  WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError,
  WorkspaceCountExceededError,
  WorkspaceRunCountExceededError,
} from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { silentLogger } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';
import { FakeClock, FakeMetricsStore, FakeRegistry } from '../../helpers/fakes.js';
import { InMemoryKube } from '../../helpers/InMemoryKube.js';

let kube: InMemoryKube;
let metrics: FakeMetricsStore;
let registry: FakeRegistry;
let clock: FakeClock;
let service: WorkspaceService;

const input = (over: Partial<CreateWorkspaceInput> = {}): CreateWorkspaceInput => ({
  soeid: 'pp1',
  csiid: '1001',
  devworkspaceValue: 'java-maven',
  editor: 'vscode',
  ...over,
});

beforeEach(() => {
  clock = new FakeClock();
  kube = new InMemoryKube(() => clock.now());
  metrics = new FakeMetricsStore();
  registry = new FakeRegistry();
  const recorder = new MetricsRecorder(metrics, clock, silentLogger);
  const namespaces = new NamespaceService(kube, recorder, { emailDomain: 'example.com' }, silentLogger);
  service = new WorkspaceService(
    kube,
    namespaces,
    registry,
    recorder,
    {
      limits: { maxRunning: 2, maxTotal: 3 },
      editors: { vscode: 'che-code', intellij: 'che-idea', goland: 'che-goland' },
      devSpacesServiceFqdn: 'https://devspaces.test',
    },
    clock,
    silentLogger,
  );
});

const wsNamespace = 'pp1-devspaces-1001';
const onlyWorkspace = () => kube.workspaces()[0]!;

describe('create', () => {
  it('creates the namespace and a started DevWorkspace, and returns it', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    expect(kube.namespaces.has(wsNamespace)).toBe(true);
    expect(kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(ws).toMatchObject({
      namespace: wsNamespace,
      csiid: '1001',
      stack: 'java-maven',
      version: '2.0.0',
      displayName: 'Java Maven',
      editor: 'Visual Studio Code',
      phase: 'Starting',
    });
    expect(ws.id).toMatch(/^java-maven-[0-9a-f]{4}$/);
    expect(onlyWorkspace().spec?.started).toBe(true);
  });

  it('uses a requested stack version', async () => {
    expect((await service.create(input({ stackVersion: '1.0.0' }))).version).toBe('1.0.0');
  });

  it('applies the JetBrains editor profile to the devfile', async () => {
    await service.create(input({ editor: 'intellij' }));
    const names = onlyWorkspace().spec?.template?.components?.map((c) => c.name);
    expect(names).toContain('che-idea-server');
    expect(names).not.toContain('venv');
    expect(onlyWorkspace().spec?.contributions?.[0]?.uri).toContain('che-incubator/che-idea/latest');
  });

  it('records a create metric with the stack’s resources, version and editor', async () => {
    await service.create(input());
    expect(metrics.records.filter((r) => r.resourcetype === 'workspace')).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({
        eventtype: 'create',
        soeid: 'pp1',
        csiid: '1001',
        workspaceversion: '2.0.0',
        editorname: 'vscode',
        limitcpu: 2,
        limitmemory: 4,
        storage: 3,
        status: { failed: false, error: '', message: '' },
      }),
    ]);
  });

  it('rejects an unknown stack before creating anything in the cluster', async () => {
    await expect(service.create(input({ devworkspaceValue: 'cobol' }))).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
      WorkspaceConfigNotFoundError,
    );
    expect(kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('records a failed metric when the cluster refuses the workspace', async () => {
    kube.failOnce('create:DevWorkspace', new UpstreamError('webhook denied'));
    await expect(service.create(input())).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
    const failed = metrics.records.find((r) => r.resourcetype === 'workspace');
    expect(failed?.status).toMatchObject({ failed: true, error: 'webhook denied' });
  });

  it('enforces the total workspace limit', async () => {
    for (const phase of ['Stopped', 'Stopped', 'Stopped']) {
      const ws = await service.create(input());
      kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, ws.id, phase);
    }
    await expect(service.create(input())).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(WorkspaceCountExceededError);
  });

  it('enforces the running workspace limit', async () => {
    const a = await service.create(input());
    const b = await service.create(input());
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, a.id, 'Running');
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, b.id, 'Running');
    await expect(service.create(input())).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(WorkspaceRunCountExceededError);
  });

  it('counts workspaces without a phase as running', async () => {
    await service.create(input());
    await service.create(input());
    await expect(service.create(input())).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(WorkspaceRunCountExceededError);
  });

  it('does not count stopped or failed workspaces as running', async () => {
    const a = await service.create(input());
    const b = await service.create(input());
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, a.id, 'Stopped');
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, b.id, 'Failed');
    await expect(service.create(input())).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });
});

describe('list / get', () => {
  it('lists across all of the user’s namespaces and nobody else’s', async () => {
    await service.create(input({ csiid: '1001' }));
    await service.create(input({ csiid: '1002' }));
    kube.seedNamespace('pp2-devspaces-1', 'pp2');
    await kube.create('DevWorkspace', 'pp2-devspaces-1', {
      metadata: { name: 'theirs' },
      spec: { started: false },
    });
    const list = await service.list('pp1');
    expect(list).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(list.map((w) => w.csiid).sort()).toEqual(['1001', '1002']);
  });

  it('gets one workspace and 404s for unknown or foreign ids', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    expect((await service.get('pp1', ws.id)).id).toBe(ws.id);
    await expect(service.get('pp1', 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
    await expect(service.get('pp2', ws.id)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });
});

describe('start / stop', () => {
  it('starts a stopped workspace and records it', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, ws.id, 'Stopped');
    await service.start('pp1', ws.id);
    expect(onlyWorkspace().spec?.started).toBe(true);
    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ eventtype: 'start', resourcename: ws.id });
  });

  it('is a no-op for a workspace that is already running or starting', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, ws.id, 'Running');
    const before = metrics.records.length;
    await service.start('pp1', ws.id);
    expect(kube.calls).not.toContain('setWorkspaceStarted');
    expect(metrics.records).toHaveLength(before);
  });

  it('refuses to start when the running limit is reached', async () => {
    const a = await service.create(input());
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, a.id, 'Stopped');
    const b = await service.create(input());
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, b.id, 'Stopped');
    const c = await service.create(input({ csiid: '1002' }));
    kube.setPhase('pp1-devspaces-1002', c.id, 'Stopped');
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, a.id, 'Running');
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, b.id, 'Running');
    await expect(service.start('pp1', c.id)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(WorkspaceRunCountExceededError);
  });

  it('stops a workspace and records how long it ran', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    kube.setPhase(wsNamespace, ws.id, 'Running');
    clock.advance(90 * 60 * 1000);
    await service.stop('pp1', ws.id);
    expect(onlyWorkspace().spec?.started).toBe(false);
    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ eventtype: 'stop', duration: '1 hour 30 minutes' });
  });

  it('404s for an unknown workspace', async () => {
    await expect(service.stop('pp1', 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
    await expect(service.start('pp1', 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });

  it('records a failed stop', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    kube.failOnce('setWorkspaceStarted', new UpstreamError('patch failed'));
    await expect(service.stop('pp1', ws.id)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)?.status).toMatchObject({ failed: true, error: 'patch failed' });
  });
});

describe('delete', () => {
  it('deletes the workspace and records its lifetime', async () => {
    const ws = await service.create(input());
    clock.advance(2 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    await service.delete('pp1', ws.id);
    expect(kube.workspaces()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(metrics.records.at(-1)).toMatchObject({
      eventtype: 'delete',
      duration: '2 hours',
      limitcpu: 2,
      storage: 3,
    });
  });

  it('also removes the legacy DevWorkspaceTemplate a workspace contributes', async () => {
    kube.seedNamespace(wsNamespace, 'pp1', '1001');
    await kube.create('DevWorkspaceTemplate', wsNamespace, { metadata: { name: 'legacy-template' } });
    await kube.create('DevWorkspace', wsNamespace, {
      metadata: { name: 'legacy' },
      spec: { contributions: [{ name: 'x', kubernetes: { name: 'legacy-template' } }] },
    });
    await service.delete('pp1', 'legacy');
    expect(kube.objects.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it('404s for an unknown workspace', async () => {
    await expect(service.delete('pp1', 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
  });
});

describe('helpers', () => {
  it('isActive', () => {
    expect(isActive({ status: {} })).toBe(true);
    expect(isActive({})).toBe(true);
    for (const phase of ['Running', 'Starting', 'Stopping'])
      expect(isActive({ status: { phase } })).toBe(true);
    for (const phase of ['Stopped', 'Failed']) expect(isActive({ status: { phase } })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('toWorkspace tolerates a sparse resource', () => {
    expect(toWorkspace({ metadata: { name: 'x', namespace: 'pp1-devspaces-5' } })).toMatchObject({
      id: 'x',
      csiid: '5',
      phase: 'Starting',
      mainUrl: '',
      created: '',
      limitcpu: 0,
    });
  });
});

import axios, { AxiosError } from 'axios';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  AppError,
  ConflictError,
  ForbiddenError,
  NotFoundError,
  UpstreamError,
  errorMessage,
} from '../../../src/utils/errors.js';
import { describeError, isK8sStatus, k8sStatus, toAppError } from '../../../src/utils/k8s-errors.js';

describe('k8sStatus', () => {
  it('reads code, statusCode, response.status and body.code', () => {
    expect(k8sStatus({ code: 404 })).toBe(404);
    expect(k8sStatus({ statusCode: 403 })).toBe(403);
    expect(k8sStatus({ response: { status: 409 } })).toBe(409);
    expect(k8sStatus({ body: { code: 500 } })).toBe(500);
    expect(k8sStatus({ body: JSON.stringify({ code: 422 }) })).toBe(422);
  });
  it('is undefined for non-http errors', () => {
    expect(k8sStatus(new Error('x'))).toBeUndefined();
    expect(k8sStatus(null)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(k8sStatus('boom')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(k8sStatus({ body: 'not json' })).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('isK8sStatus compares', () => {
    expect(isK8sStatus({ code: 404 }, 404)).toBe(true);
    expect(isK8sStatus({ code: 404 }, 403)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('describeError', () => {
  it('describes kubernetes api errors with the server message', () => {
    expect(describeError('ctx', { code: 422, body: { message: 'invalid spec' } })).toBe(
      'ctx: Kubernetes API error (422) invalid spec',
    );
  });
  it('describes 404 plainly', () => {
    expect(describeError('ctx', { code: 404 })).toBe('ctx: Resource not found (404)');
  });
  it('describes axios errors', () => {
    const withResponse = new AxiosError('boom', 'ERR', undefined, {}, { status: 503, data: {} } as never);
    expect(describeError('ctx', withResponse)).toBe('ctx: HTTP 503 boom');
    const noResponse = new AxiosError('down', 'ECONNREFUSED', undefined, {});
    expect(describeError('ctx', noResponse)).toBe('ctx: No response received from server');
    const bare = new AxiosError('odd');
    expect(axios.isAxiosError(bare)).toBe(true);
    expect(describeError('ctx', bare)).toBe('ctx: Axios error odd');
  });
  it('flags TLS problems', () => {
    expect(describeError('ctx', new Error('self signed certificate in chain'))).toBe(
      'ctx: TLS/SSL certificate error',
    );
  });
  it('falls back to the message or string form', () => {
    expect(describeError('ctx', new Error('plain'))).toBe('ctx: plain');
    expect(describeError('ctx', 'text')).toBe('ctx: text');
  });
});

describe('toAppError', () => {
  it('maps statuses to typed errors and keeps the cause', () => {
    const cause = { code: 404 };
    const notFound = toAppError('ctx', cause);
    expect(notFound).toBeInstanceOf(NotFoundError);
    expect((notFound as { cause?: unknown }).cause).toBe(cause);
    expect(toAppError('ctx', { code: 403 })).toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
    expect(toAppError('ctx', { code: 409 })).toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
    const upstream = toAppError('ctx', { code: 500 });
    expect(upstream).toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
    expect(upstream.status).toBe(502);
  });
  it('passes AppErrors through untouched', () => {
    const original = new ConflictError('x');
    expect(toAppError('ctx', original)).toBe(original);
  });
  it('wraps unknown failures as upstream errors', () => {
    expect(toAppError('ctx', new Error('weird'))).toBeInstanceOf(UpstreamError);
  });
});

describe('errors', () => {
  it('carry status, code and class name', () => {
    const e = new NotFoundError('nope');
    expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(AppError);
    expect([e.status, e.code, e.name]).toEqual([404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'NotFoundError']);
  });
  it('errorMessage handles non-errors', () => {
    expect(errorMessage(new Error('a'))).toBe('a');
    expect(errorMessage(42)).toBe('42');
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 4f07f43efec10f27c65f75b443290ff6b9e41c4e1f71b9e5ce4ce8faa87565e6 tests/unit/utils/logger.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createLogger, redact } from '../../../src/utils/logger.js';

function capture(level: Parameters<typeof createLogger>[0] = 'info') {
  const lines: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  return { lines, logger: createLogger(level, (l) => lines.push(JSON.parse(l))) };
}

describe('logger', () => {
  it('writes one JSON line with level, event and fields', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    logger.info('hello', { a: 1 });
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({ level: 'info', event: 'hello', a: 1 });
    expect(typeof lines[0]?.time).toBe('string');
  });

  it('filters below the configured level', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture('warn');
    logger.debug('d');
    logger.info('i');
    logger.warn('w');
    logger.error('e');
    expect(lines.map((l) => l.event)).toEqual(['w', 'e']);
  });

  it('is silent when asked', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture('silent');
    logger.error('e');
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('child loggers carry bindings', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    logger.child({ requestId: 'r1' }).info('x', { b: 2 });
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({ requestId: 'r1', b: 2 });
  });

  it('redacts sensitive keys, nested and in arrays', () => {
    const { lines, logger } = capture();
    logger.info('x', {
      token: 'ghp_secret',
      nested: { password: 'p', ok: 1 },
      list: [{ authorization: 'Bearer x' }],
    });
    expect(lines[0]).toMatchObject({
      token: '[redacted]',
      nested: { password: '[redacted]', ok: 1 },
      list: [{ authorization: '[redacted]' }],
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(lines[0])).not.toContain('ghp_secret');
  });

  it('serialises errors as name and message only', () => {
    expect(redact(new Error('bad'))).toEqual({ name: 'Error', message: 'bad' });
  });

  it('stops redacting past a sane depth', () => {
    const deep = { a: { b: { c: { d: { e: { f: { g: 1 } } } } } } };
    expect(redact(deep)).toEqual(deep);
  });
});

  it.each(['', 'ab', 'a b', 'a/b', '../etc', 'x'.repeat(21), 'user;rm', 'üser'])('rejects %j', (value) => {

  });

    expect(() => parseCsiid(value)).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
});

    expect(parseTokenName('My-Token')).toBe('my-token');
  });
  it.each(['-bad', 'bad-', 'has space', 'UPPER_CASE', 'x'.repeat(64)])('rejects %j', (value) => {
    expect(() => parseResourceName(value)).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
  it('rejects bad token names', () => {
    expect(() => parseTokenName('a/b')).toThrow(BadRequestError);
    expect(() => parseTokenName('')).toThrow(BadRequestError);
  });
});

describe('namespace helpers', () => {
  it('builds and parses workspace namespaces', () => {
    expect(workspaceNamespace('pp58386', '177398')).toBe('pp58386-devspaces-177398');
    expect(csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace('pp58386-devspaces-177398')).toBe('177398');
    expect(csiidFromWorkspaceNamespace('pp58386-devspaces-deploy-177398')).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('builds and parses deployment namespaces', () => {
    expect(deploymentNamespace('pp58386', '177398')).toBe('pp58386-devspaces-deploy-177398');
    expect(csiidFromDeploymentNamespace('pp58386-devspaces-deploy-177398')).toBe('177398');
    expect(csiidFromDeploymentNamespace('pp58386-devspaces-177398')).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('extractClusterName', () => {
  it('reads the cluster from api and apps URLs', () => {
    expect(extractClusterName('https://api.namctiswd004d.ecs.dyn.nsroot.net:6443')).toBe('namctiswd004d');
    expect(extractClusterName('https://console-openshift-console.apps.prod1.ecs.dyn.example.net/')).toBe(
      'prod1',
    );
  });
  it('falls back for anything else', () => {
    expect(extractClusterName('http://localhost')).toBe('unknown-cluster');
    expect(extractClusterName(undefined)).toBe('unknown-cluster');
  });
});

  it('appends the suffix', () => expect(cleanWorkspaceName('java-maven', 'ab12')).toBe('java-maven-ab12'));
  it('sanitises to DNS-1123', () => {
    expect(cleanWorkspaceName('Java_Maven 17!!', 'ab12')).toBe('java-maven-17-ab12');
    expect(cleanWorkspaceName('---', 'ab12')).toBe('workspace-ab12');
  });
  it('never exceeds 63 characters', () => {
    expect(cleanWorkspaceName('x'.repeat(200), 'ab12').length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(63);
  });
  it('generates 4-hex-char suffixes by default', () => {
    expect(cleanWorkspaceName('py')).toMatch(/^py-[0-9a-f]{4}$/);
    expect(randomSuffix()).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{4}$/);
  });
});

    [undefined, 0],

    ['1Gi', 1],
    ['512Mi', 0.5],
    ['1024Ki', 0.001],
    ['1Ti', 1024],
    ['1G', 0.931],

    ['', 0],
    ['nonsense', 0],
    ['5Zz', 0],
    [undefined, 0],

    {
      name: 'tools',
      container: { cpuLimit: '2000m', cpuRequest: '500m', memoryLimit: '4Gi', memoryRequest: '1Gi' },
    },
    { name: 'db', container: { cpuLimit: '1', memoryLimit: '512Mi' } },
    { name: 'checode', container: { cpuLimit: '9', memoryLimit: '9Gi' }, volume: { size: '9Gi' } },

    { name: 'home', volume: { size: '2Gi' } },
  ];

  it('sums containers and volumes, excluding the editor component', () => {
    expect(summarizeComponents(components)).toEqual({
      limitcpu: 3,
      requestcpu: 0.5,
      limitmemory: 4.5,
      requestmemory: 1,
      storage: 3,
    });
  });

  it('honours a custom exclusion list', () => {
    expect(summarizeComponents(components, ['checode', 'db']).limitcpu).toBe(2);
  });

  it('returns zeros for nothing', () => {
    expect(summarizeComponents(undefined)).toEqual({
      limitcpu: 0,
      limitmemory: 0,
      requestcpu: 0,
      requestmemory: 0,
      storage: 0,
    });
  });

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

describe('getFormattedTimestampCST', () => {
  it('formats in America/Chicago as MM-DD-YY HH:mm:ss CST (winter, UTC-6)', () => {
    expect(getFormattedTimestampCST(new Date('2026-01-15T18:30:05Z'))).toBe('01-15-26 12:30:05 CST');
  });

  it('follows daylight saving time (summer, UTC-5)', () => {

  });

  it('uses 00 rather than 24 for midnight', () => {

  });

    [59, '59 seconds'],
    [60, '1 minute'],
    [61, '1 minute 1 second'],
    [185, '3 minutes 5 seconds'],
    [3600, '1 hour'],
    [3660, '1 hour 1 minute'],
    [7500, '2 hours 5 minutes'],
    [-5, '0 seconds'],
    [59.9, '59 seconds'],
  ])('%s s → %s', (seconds, expected) => {
    expect(formatDuration(seconds)).toBe(expected);
  });
});

describe('durationSince', () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-01-15T12:00:00Z');
  it('measures from a date or an ISO string', () => {
    expect(durationSince(new Date('2026-01-15T11:58:00Z'), now)).toBe('2 minutes');
    expect(durationSince('2026-01-15T10:00:00Z', now)).toBe('2 hours');
  });
  it('returns 0 seconds when the start is missing or invalid', () => {
    expect(durationSince(undefined, now)).toBe('0 seconds');
    expect(durationSince('not a date', now)).toBe('0 seconds');
  });
});

@@@FILE@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e text nl 0dcf60faca9b77bc218cfeef8a5d2769cd6ee37fa5f63327ece9ddf99e176672 unpack.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Recreate the whole workspace-mgr app from the text file made by pack.sh (all-code-file.txt).
# Needs only bash, awk and coreutils (sha256sum or shasum, base64, mktemp). Works in Git Bash on Windows.
#
#   bash unpack.sh all-code-file.txt                 recreate in the current directory
#   bash unpack.sh all-code-file.txt -o /some/dir    recreate in another directory (created if missing)
#   bash unpack.sh all-code-file.txt --dry-run       show what would happen, write nothing
#   bash unpack.sh all-code-file.txt --force         overwrite existing files that differ
#   bash unpack.sh all-code-file.txt --list          list the files in the bundle
#
# Every file is checked against its SHA-256 BEFORE anything is written to the target. Paths that could
# escape the target directory are rejected. Existing files that differ are never overwritten without --force.
# If an editor or git converted the file's line endings to CRLF, they are converted back, but only when the
# checksum proves the result is identical to the original.
# Afterwards:  npm ci && npm test && npm run build && npm start

set -euo pipefail
export LC_ALL=C

IN=""
OUT="."
FORCE=0
DRY=0
LIST=0

while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    -o|--out) OUT="${2:?-o needs a directory}"; shift 2 ;;
    -f|--force) FORCE=1; shift ;;
    -n|--dry-run) DRY=1; shift ;;
    -l|--list) LIST=1; shift ;;
    -h|--help) sed -n '2,14p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; exit 0 ;;
    -*) echo "unpack: unknown argument: $1" >&2; exit 1 ;;
    *) if [ -z "$IN" ]; then IN="$1"; else echo "unpack: unexpected argument: $1" >&2; exit 1; fi; shift ;;
  esac
done

if [ -z "$IN" ]; then
  sed -n '2,14p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'
  exit 1
fi
[ -f "$IN" ] || { echo "unpack: no such file: $IN" >&2; exit 1; }

# Accept Windows-style paths (C:\dir) when running under Git Bash / MSYS / Cygwin.
if command -v cygpath >/dev/null 2>&1 && printf '%s' "$OUT" | grep -Eq '^[A-Za-z]:[\\/]'; then
  OUT="$(cygpath -u "$OUT")"
fi

hash_of() {
  if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then sha256sum "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1
  elif command -v shasum >/dev/null 2>&1; then shasum -a 256 "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1
  else echo "unpack: need sha256sum or shasum to verify files" >&2; exit 1; fi
}

STAGE="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "$STAGE"' EXIT
MANIFEST="$STAGE/.manifest"
: > "$MANIFEST"
mkdir -p "$STAGE/files"

# ---- 1. split the text file into a staging directory (nothing touches the target yet) -----------------
# extract STRIP: with STRIP=1 exactly one CR is removed from every line, which undoes a naive LF -> CRLF
# conversion of the whole text file. It is done here, byte-exact, because the Windows sed/awk text modes would
# also eat the CR of a file that legitimately has CRLF endings.
# BINMODE=3: gawk on Windows (Git Bash) otherwise strips the CR from CRLF files; other awks ignore it.
extract() {
rm -rf "$STAGE/files"; mkdir -p "$STAGE/files"; : > "$MANIFEST"
awk -v BINMODE=3 -v strip="$1" -v stage="$STAGE/files" -v manifest="$MANIFEST" '
function fail(msg) { print "unpack: " msg > "/dev/stderr"; failed = 1; exit 1 }
function safe(p,   t) {
  if (p == "" || p ~ /^\// || p !~ /^[A-Za-z0-9._@+=, \/-]+$/) return 0
  if (p ~ /\/\// || p ~ /\/$/) return 0
  t = "/" p "/"
  if (t ~ /\/\.\.\// || t ~ /\/\.\//) return 0
  return 1
}
function finish(   cmd) {
  if (enc == "b64") {
    close(pipecmd)
  } else {
    if (!have) printf "" > outpath
    else if (nl == "nl") printf "%s\n", prev > outpath
    else printf "%s", prev > outpath
    close(outpath)
  }
  print path "\t" sha "\t" enc >> manifest
  infile = 0; count++
}
{
  if (strip + 0) sub(/\r$/, "")
  m = $0; sub(/\r$/, "", m)
  if (!infile) {
    if (m ~ /^#### token: /) { tok = substr(m, 13); next }
    if (m ~ /^#### files: /) { declared = substr(m, 13) + 0; next }
    if (tok != "" && index(m, "@@@FILE@" tok " ") == 1) {
      rest = substr(m, length("@@@FILE@" tok " ") + 1)
      n = split(rest, a, " ")
      if (n < 4) fail("malformed file header: " m)
      enc = a[1]; nl = a[2]; sha = a[3]
      path = substr(rest, length(a[1]) + length(a[2]) + length(a[3]) + 4)
      if (!safe(path)) fail("unsafe path in bundle: " path)
      if (path in seen) fail("duplicate path in bundle: " path)
      if (enc != "text" && enc != "b64") fail("unknown encoding \"" enc "\" for " path)
      seen[path] = 1
      outpath = stage "/" path
      dir = outpath; sub(/\/[^\/]*$/, "", dir)
      system("mkdir -p \"" dir "\"")
      have = 0; prev = ""; infile = 1
      if (enc == "b64") pipecmd = "base64 -d > \"" outpath "\""
      next
    }
    next
  }
  if (index(m, "@@@END@" tok) == 1 && m == "@@@END@" tok) { finish(); next }
  if (enc == "b64") { print m | pipecmd; next }
  if (have) printf "%s\n", prev > outpath
  prev = $0; have = 1
}
END {
  if (failed) exit 1
  if (infile) fail("missing end marker for " path " (truncated file?)")
  if (tok == "") fail("not a workspace-mgr bundle (no token header found)")
  if (count != declared) fail("bundle declares " declared " files but contains " count)
}
' "$IN"
}

# ---- 2. verify every file against its recorded SHA-256 (repairing CRLF conversion if provable) --------
BAD=""
verify() {
  local path sum enc
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r path sum enc; do
    [ -n "$path" ] || continue
    if [ "$(hash_of "$STAGE/files/$path")" != "$sum" ]; then BAD="$path"; return 1; fi
  done < "$MANIFEST"
  return 0
}

REPAIRED=0
extract 0
if ! verify; then
  # Was the whole text file converted from LF to CRLF (an editor, e-mail client or git autocrlf)? Try the exact
  # inverse; it is only accepted if EVERY file then matches its checksum.
  if head -n 1 "$IN" | od -An -c | grep -q '\\r'; then
    first_bad="$BAD"
    extract 1
    if verify; then
      REPAIRED=1
    else
      echo "unpack: checksum mismatch for $first_bad (file was edited or damaged in transit; the line endings look converted to CRLF but converting them back did not restore it); nothing was written" >&2
      exit 1
    fi
  else
    echo "unpack: checksum mismatch for $BAD (file was edited or damaged in transit); nothing was written" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
fi
[ "$REPAIRED" = 1 ] && echo "Note: line endings had been converted to CRLF; converted back, every file matches its checksum." >&2

TOTAL="$(grep -c . "$MANIFEST" || true)"

if [ "$LIST" = 1 ]; then
  while IFS=$'\t' read -r path sum enc; do
    [ -n "$path" ] && printf '%s  (%s bytes, %s)\n' "$path" "$(wc -c < "$STAGE/files/$path" | tr -d ' ')" "$enc"
  done < "$MANIFEST"
  echo; echo "$TOTAL file(s)"
  exit 0
fi

# ---- 3. copy into the target, never clobbering differing files unless --force -----------------------------
created=0; overwritten=0; unchanged=0; skipped=0
[ "$DRY" = 1 ] || mkdir -p "$OUT"
while IFS=$'\t' read -r path sum enc; do
  [ -n "$path" ] || continue
  src="$STAGE/files/$path"
  dst="$OUT/$path"
  if [ ! -e "$dst" ]; then action=created
  elif [ ! -f "$dst" ]; then action=skipped
  elif cmp -s "$src" "$dst"; then action=unchanged
  elif [ "$FORCE" = 1 ]; then action=overwritten
  else action=skipped; fi

  if [ "$DRY" = 0 ] && { [ "$action" = created ] || [ "$action" = overwritten ]; }; then
    mkdir -p "$(dirname "$dst")"
    cp "$src" "$dst"
    case "$path" in *.sh) chmod +x "$dst" 2>/dev/null || true ;; esac
  fi
  case "$action" in
    created) created=$((created + 1)) ;;
    overwritten) overwritten=$((overwritten + 1)) ;;
    unchanged) unchanged=$((unchanged + 1)) ;;
    skipped) skipped=$((skipped + 1)) ;;
  esac
  note=""; [ "$action" = skipped ] && note="  (exists and differs; use --force to overwrite)"
  label="$action"; [ "$DRY" = 1 ] && label="would be $action"
  printf '%-20s%s%s\n' "$label" "$path" "$note"
done < "$MANIFEST"

echo
[ "$DRY" = 1 ] && printf 'Dry run: '
echo "$created created, $overwritten overwritten, $unchanged unchanged, $skipped skipped -> $OUT"
[ "$skipped" -gt 0 ] && exit 2
exit 0
@@@END@ba546fc64dce1166e094386e
